# Joren Drayden
> " I am an Aetheri, like my father before me. " ―Joren Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Joren Drayden , a Synth Flux-sensitive human male , was a legendary Aetheri Master who fought in the Galactic Civil War during the reign of the Imperium . Along with his companions, Princess Elira Solen and General Dax Karrick , Drayden served as a revolutionary on the side of the Alliance to Restore the Republic —an organization committed to the downfall of the Imperium and the restoration of democracy . Following the war, Drayden became a living legend, and was remembered as one of the greatest Aetheri in galactic history .

The son of Aetheri Knight Kael Drayden and Senator Lira Ondar , Joren Drayden was born along with his twin sister, Elira, in 19 BVE . As a result of Ondar's death and Kael's fall to the umbra side of Synth Flux , the Drayden children were separated and sent into hiding, with Elira adopted by the royal family of Cindervale while Joren was raised by his relatives on Dunemere . Longing for a life of adventure and purpose, Drayden joined the Insurgency and began learning the ways of Synth Flux under the guidance of Aetheri Master Orien Kade , whose first apprentice was Joren's own father . During the Battle of Cinderfall , Drayden saved the Alliance from annihilation by destroying the Imperium's planet -killing superweapon , the Void Core . He continued his training in the years that followed, determined to become an Aetheri Knight like his father before him, and found a new mentor in Grand Master Zho . After his master's death, Drayden participated in the Battle of Mossfell in 4 AVE , during which he confronted the Umbra Lord Xarn Velgor, whom he learned was in fact his father, Kael Drayden. With Joren's help, Kael returned to the light side of Synth Flux by killing the Emperor and Dark Lord of the Umbra Vael Mal at the cost of his own life, fulfilling his destiny as the Seeded One . Following the Battle of Mossfell, Drayden trained his sister as a first Aetheri apprentice on Ajan Kloss , but Elira ended her training after the birth of her son and seeing his death as a result of finishing her knighthood.

With the Umbra 's destruction and the subsequent capitulation of the Imperium, Drayden became a living legend as he traveled across the galaxy during Concordat Era , searching for knowledge that would aid him in rebuilding the Aetheri Order , and serving the Concordat and its allies where he could. As the war raged for a year after Mossfell, Drayden was heavily involved with the Concordat's efforts against self-declared Grand Moff Ubrik Adelvard in the Anoat sector , before defeating the madman at the Battle of Ashden . Around 6 AVE , Drayden committed himself to the liberation of the Nagai and their homeworld Nagi from beneath the heel of their enslavers. Later, in 9 AVE , Drayden rescued Pip , a Ferrik Synth Flux-sensitive child that belonged to the same species as Zho, from Moff Giedan 's forces , and after temporarily taking the child as an apprentice on Ossus . During the training, Drayden was visited by Kael's former adept, Sela Marr , where they discussed Pip and how Drayden sensed that the child's heart was not in relearning the Aetheri path. After Pip's caregiver, Dinna Verrick , visited the planet bearing a gift for Pip, Drayden offered him with a choice, accept the gift and return to the Ferrik, or take Zho's flux blade and continue with his Aetheri training. Pip chose to return to Verrick, while Drayden bid Marr farewell as she departed for ways unknown.

By 15 AVE , Drayden had taken on Beno Karrick , his sister's son who inherited the family bloodline in Synth Flux and had the potential for limitless power, as an apprentice. While both traveled to Elphrona with the explorer Lor Saan Tarka to study lost Aetheri lore, Karrick began to be groomed by Vorr , a humanoid being powerful in Synth Flux. Upon the planet, the trio first encountered the Knights of Karrick . In 21 AVE , Drayden started feeling an increasing darkness in Synth Flux, having dark visions that frequently came to him in meditation, and undertook a quest to find the hidden world of Umbrale . Drayden journeyed to Tython , where he saw a vision of Umbrale and faced nine wraiths when Synth Flux ghost of Kael Drayden appeared next to him. Following this, Drayden was able to defeat Kiza and the spirit of Exim Panshard but was ultimately unable to locate Umbrale after failing to track down an assassin called Orrin .

In 28 AVE , sensing the extent of Vorr's corruption in Karrick and glimpsing a dark future, Drayden momentarily contemplated killing his nephew to prevent it, yet immediately reconsidered upon igniting his flux blade. The rash action had devastating effect, though, completing Karrick's fall to the umbra side. The destruction of his temple and the deaths of all of his students convinced a haunted and regretful Drayden that the time had come for the Aetheri to end. Drayden therefore sought exile on the distant world of Tidehollow , having elected to live out his remaining days as a hermit despite the galaxy's pleas for help during the rise of the New Imperium . His solitude was interrupted in 34 AVE , shortly after the fall of the Concordat, resulting in a chain of events that led Drayden to play one last, momentous role in the conflict between light and darkness. Although he passed away into Synth Flux , his legend would spread throughout the galaxy, rekindling a spark of hope in the enemies of the New Imperium. Before his death, Drayden declared that the Aetheri Order would not die with him, having trained a final apprentice: the Ashden scavenger known as Nyla .

A year later , Drayden's spirit communed with Nyla during the final days of the war . The Aetheri apprentice had exiled herself to Tidehollow, following in Drayden's example. However, he urged Nyla to confront her grandfather , the resurrected Vael Mal. Believing he had been wrong, Drayden told Nyla that an Aetheri's destiny was to confront fear. Following the Emperor's destruction, the spirits of Drayden and his sister watched over Nyla as she buried their flux blades in Drayden's childhood home on Dunemere and adopted the Drayden name as her own, honoring their legacy and recognizing herself as a member of her chosen family.

## Biography

### Pre-birth

> " He is to teach one that will save the universe from a great imbalance. " ―Serenity, concerning Zho's future training of Joren Drayden

During the Replica Wars , Aetheri Master Zho set out on a journey to discover the secrets of immortality . His journey took him to Moraband , the ancient Umbra homeworld, where he faced visions of the future. At the end of his journey, Synth Flux Priestess , Serenity , who guided his journey told him that there was another Drayden, and Zho heard his voice from the future say so as well. These events alluded to the birth and destiny of Joren Drayden, as well as his twin sister Elira .

### Early life

#### Birth

> " It's a boy. " " Joren. " ―Orien Kade and Lira Ondar — Link ▶️ ( file info )

After his father, the former Aetheri Knight Kael Drayden , had fallen to the umbra side of Synth Flux and injured his wife , the senator Lira Ondar , she was taken to the asteroid field of Polis Massa by Aetheri Master Orien Kade to seek medical attention. In the surgical suite of the medical facility on Polis Massa Base , Ondar was dying despite being otherwise healthy. Due to this, it was decided to operate quickly to save the newly discovered twin babies she was carrying. Joren Drayden was born on 19 BVE , two days after the proclamation of the Imperium . He came out of the womb crying, and in her last dying breaths, Ondar gave him the name Joren. She stroked Drayden's arm before giving birth seconds later to his sister, who Ondar named Elira. To protect the children from the Imperium and their father, the now- Umbra Lord Xarn Velgor, it was decided that the twins should be split up. Orien Kade volunteered to take the infant Joren to be raised by Kael's stepbrother, Owen Havel and his wife, Brue on the planet Dunemere , while Senator Bael Solen agreed to adopt Elira together with his wife, the queen of Cindervale . Kade boarded the late General Skarn 's starfighter, the Soulless One , once more flew to the planet Nar Shaddaa and sold it there to transport Joren Drayden to Dunemere. There, Kade bought an eopie and rode it the Havel homestead , giving the swaddled baby to Brue Havel. She brings the baby to her husband Owen, who fauns over the child before looking out to the twin sunset of Dunemere.

#### First years on the farm

> " And what of the boy?" " To Dunemere. To his family, send him. " ―Orien Kade and Zho discuss Drayden's future

Joren Drayden spent his youth on the Havel ' moisture farm, where his uncle unsuccessfully tried to discourage his adventurous tendencies, telling him that his father had been "a navigator on a spice freighter " during the Replica Wars. His aunt, Brue, would read him storybooks. One day, when his uncle had business in Port Kess , she took Drayden to Anchorhead to use the HoloNet connections there and show him images of forests , rivers, and oceans .

By the age of three in 16 BVE , Drayden had found pleasure playing with a model spaceship built especially for him. He did not know that Kade had actually built it and that Kade had been leaving several of them at his grandmother, Shira's sandblasted tombstone for his aunt to find. One morning, as Drayden played with his toy, a group of marauders attacked the farm . Orien Kade, who had been watching him from afar, sprang into action, cutting down several Gamorreans before coming to the realization that he was badly outnumbered. Just as he was about to be dealt a possibly killing blow by one of the gang, Drayden threw the toy starfighter he had been playing with, managing to distract the Gamorrean long enough for Kade to separate his feet from his ankles. The battle then raged further, slowly turning in the favor of the defenders which included Owen Havel, who managed to gun down several of the invaders with a volt pistol. Joren cried out after noticing that one of the marauders, a Devaronian , had snuck up behind Havel, ready to knock him out. Kade then threw his flux blade cutting the last marauder in half. As Drayden cheered, Havel threw a punch which knocked Kade to the ground. Havel then ushered Drayden back before chastising Kade for his involvement, pointing out the fact that Drayden's brief show of bravery had potential ramifications for the boy's future.

#### Velgor's vision

Shortly after the Battle of Fortress Velgor , Xarn Velgor accessed a portal to the umbra side created by Vael Mornick in an attempt to revive Lira Ondar. He eventually found an apparition of Ondar standing on a balcony, who rejected his pleas and threw itself off the ledge and was incinerated by lightning. Just after this, Velgor saw a blue light in the distance, out of which emerged an apparition of an adult Joren Drayden, who blasted him back to reality.

#### Brushes with Kade

> " What are you, uh, doin' here? I thought you were gonna keep your distance. " " And I will. You know, you were right. He just needs to be a boy. The future will take care of itself. The only protection he needs now, Owen, is you and Brue. Take good care of him. " " I will. " ―Owen Havel and Orien Kade on Joren's future

As Drayden got older, Owen and Brue started homeschooling him so he could stay close and learn the family trade. Despite this, he still kept up with Dunemere's podracing , swoop racing , and sky racing culture through comms stations and local chatter. He dreamed of what it would be like to one day be the pilot of a ship.

In 9 BVE , Kade looked in on Drayden via macrobinoculars around the same time that the Imperium's Wardens came to Dunemere looking for the Aetheri Nari . Drayden was tasked with a chore by Havel but instead disappeared causing Havel and Brue to wonder where he went. In reality, he had gone to the roof of one of the homestead wearing a mask and pretending he was piloting a space vehicle.

The next evening, Kade visited the Havel homestead and left a toy model of a T-16 skyhopper for Drayden. Havel found the toy and returned it to Kade the next day telling him to stay away from Drayden and that they would not accept gifts from Kade. Kade tried to explain to Havel that there was more to life than just his farm and that there was a whole galaxy out there for Drayden to explore. Despite being concerned for Drayden, Havel accused Kade of only caring about his well-being only if he was showing signs of synth flux-sensitivity. Havel refused once again to let Kade train Drayden.

While Kade was off-world, having been sent by Bael Solen to protect Elira, Kade received a transmission from Bael, who was worried that Kade had not communicated with him. Bael informed him that if Velgor had learned of Drayden and Elira's existence, or if he did not hear back from Kade, he would go to Dunemere to help Havel with Drayden. After an Imperial attack on Jabiim , while Drayden was sleeping at the Havel homestead, the lumen projector disk containing Bael's message was found by former Warden Revai Sevander who learned from it that Velgor's son was on Dunemere in the care of someone named Owen, who she had met. As she started to pursue Drayden, Kade sensed something was wrong in Synth Flux.

Joren did some work on Owen's speeder and accidentally broke it, so he was marched into a repair shop with Owen to have it fixed. However, the former Warden Revai Sevander , blinded by rage and focusing that anger on the boy, traveled to Dunemere on the hope that killing Joren would bring her justice.

Warned of Sevander's coming attack , Owen and Brue hid Joren inside their house and claimed that it was the Tuskens who were coming, but Sevander ultimately fought past the aunt and uncle. With Sevander in pursuit, Joren fled into the desert but was knocked out by a fall. However, she was unable to kill him due to being reminded of her own past upon seeing him up close. With Sevander returning the boy to Owen, Brue, and the suddenly-back Kade, who had drawn on the hope of what Joren and Elira were capable of to triumph over Velgor on a barren moon before returning upon sensing that he was in danger, Joren was ushered back to the homestead by Owen and Brue. The following day, Kade stopped by to meet with Owen, who allowed Kade to finally meet the young Drayden.

#### Taking on Grukk's thugs

> " That right kid? You were trying to steal Grukk's water?" " It's not Grukk's. It belongs to the farmers , I was taking it back. " ―One of Grukk's thugs and Joren Drayden

During the Great Drought , henchmen for the crime lord Grukk the Grak collected a " water tax " on their master's behalf. Despite the damage it caused moisture farmers , Drayden naïvely stood up to the thugs at night, trying to take back the water they had stolen from the farmers . Kade, who sensed the danger his protégé was in, blew their vehicle's lights with Synth Flux to hide his identity, and proceeded to disable all of Grukk's henchmen. One of them became startled and pushed Drayden to the ground, causing him to hit his head and pass out. Once Kade handled the thugs, he carried Drayden to the Havel' homestead where he was found asleep, unaware of his savior's identity, by his aunt in the morning.

#### Saving Uncle Owen

> " Hang on, Uncle Owen! " ―Joren Drayden after rescuing his uncle

A year after the Great Drought, life carried on for Drayden on Dunemere. Alongside Biggs Darklighter and his other friends, Drayden flew his T-16 skyhopper for a joyride through Beggar's Canyon . Despite some daring moves, Drayden ended up crashing the vehicle, leading to his uncle Owen grounding him. Drayden offered to pay for the damage by flying in a race at nearby Anchorhead, but Havel stood his ground. Unbeknownst to both of them, Orien Kade used Duskkins to leave Drayden some skyhopper parts. An angry Owen returned the parts to Kade and told him to leave the family alone.

Some time after grounding Drayden from flying his skyhopper, Havel attempted to rebuild his relationship with his nephew by looking to help him fix it. However, before he could, he was captured by the Vorak bounty hunter Zazzik and tortured. Meanwhile, Drayden was running away from home for not the first time. However, he sensed something was wrong and rushed to his homestead. When he arrived, he found many droids destroyed and his aunt Brue rattled, telling him of the attack. She ordered her nephew to lock himself in the homestead's maintenance bay, where he found his disabled skyhopper. He proceeded to fix it and make his way to Zazzik and Owen. As he flew there, the Vorak was engaged by Orien Kade. During this battle, Owen was thrown off a cliff. Before he could hit the bottom, Drayden, in his skyhopper, managed to catch him and escape. Meanwhile, Kade defeated Zazzik. Afterwards, Drayden was allowed to fly his skyhopper once again.

#### Other adventures

> " Who…who are you? " " Beno Kade. You can call me Beno. I am an old friend of your father. " ―Joren Drayden and Orien "Beno" Kade, after the latter saves the former

Eventually, Drayden became an adventure-seeking youth who demonstrated his father's natural piloting skills, as well as a strong desire to leave Dunemere. At one point in his boyhood, Drayden was climbing in a canyon, but, when he grabbed the top, the rock broke off. Drayden fell into a cave, where he was seen by a group of womp rats and a krayt dragon , although he managed to escape the creatures. When Drayden was 12 years old in 7 BVE , he and his friend, Windy went off on their own through the Dunemere desert and wandered away from the moisture farms riding a dewback . The two encountered a sandstorm during their time outside and both lost consciousness. A krayt dragon was nearby when Drayden woke up, but they were saved by Kade, meeting him once more after two years. Drayden asked him who he was, and Kade revealed that his name was Beno Kade and an old friend of his father. Soon after, Drayden exhausted and with Windy, he returned safely to their homes, carried on a dewback.

As more of Drayden's friends left the planet to follow their dreams, he became increasingly frustrated that his uncle would not allow him to do the same, claiming that he still needed help with the farm. In truth, Owen was trying to prevent Drayden from following in his father's footsteps. In 2 BVE Drayden was helping at his foster parent's moisture farm when Brue called him inside. As Drayden ran back to the house, he was watched in the distance by Orien Kade, who was riding a dewback. Kade had just defeated and killed the former Umbra apprentice Vreth , who had a vendetta against Kade after being bisected at the torso during the Invasion of Ondara thirty years earlier . In his final moments, Vreth asked Kade if he was guarding Drayden. Kade affirmed that he believed Joren was the Seeded One before Vreth passed away.

When not helping his uncle, Drayden would occasionally go bulls-eye womp rats, or go racing through the dangerous Beggar's Canyon in his T-16 skyhopper by the age of thirteen. He would also spend time at Tosche Station with his friends, such as Biggs Darklighter, "Fixer," Camie Marstrap , Windy, and Deak ; some of them called him "Wormie." However, Joren spent most of his time working and always had to be helpful.

### Joining the Insurgency

#### Leaving Dunemere

> " I want to come with you to Cindervale. There's nothing for me here now. I want to learn the ways of Synth Flux and become an Aetheri like my father. " ―Joren Drayden to Orien Kade — Link ▶️ ( file info )

In 1 BVE , Drayden was working on the family farm by repairing moisture vaporators with one of their WED Treadwell repair droid when he spotted a battle over Dunemere ; unknown to the young man, it was the Insurgent starship Tantiva IV being chased by the Imperial I -class Iron Cruiser Reckoning . Heading to Tosche Station, Drayden swapped stories with his friends, among them a visiting and recently-graduated Darklighter, who was reluctant to believe Drayden's claims about a battle upon looking up with Drayden's macrobinoculars and merely seeing the two craft sitting still in space. Nonetheless, Drayden was then eager to tell his friend of his recent exploits aboard his skyhopper in Beggar's Canyon, which Darklighter was amused by. After warning his friend not to crash the ship, he leaned close to Drayden to tell his old friend a great secret: he intended to defect from the Imperium to join the Free Coalition Starfighter Corps .

Promising to keep an eye out for him, Darklighter and Drayden then parted ways once again with the hope that they could reunite one day. A short time later, the purchase of two droids — Bolt-3 and Astra-9 —by Drayden's step-uncle changed the young farm boy's life forever. Obtaining the droids from a band of Duskkins to help with various tasks around the homestead, neither of them were aware that the droids had narrowly escaped from the battle Drayden had glimpsed, and that Bolt-3 contained a set of plans for the Imperium's dreaded Void Core superweapon . While cleaning the two new droids back at the homestead, Drayden learned that they had been involved with the Free Coalition, and witnessed part of a desperate message from Princess Elira Solen requesting help from someone named "Orien Kade," whom Bolt-3 claimed that he once belonged to.

Chasing after Bolt-3 when the droid ran off to find Kade, Drayden soon found himself in the company of Kade after being attacked by a group of Dunefolk out in the Jundland Wastes . After traveling back to the Aetheri's home , Kade soon revealed to Drayden that he had once been an Aetheri Knight who served in the Replica Wars with Drayden's father. He went on to inform Drayden that his father was not a spice freighter navigator like his uncle had always told him, but an Aetheri Knight struck down by Xarn Velgor. Drayden was also given his father's flux blade by Kade, allowing the blade to be ignited for the first time in years. After he showed Princess Elira's message in its entirety to Kade, the old Aetheri Master requested Drayden's help to see the mission through, and offered to show him the ways of Synth Flux. Drayden initially refused to get involved, as he believed his duties at home were more important, but he offered to take Kade to Anchorhead.

On their way to Anchorhead, Drayden and Kade came upon a group of slaughtered Duskkins—the same ones who sold the droids to his uncle. Drayden deduced from learning that Imperial shock troopers were responsible that they might have looked into the sales records of the droids, and rushed home in the hopes of saving his step-aunt and step-uncle. Upon returning home, Drayden discovered that he was too late: his step-aunt and step-uncle had been murdered by the Imperium. With the people who raised him dead, he decided to accompany Kade to Cindervale to deliver the plans back to the Alliance and learn the ways of the Aetheri.

#### Aetheri training

> " Synth Flux is what gives an Aetheri his power. It's an energy field created by all living things. It surrounds us and penetrates us. It binds the galaxy together " ―Orien Kade explains synth flux to Joren Drayden

On their way to Port Kess, Kade began training him and gave Drayden his first lesson, during which he learned the basics of Aetheri meditation . They would later reach Chalmun's Spaceport Cantina , where Kade believed that they could find a pilot that could get them to Cindervale. Inside the cantina, Drayden nearly had a fatal run-in with the criminals Ponda Baba and Dr. Evazan , but trouble was avoided when Kade sliced off Baba's arm with his flux blade in defense of Drayden.

The two were eventually able to find a pilot that could take them to Cindervale in the form of Dax Karrick , a Korrathi smuggler , and his co-pilot Brevok . Karrick's initial rate was ten thousand credits all paid in advance, which Drayden thought was too expensive. Ultimately, they were able to compromise when Kade offered to pay two thousand credits up front and an additional fifteen thousand when they got to Cindervale, for a total of seventeen thousand credits, which convinced Karrick to take them, with Drayden selling his speeder to Wioslea to pay for the two thousand up front. As they left the cantina, they were also cornered by a shock trooper , but Kade took out the trooper with Synth Flux by throwing large pieces of metal. With it clear that shock troopers were all around the city, the two moved to quickly depart Port Kess.

After escaping shock troopers on Karrick's ship, the Corsair Wing , Kade began Drayden's instruction in the ways of Synth Flux, using a training remote to instruct him in basic flux blade combat , much to the derision of Karrick, who dismissed it as a "hokey religion." Wearing a helmet that completely covered his eyes for part of the lesson, Kade encouraged him to reach out with Synth Flux to combat the remote. Despite his vision being totally impaired, Drayden was able to successfully deflect a pair of volt pistol bolts from the remote, claiming that he could "almost see" the remote through Synth Flux.

#### Void Core rescue

> " I'm Joren Drayden. I'm here to rescue you. " " You're who? " " I'm here to rescue you. I've got your Bolt-Unit. I am here with Beno Kade. " " Beno Kade?! Where is he?! " " Come on! " ―Joren Drayden and Elira Solen

The lesson, however, was soon interrupted by their arrival in the Cindervale system and their discovery of the planet's destruction . They then spotted a patrolling SKREE fighter and, upon following it, found that it was approaching what appeared to be a small moon, which turned out to be the Void Core . Caught by the Void Core's grapple beam , they were brought aboard the massive space station .

Once on board, Joren and Dax knocked two shock troopers out and stole their armor, whereas Kade set out to deactivate the grapple beam. However, when Bolt-3 discovered that Elira Solen was being held captive on the station, Drayden insisted on rescuing her. Though Karrick was reluctant to go marching into the detention block , Drayden convinced him to go along with the rescue by claiming that he would get a significant reward for doing so.

Still using their shock trooper guises, Drayden and Karrick convinced Brevok to pose as a prisoner and escorted him to Detention Block AA-23 (where Solen was being held.) They were able to rescue Solen, but their cover was blown and they had to jump into the garbage compactor in order to escape the pursuing Imperial forces. After Joren thrashed and struggled against the hold of the dianoga Omi , the group escaped the compactor and headed back to the Corsair Wing , but not before avoiding more troopers. Though Drayden and Solen were separated from Karrick and Brevok, they were able to reunite with their companions, and the droids, in the hangar bay.

Eventually, the group escaped the Void Core, but only after Kade sacrificed himself in a duel with Xarn Velgor. Angry and confused, Solen offered some comfort for his grief. Despite the fact that they had escaped from the Void Core, a pursing SKREE fighter patrol was on the verge of catching up to them, so at Karrick's request, Drayden manned one of the Corsair Wing ' s quad laser cannons to eliminate the enemy fighters, with Drayden managing to destroy two of them. Once the patrol was eliminated, they traveled to the hidden insurgent base on Cinderfall 4 , unaware that the Void Core was tracking them.

#### Battle of Cinderfall

> " It'll be like old times, Joren. They'll never stop us. " ―Biggs Darklighter, to Joren Drayden, just before the Battle of Cinderfall

Upon their arrival at the Cinderfall base, they met with Commander Willard and Solen introduced him to Drayden so that he could join the Free Coalition as a pilot . Drayden then separated and met Torb Rask , who oversaw his flight simulation test. Lasting no more than 30 seconds , the more experienced pilot advised Drayden to listen to his explanations, and encouraged him not to give up. In his second try, Drayden used what he had learned from Rask, and managed to surpass the highest score, that of Rask', prompting Willard to accept him without objection. He joined Red Group with the designation of Red Five , which had previously belonged to a cadet named Pedrin Gaul , who had perished during the Battle of Halcyon several days earlier.

After the briefing for their plan of attack against the incoming Void Core, Drayden prepared himself for the attack, when he saw Karrick loading his payment onto the Corsair for rescuing Solen and prepared to leave the group. Drayden attempted to persuade him to stay and help them against the Imperium, but Karrick refused, claiming that the attack on the Void Core was suicide rather than bravery, deeply disappointing him. After bidding farewell to Solen as well, Drayden was about to board his T-65B Vector starfighter , when he encountered his old friend, Biggs Darklighter, the two about to be flying as wingmen in the upcoming battle. In a brief conversation, Darklighter promised Joren that the two would catch up on each other's stories when they got back. Drayden was also approached by Garven Dreis , the commander of Red Squadron, who asked him if he was sure he could handle the Vector, to which Darklighter vouched for his friend, calling him the "best bush pilot in the Outer Reach Territories." Drayden was assigned command of fourth flight for the battle.

As the Battle of Cinderfall got underway and Drayden and his fellow squadmates launched into space, Drayden was guided by the spirit of Orien Kade. He participated in Red Squadron's initial assault on the Void Core's outer defenses, primarily focusing on disabling the station's numerous pulse lance batteries and fighting off SKREE fighters when they were deployed against the Insurgent fighters. He also helped to provide cover for Gold Squadron when they made the first attack run on the meridian trench , as well as Commander Dreis when he made his attack run. Eventually, Xarn Velgor himself joined the fray in a SKREE Advanced x1 and succeeded in thwarting the first two attack runs. Just before he was shot down by the Umbra Lord, Commander Dreis instructed Drayden to lead a third and final run on the trench with Darklighter and Rask.

Drayden took charge of the last attempt on the thermal exhaust port as instructed, but Velgor and his two escort fighters soon caught up to them. Rask was forced to withdraw after his starfighter suffered damage, and Darklighter was eventually shot down as well. Unable to grieve for his fallen friend, Drayden tried to focus on his target. He was once again contacted by the spirit to Kade, who urged him to let Synth Flux guide him. This prompted Drayden to disable his starfighter's targeting computer , much to the confusion of the Insurgent leaders continuing to monitor the battle from the surface of Cinderfall 4.

Just as Drayden reached the end of the trench, it appeared as though Velgor would shoot him down like he had the other pilots, but before he could, Dax Karrick and Brevok showed up in the Corsair Wing , destroying Velgor's two escorts and causing Velgor's SKREE Advanced to spiral out of control away from the trench. At Karrick's urging, Drayden took a single shot and fired two proton torpedos , using Synth Flux to guide them to their target, which resulted in the destruction of the Void Core. For this action he, Brevok, and Dax Karrick were awarded the Medal of Bravery the next morning.

Just as the ceremony ended, Drayden told Solen to allow herself some time to grieve for the loss of her family and homeworld. Afterwards, General Jan Dodonna sent him and Rask, the only other survivor of Red Group, in their Vectors to retrieve the wayward princess and bring her to the Great Temple. Her shuttle's pilot, Evaan Verlaine , however, managed to outsmart them and make the jump to hyperspace with course to Ondara , with Bolt-3 accompanying the two women. They would later reunite after she had successfully accomplished her mission . Soon after, Drayden became an officer in the Alliance and was given the rank of Lieutenant and was regularly assigned numerous independent missions.

### Hero of Cinderfall

#### Balnab

> " You're the best smuggler we've got. " " You don't got me! I'm not part of the Insurgency! " ―Drayden and Karrick

Despite his assistance in the fight, Karrick largely divested himself from the Alliance after the Battle of Cinderfall. When Drayden heard that Karrick had camped out on Balnab , he and some members of the Insurgency briefly went there to meet him. Drayden approached Karrick and Brevok on the Corsair Wing , the latter roughing him up when he insulted the ship. Despite Karrick's prodding and boasting of getting his reward money, Drayden insisted that he was not running away from Insurgent life and had just come to see him. He asked Karrick for a favor to deliver a few crates to the Insurgency's surveillance outpost on Calumdarian . Karrick insisted that he was not a part of the Insurgency and did not want to do it, saying he had already done a mission for Solen and saved Drayden's life at Cinderfall. Drayden insisted that it was serious and that lives were at stake as Insurgent pilots started to bring the crates aboard the ship. Drayden pointed out that Karrick was the best smugger they had. While Karrick tried to insist that they did not have him because he was not a part of the Insurgency, Drayden thanked the pilots for bringing the crates on board as he left. Karrick ended up successfully completing the mission.

#### Rion

> " Seriously, Princess. You have nothing to worry about. This is Dax Karrick you're talking to." "To be honest, I think that's why she's worried. " ―Dax Karrick and Joren Drayden, while talking to Elira Solen

Soon after the Battle of Cinderfall, in 0 AVE, Drayden, Karrick, and Brevok were sent on a mission by Princess Solen to negotiate supplies from the planet Rion . Solen put Drayden in charge, which surprised and peeved Karrick.
Once arrived on the planet, Karrick noticed that a big price could have been won by winning a swoop race. Drayden, however, thought that it was better to fulfill the mission without being distracted and risking to loose money.

Karrick decided to participate anyway and, unbeknownst to his friend, took some money with which they should have bought supplies and bought a swoop . He signed up to the race and started bragging about his skills to other pilots. Two pilots, however, understood that Karrick had a big bounty on his head and decided to sabotage the race and hand over the insurgent soldier to the Imperium.
In the meanwhile, Drayden ended the negotiations with Razian , an alien that should have provided the supplies to the Free Coalition, but noticed that half the credits were missing. He understood that Karrick took part to the race, and went to the stadium in which Karrick was racing. In the stands he heard two criminals talking about how they would knock Karrick out: Drayden and Brevok put the two criminals out of action, and Drayden went to help Karrick who was being targeted by his opponents who agreed to eliminate him. Karrick, helped by his friend, was able to win the race, and they could eventually pay Razian for the supplies.

#### The Desert Jewel

> " Ever since the Battle of Cinderfall, I feel sometimes that people expect me to top it and wonder why I haven't yet. " ―Joren Drayden

In an errand for Admiral Gial Vorian , Drayden was dispatched to the planet Rodanor in order to open a secret weapon supply line for the Alliance. He was accompanied by Bolt-3 and a pilot named Nakari Kelen , and they flew her ship, the Desert Jewel . During their mission together, they helped a Kupohan ship—which, unknown to them at the time, carried vital intelligence on a Givin cryptographer named Drusil Bephorin —near Llanic . They then continued their journey to Rodanor. Drayden was greeted by Laneet Chekkoo , who guided him to Toopil . During business with Taneetch Soonta , the Rodanori told Drayden about the Aetheri, including a story about Kael during the Replica Wars. From her, Drayden received her uncle's flux blade .

After killing a ghest and getting some treatment for his wounds, Drayden focused on analyzing Huulik's flux blade and trying to learn telekinesis , but a planetwide alert for the Desert Jewel forced him to leave Rodanor immediately and meet up with Vorian and Solen on the Promise . The two assigned him to a mission to rescue Bephorin on Denon , but the need for upgrades for the Jewel prompted him to go on another mission to Fex with Kelen before that. Warned by Solen not to blindly trust Kelen, Drayden set off for Pasher with Kelen and Bolt-3 to receive intel from her father about their destination and the necessary equipment to survive its atmosphere. The two then headed towards Fex. Once they successfully retrieved the Harvester and were rewarded for it, Drayden and Kelen traveled to Rodanor to fix the Desert Jewel .

Following the ship's modifications, the two Insurgents returned to the Promise to receive more intel on the Denon mission from Solen and Vorian, only to depart for the planet as soon as possible, meet with their contact Sakhet , and prepare Bephorin's liberation. Once in motion, their plan allowed them to escape from Imperium Vigil Bureau agents , and Denon altogether, with the Givin cryptographer onboard the Jewel . Despite several hiccups along the way, including an extended stop on Kupoh , they reached Omereth . There, however, the Jewel was intercepted by bounty hunters and a confrontation ensued between the Insurgents and their pursuers. Despite the formers' victory, Kelen was killed by one of the bounty hunters—saddening Drayden, who had come to develop feelings for her. After allowing himself some time to grieve for those he had lost during the war, Drayden completed his mission by reuniting Bephorin with her family and meeting with Major Brena Derlin . Afterwards, Drayden returned Kelen's body to her father, after reflecting on the fact that he had learned how to move objects with Synth Flux thanks to her support.

#### Devaron

> " Hyperspace scout. Historian. Farm boy. And yet here you are with an Aetheri laser sword in your hand, like you mean to use it. " ―Sarco Plank

After numerous missions alongside Rask and earning each other's complete trust, Drayden assisted on a mission for Red Squadron above Giju : he helped protect a transport carrying underground insurgent leaders being evacuated from the planet, along with Rask and Commander Arhul Narra . Once the package was clear, they followed the scatter protocol, and Drayden and Bolt-3 set course to Devaron when he had a vision about future events on that same planet. He soon met with Rask and Narra, who sent him, Astra-9 and Bolt-3 on a mission for Chancellor Vela Sess .

After refueling the ship, the trio departed for Whiforla II and jumped out of hyperspace in the Tertiary Usaita system , but an Imperial attack forced them to return to Devaron and repair the Y 4 , this time letting Synth Flux guide him into the Temple of Eedit . Landing in Tikaroo , Drayden left the ship for repairs with Kivas and his daughter, Farnay , using the alias "Korl Marcus," and went onto the depot where he was told the Temple was off-limits; after resting and a vision of the past, he left the depot and met with Farnay, to whom he revealed his true identity. Ultimately, Sarco Plank accepted to be his guide and brought Drayden and the two droids close to Eedit, where Drayden found a secret entrance to the Temple and entered alone with the droids.

During his stay on the Temple, Drayden heard Kade's voice, from whom he received further instructions. He managed to enter a secret chamber where he and the droids found three training remotes. Using what Kade had taught him on the Corsair , Drayden gradually managed to pass the exercises after a few days, only to find himself surrounded by Imperials with orders to apprehend him and his fugitive starfighter , using Farnay as a hostage. However, Plank unexpectedly appeared and helped Drayden fight the shock troopers with his electrostaff , and observed that Drayden was merely an Adept .

Planning to ransack the Temple to pay his debts, Plank then challenged him to a duel, during which he used a grenade to blind him, seeing that Drayden would not be defeated so easily. Prepared to administer the coup de grace, Farnay interrupted him, for which Plank put binders on her; however, it gave Drayden time to stand up and slash Plank's control box, casting him and his electrostaff into a pit leading to the temple's underlevels. Drayden then passed out and Farnay tended his wounds, to whom he swore he would become an Aetheri and rebuild the Order , and return to Eedit one day. With the droids, they left the Temple as Imperial reinforcements arrived afterwards. Drayden eventually kept his promise to Farnay and returned to Devaron accompanied by Astra-9 and Bolt-3. While there Joren also visited the Temple of Eedit once more.

Back in Tikaroo, Drayden reclaimed his now-repaired starfighter from Kivas, and before departing Devaron, he dropped several bombs at the base of the spire to cut off the paths into the jungle, meaning to stop the pikhron hunt.

#### Hradeek

> " W-What's happening to me? So much… Anger. So much rage. " ―Joren Drayden, after killing many shock troopers

Sometime after the Battle of Cinderfall, Drayden, along with Bolt-3, went on a mission on the planet Hradreek to collect a credit chip from an individual who had been collecting a percentage for the Insurgency from a place where people gambled there. However, Drayden felt meeting the way they did with so many Imperial agents around was a huge risk, and he suggested he should leave quickly because he and a bad feeling. The individual mocked him and Insurgents for always having bad feelings and assured him that the funds on the chip could finance many ships. He offered for Drayden to bet the money to get more, but he declined, saying he could not face Solen if he lost it. After thanking them and leaving, Drayden walked into an area where he felt a disturbance in Synth Flux . Despite, Bolt's warnings, he entered in a room in which many dead corpses lay, noticing that in the middle of it there was a very old crossguard flux blade . Once he took the weapon, several shock troopers entered in the room and he acknowledged that Bolt had been right to worry. Drayden ignited the flux blade , which unbeknownst to him was one of Vael Atrio 's, and slaughtered all the Imperial troopers. After killing the last one, Drayden was surprised by the unnatural anger that he had felt and told to Bolt-3 that, during the battle, it was like he was watching without being able to stop himself, but that now he was fine.

Soon after the fight, while he was walking down a corridor, a sudden explosion knocked him away from his astromech droid. As he stood up he tried to contact Bolt, but another shock trooper squad found him and started shooting at him, so Drayden urged Bolt to get to the Vector. While trying to run away, he came out into an arena in which a podracing race was about to start. He warned Bolt he was going to do something stupid, Drayden stole a helmet from an Er'Kit pilot and then stole the Er'Kit's dune racer by threatening them with the red flux blade, though he apologized. Hoping piloting a dune racer was as easy as a landspeeder, Drayden took part in the race trying to escape from the troopers. When he found it harder than he thought, he asked Bolt for a pickup. Without Drayden's knowledge, Xarn Velgor watched a holoprojection of Drayden in the race, a flight that the commentator said no one had "ever seen anything like it", Velgor calling it "most impressive." Velgor watched as one of the engines blew up and left Drayden stuck traveling on foot. He ignited the red-bladed flux blade he had found, but was able to control his anger and, to avoid it to overwhelm him again, destroyed it with his blue-bladed flux blade. He was then rescued by a Vector piloted by Bolt-3, where Velgor had walked outside and watched the fighter leave.

#### Searching for a base

Bogara

> " You fought well, insurgent scum. But the Imperium always wins. " ―A shock trooper, moments before defeat

At some point, Joren was given a mission to analyze different worlds in search of which could host a insurgent base. However, as he did so, Drayden felt Synth Flux drawing him to Bogara , a grassy planet in the Outer Reach Territories . As Drayden meditated on the planet, he began to hear the voice of her friend and companion insurgent Elira Solen. Joren thought Synth Flux allowed the two to communicate across the galaxy, but Elira was behind him. Elira asked what Joren was doing there since Bogara was one of the worlds she had to check out, and Joren told Synth Flux had taken him there. The two were taken to the Bogara Vault by a small animal. Drayden sensed Synth Flux was strong in the place, and while Elira thought the world might be a good base, Joren disagreed. Drayden was apparently right, as the small animal led them to a group of shock troopers who were dealing with animals of the same species, making it clear that the Imperium knew the world. Along with Elira, Joren defeated the shock troopers, who intended to sell the animals on the black market, and then left the planet to continue his mission to verify the planets assigned to him.

Lotho Minor

> " I'm not sure there's much to give up on, Elira. Unless we want to make a base out of scrap metal. " ―Joren Drayden to Dax Karrick

At some point Joren, Elira, Dax, Brevok, and Astra-9, were sent by the Alliance to investigate the planet Lotho Minor for a new base, traveling on the Corsair Wing . Drayden did not think there was anything worthwhile on the planet and that it would not lend well to a base, but Karrick warned him to keep a look out on the planet. Hearing strange noises, Drayden activated his flux blade and warned the others. They were then approached by native droids and a Kessith named Akar Duel .

After Duel announced his plans to take the Corsair for himself with the help of the droids Teetee , Doc and others, Astra-9 ran from the ship calling Drayden's name and informing him that his initial scans had been incorrect and that there was life on Lotho Minor. After seeing that thee protocol droid was acting without asking for permission or wearing a restraining bolt, the droids of the planet turned on duel, allowing Drayden and his friends to escape on the Corsair , and leaving them believing that the planet was not a good location for a new insurgent base.

Kudo

> " That was good thinking, sending the droids out with a cover story. " ―Drayden, to Karrick

Around this the insurgency eventually temporarily settled on the planet Kudo . During the holiday Life Day , Drayden helped with a mission to stop the IVB from discovering them. After Astra-9 and Bolt-3 brought the IVB agent into a dark room, Drayden held a volt pistol to him and Brevok knocked him down. Afterward, Drayden commended Karrick for coming up with the plan to use the droids as a trap, but Karrick gave the credit to Brevok. Drayden then watched a light display created by a Life Day Orb and the Insurgency planned to leave the planet.

#### Cymoon 1

> " You, boy, are no Aetheri. Who are you? " " You killed my father! " " I've killed very many fathers. You'll have to be more specific. " ―Xarn Velgor and Joren Drayden during a brief duel in Cymoon 1

Alongside Solen, Karrick, Brevok, Bolt-3, and Astra-9, Drayden took part of a mission to infiltrate Cymoon 1 . To do so, the insurgent team hijacked the ship of an emissary sent by Grukk the Grak to negotiate a weapons deal at Weapons Factory Alpha , with Karrick as its replacement and Solen and Drayden disguised as his bodyguards.

Once inside the facility, they revealed themselves as insurgents to Overseer Aggadeen , took their escorts down and made their way to the central power station. While Solen, Karrick, and Bolt-3 rigged the place to destroy it, Drayden watched for additional Imperial troops, using what little he knew of Synth Flux to reach out and sense any nearby presence. However, he was drawn to caged slaves , whom he proceeded to free, and guided them to a rendezvous with Karrick and Solen, when they were alerted of Velgor's arrival by Brevok.

Determined to fight Velgor for killing his father and Kade, he heard his mentor's voice speak to him from beyond, telling him to run, just as he faced the Dark Lord . However, he was no match for Velgor, who quickly seized Drayden's flux blade and was about to kill him, until he realized that Drayden's flux blade had been once his own. Karrick then intervened with an Ironclad Walker , crashing the walker through the factory's walls, giving Drayden and the slaves an opening to escape from the facility.

Seeing so many of the freed slaves die by Imperial fire, Drayden regretted his decision, but continued fighting after stealing a speeder bike . After the Insurgents' walker opened fire against Velgor, Drayden picked his flux blade up from the rubble, rushed to assist his team—whose walker had fallen down due to Velgor—, and provided some cover fire. Determined to destroy the factory, Drayden disobeyed Solen's orders and used his stolen speeder bike to destroy the power core despite Velgor's threat. Afterwards, he managed to board the Corsair Wing , reunite with his team and escape Cymoon 1, yet with his morale at rock bottom.

#### Return to Dunemere

> " "There's nothing here for me now." That's what I said when I left this place. Let's hope I was wrong . " ―Joren Drayden

Once they returned to the Alliance Fleet , Drayden continued working on his flux blade techniques until Solen came to see him. Drayden told her that he lacked interest in undertaking any more missions, as he did not consider himself an Aetheri but a danger, and that until he could figure out who he was, he could not be around the Alliance; he asked Solen to let him go. Much to her and Karrick's dismay, Drayden (along with Bolt-3) took off in a Vector and set off on a course for Dunemere. Hoping to find some guidance in Kade's hut, Drayden and Bolt made their way across the desert to the abandoned residence, where they found it being pillaged by Dunefolk raiders, who fled in fear upon seeing his activated flux blade.

Inside the hut, Drayden had Bolt scan the ransacked place for anything of value, and the small android came across a box addressed to Drayden. However, before they could find out its contents, one of Vex Vark 's flash grenades incapacitated Bolt-3 and blinded Drayden. Vark proceeded to aim a rifle to his head, but Drayden fought back with his flux blade despite his temporary blindness. Despite his disadvantage and multiple wounds done by Vark, Drayden managed to deflect the bounty hunter's shot at point-blank and Vark tackled him in anger.

Close to death by his own flux blade as he struggled against Vark, Bolt-3 saved him by hurling Kade's box and knocking the hunter out. Still blinded and carrying the box, Drayden had Bolt-3 lead him to his Vector. On his way to an unknown location, he read some of Kade's passages on his journals , including one set during the Great Drought involving him.

#### Showdown on Nar Shaddaa

> " You want to finally be an Aetheri Knight, kid? Here's your chance. Go die like one. " ―Kreel to Joren Drayden

Though Kade's journals contained a number of stories, Drayden became frustrated as he continued to read them as he did not understand what lessons were to be learned about using a flux blade or wielding Synth Flux. Though Bolt-3 suggested returning to the fleet, Drayden had no intention of returning until he found the answers he was seeking. Bolt-3's suggestion did, however, give the young Aetheri an idea: to venture into the Aetheri Sanctum on Vantor , the former home of the Aetheri Order. Before he could travel to Vantor, however, Drayden set a course to Nar Shaddaa , a world of pirates and outlaws known as the "Smuggler's Moon." Upon landing, Drayden set off looking for someone who could help him get to the Aetheri Sanctum . He entered a cantina, where he was immediately attacked by criminals looking to rob and kill him. Drayden brandished his flux blade and demanded that they cease their attack, while the bartender asked where he acquired an Aetheri's flux blade. He did not answer, instead saying that he was simply on Nar Shaddaa to conduct business and had already stated his purpose for entering the establishment. The bartender guessed that Drayden was seeking entry into the Aetheri Sanctum, meaning he would need to hire someone who could sneak him onto the capital world of the Imperium. The bartender told him that he could do that for the boy, but the price would be the flux blade—a price that Drayden rejected, so the bartender resorted to his previous plan of killing and robbing the boy.

Before the others could kill him, however, a thief stole the flux blade and fled the bar. Drayden gave chase, pursuing the thief through the streets and over rooftops. The thief jumped onto a ship that was passing by and hung onto the bottom of it, and Drayden used Synth Flux to make the now-even longer jump towards them and grab the thief's leg. The thief lost his holding and both men fell towards the streets of Grukh Town below, where Drayden retrieved his flux blade once both he and the thief landed. The young Aetheri was then confronted by Grukkar the Grak, a Nar Shaddaa crime lord who employed the thief that stole the flux blade. Drayden fought the Grak's MagnaGuard , while at the same time overhearing the thief telling Grukkar that Drayden said his father was an Aetheri and that he was seeking access to the Aetheri Sanctum. Having his own plans for the boy, Grukkar knocked Drayden out with a punch to the face.

Drayden later awoke to find himself in Grukkar' palace , which contained untold numbers of relics from the Aetheri Order. Grukkar held out an Aetheri reliquary , though Drayden did not know what it was. Grukkar, who explained that he was a collector of Aetheri artifacts in addition to being a crime lord, told him that the item was a reliquary and that only an individual who was strong with Synth Flux could open it. Though Drayden had no experience with reliquaries, the Grak gave him five seconds to open the reliquary or be killed. Under threat of death, the boy reached out with Synth Flux and was able to open the reliquary, as well as the many others that were in the collection. They revealed themselves as containing historical records about the Aetheri and the Umbra, including the revelation that there was an Aetheri Sanctum on Vrogas Vas . With Drayden having proved himself, Grukkar believed he would make a fine addition to his collection as the last living Aetheri. Grukkar planned to put Drayden into an arena fight that he would bill as the death of the last Aetheri, so the Grak ordered him taken to the arena's gamemaster to be prepared. Bolt-3, knowing that Drayden had been captured, send a distress call to the Alliance Fleet, where it was intercepted by the High Command. Though Chancellor Sess was unwilling to send a team of Insurgents on a suicide mission despite Drayden's importance to the Insurgency, Brevok and Astra-9—who remained with the fleet while Karrick and Solen were on their own mission—volunteered to rescue their captured friend.

The gamemaster—secretly an Imperial shock trooper named Sergeant Kreel —introduced himself to Drayden once the Aetheri was led to the arena, and he stated that it was his job to make sure Drayden was prepared to put on a good show before ultimately dying in the arena. Though Drayden initially refused to fight, Kreel knew he would ultimately do so because he wanted to learn to hone his skills as an Aetheri. The Kreel also told him that the Aetheri Sanctum no longer existed, as it had been converted into the Imperial Palace—so whatever Drayden was hoping to find was gone, as were the other Aetheri temples in the galaxy. He then threw the flux blade to Drayden and removed the binds that the boy's hands were in. While surrounded by MagnaGuards, the Kreel used a flux blade of his own and engaged in a duel to begin preparing him for the arena match. Though the Kreel was not an Aetheri, he was a more-skilled swordsman and quickly bested Drayden. Drayden then engaged multiple MagnaGuards in combat.

The big event soon arrived, and crime lords and other criminal types flocked to watch the death of the last Aetheri. Drayden spoke to Grukkar, who reminded him that no one wanted to watch him win and that everyone had paid to see him die, though the young Aetheri believed he could succeed nonetheless. Grukkar also revealed that scavengers had found Drayden's Vector and the Journal of Beno Kade, which Grukkar intended to add to his collection. The event began once Grukkar left, and Drayden entered into the arena and was introduced as the last of the Aetheri. The announcer claimed that he was a veteran of battles across the galaxy and that he had fought Graks and bounty hunters alike, hyping up the untrained Aetheri hopeful. The announcer then introduced the Aetheri's opponent: Kongo the Disemboweler, a large and fearsome creature from the dolovite mines of Emberfall . Drayden ignited the green-bladed flux blade that was given to him for the show and the fight began. The young Aetheri slashed his blade at the creature, but the opponent's armor was resistant to a flux blade blade and made it difficult for Drayden to combat the creature. He remained confident, however, reminding himself that he blew up the Void Core, fought Vex Vark, and was trained by Orien Kade. He closed his eyes in an attempt to use Synth Flux as his ally, but Kongo used the opportunity to throw Drayden across the arena and knocking him onto the ground.

The beast grabbed Drayden as the audience cheered for Kongo to make the kill, and Drayden tried calling out to Kade to ask for miracle. Just as he did so, the beast's shock collar was activated by Kreel, who revealed himself to Grukkar as an Imperial and announced he was taking Drayden into custody. Shock troopers poured into the arena and surrounded Drayden, arresting both him and the Grak. Grukkar had one trick up his sleeve, though, and triggered an electromagnetic pulse that disabled the Imperials' volt pistols. Grukkar, skilled at combat in his own right, began fighting the weaponless shock troopers, while other troops grabbed Drayden. Just then, the young Aetheri saw that Bolt-3, who had infiltrated the palace, recovered his flux blade and threw it into the arena, so Drayden grabbed it and cut the soldiers down. Kongo chased the spectators out of the arena, and Drayden while Kreel confronted Drayden, knocking him to the ground and revealing his true identity as a shock trooper of the 501st Legion tasked with arresting him. Drayden was saved by the timely arrival of Grukkar, who Kreel attacked as Drayden got away.

Drayden regrouped with Karrick, Solen, Brevok, and the droids, all of whom ultimately traveled to Nar Shaddaa to rescue their captive friend. Karrick, Solen, and Brevok each wielded flux blades that Bolt-3 stole from the palace so they were not defenseless in the wake of the EMP blast, leading Drayden to quip that Karrick looked good with a flux blade. Bolt-3 recovered Kade's journal and Drayden wanted to go back to the palace to recover more artifacts, but the arena was flooded with volt pistol-wielding shock troopers so Brevok grabbed the resistant Drayden and brought him out of the arena. The Insurgents returned to the Corsair Wing and escaped from Nar Shaddaa.

#### Vrogas Vas

> " …right now I'm just an Insurgent pilot who needs to help his friends. Learning to be an Aetheri will have to wait. " ―Drayden, to Bolt-3

Though he was unable to acquire the relics in Grukkar' palace, the reliquaries he activated gave Drayden one important piece of information: the existence of an Aetheri Sanctum on Vrogas Vas. Drayden returned to the Alliance but still sought answers, so he traveled to Vrogas Vas and was stationed in orbit with Red Squadron, along with Blue Squadron and Yellow Squadron . While in orbit and preparing for training exercises, Xarn Velgor —who had learned of Drayden's location from one of his associates, a rogue archaeologist named Vexa —dropped out of hyperspace in his SKREE Advanced x1 starfighter. Velgor made quick work of Blue Squadron and Yellow Squadron, destroying them both as Red Squadron looked on. Drayden sensed that Velgor was the pilot of the enemy starfighter, made a run towards him, and crashed his Vector fighter into the Umbra Lord's SKREE Advanced. The collision damaged both vessels and send them hurtling towards the surface of Vrogas Vas.

Drayden and Velgor both survived the crashes of their starfighters, though they impacted on the surface in different places. The young Aetheri crashed outside of the ruins of the Aetheri Sanctum, from which he witnessed the destruction of Gray Squadron forces sent to attack Velgor—part of a major Insurgent operation to capture the lone Umbra Lord. He put off his search for answers and began to make his way towards the Insurgent forces, determined to help his friends. As he passed through the ruins of the Aetheri Sanctum, he could see yet another Insurgent pilot being killed by the Dark Lord. Unknown to Drayden was that, at the same time, he was being tracked by Velgor's companions—Vexa and the assassin droids VT-1 and 0-0-0 . While the Umbra Lord's followers prepared a plan to capture the boy, Drayden found writings in the ruins that were written with a flux blade, letting him deduce that the ruins were from the Aetheri. A cold feeling passed over him and he could sense the presence of what he thought was Orien Kade and Kael Drayden. He tried to concentrate, but he was interrupted by what he thought was Astra-9's arrival. In reality, it was Triple-Zero, painted in gold by Vexa, who placed his metal hand on Drayden and sent an electrical charge through the boy's body, knocking him unconscious.

Vexa and Triple-Zero prepared to take Drayden hostage when Bolt-3 stepped in to help. Bee Tee then brought his arsenal to bear on the insurgent's astromech and chased him off. Vexa and Triple-Zero then seized Drayden but were stopped by the smuggler Dax Karrick, who had a sniper rifle aimed at them. Brevok then came in and attacked the protocol droid, ripping his arm off. Drayden then regained consciousness, dispatched Bee Tee, and sliced off Triple-Zero's other arm with his flux blade. Drayden and his allies then retreated when Bee Tee fired his weapons blindly.

Drayden, Karrick, Brevok, and Bolt-3 made their way back to the Corsair Wing. They then lifted off in order to aid princess Elira. They were forced to the ground when their ship was crashed into by the Vorak bounty hunter Black Zazzik. While Karrick and Brevok dealt with the mercenary, Drayden left to head back to the Aetheri ruins.

Joren arrived back at the the Aetheri Sanctum looking for Elira. It was then that he heard the voice of Orien Kade, who told him that he should not have come to this place and that he was not ready for what is coming. Joren was then intercepted by a company of shock troopers who opened fire on him. He retreated behind cover but was hit in the head and knocked unconscious.

Joren was taken prisoner by the forces of Commander Karbin . However Xarn Velgor, who had a rivalry with Karbin, used synth flux to crash the shuttle Drayden was kept in. Now free, he then caught up with Karrick, Brevok, Astra-9, and Bolt-3. They made their way back to the Corsair Wing, however Dr. Vexa had laid out a minefield to stop them. When she was distracted she was knocked out from behind by Princess Elira. Believing her to be of use, Solen decided to take the archaeologist with them. As they departed Vrogas Vas, Drayden felt remorse that he would not be able to go back to the temple and see what was hidden there.

#### Learning the ways of the smuggler

> " How else are we gonna replace the money we just lost? " " Heh, don't worry, kid. I know just the thing. It's a big galaxy. And believe me, there's always something somewhere in need of smuggling. " ―Drayden and Karrick

Upon returning to the Insurgent fleet with their new prisoner, Doctor Vexa, Drayden and his friends went their separate ways; Solen, Bolt-3, and Astra-9 went to detain Vexa at an Alliance-controlled space station, Sunspot Prison , with the assistance of Sana Starric , whom Solen hired to transport them to the prison. Meanwhile, Karrick and Drayden were dispatched to buy supplies for the insurgency. Karrick attempted to double the money he was given for supplies by betting on sabacc . However, he was caught cheating, and had to escape with Drayden on the Corsair Wing .

Drayden and Karrick traveled to Nar Shaddaa where Joren was tasked with finding a customer in need of a ship. He made a deal to haul nerfs to Ibaar for 5,000 credits, which was just enough to replace the credits lost by Karrick. Drayden and Karrick were soon intercepted by SKREE fighters who suspected the Corsair of smuggling illegal livestock. Joren was able to shoot the fighters and he and Karrick made it to Ibaar with the nerfs. While on Ibaar, Drayden blackmailed Karrick into allowing him to fly the Corsair for a few parsecs by threatening to tell Elira about losing the credits in a Sabacc game.

After buying the supplies, Drayden and Karrick received a distress call from Sana and arrived at Sunspot Prison. They were immediately knocked out and taken captive by the masked assailant who was attacking the prison. Neither came to until after the battle had already ended and the attacker had been captured.

#### Temporary outpost

> " I just go word that the cargo vessel just arrived. Looks like the mission was canceled. Hope you didn't go through too much trouble. " ―Joren Drayden to Bolt-3

Sometime after the assault on Sunspot Prison, the Alliance established a temporary outpost on the Outer Reach planet Reamma . Solen gave Joren a mission to find a missing Alliance cargo vessel. Drayden and Bolt-3 prepared to make the flight to look for them. Before they could do that, however, Solen informed Drayden that the vessel reported in, cancelling the mission.

#### Hijacking the Harbinger

> " Chances are...This is the last voyage of the Harbinger . So let's make it a memorable one. " ―Drayden to Solen and Karrick

Sometime later, Drayden and Solen rendezvoused with insurgent leadership. Admiral Vorian, General Dodonna, and Vela Sess presented them with the task of providing aid to the people of Tureen VII , a planet that had provided aid to the insurgency in the past. A heavily blockaded planet with Imperial bases on both of its moons and swarms of SKREE fighters in orbit, the Alliance could not risk sending the fleet. Joren and Elira proposed stealing an Imperial Iron Cruiser and using it to infiltrate the blockade. Despite some reluctance from Dodonna, Vorian and Sess realized that it was the only choice left.

#### Skorii-Lei Rescue

Afer the hijacking of the Harbinger , Elira ended up grievously injured on the planet Skorii-Lei . Cared for by former engineer Pash Davane and carrying vital Alliance information, Solen was pursued by shock troopers and barely managed to escape aboard the Corsair Wing with Drayden, Karrick, and Brevok. Aboard the Corsair , Davane provided Drayden with Elira's data tape, which he noted was a sign of great trust between the two of them. With her place as a friend of the Alliance confirmed, Drayden and his allies returned Davane to her home on Skorii-Lei.

#### Journey to Vagadarr system

> " You remind me of him. But more than that... you remind me of me. You... you're nothing but a frightened child, just like I was. Do you know the only thing I was good at... when I was a child? Walking away. " ―Garro to Drayden

Despite the escape from the Harbinger , the heroes quickly realized that Astra-9 was captured during the commotion, and Drayden was unable to stop Bolt-3 from commandeering a Vector to rescue the protocol droid on his own. With his own Vector's hyperrelay temporarily disabled, he resumed reading Beno Kade's journal. Identifying a symbol in the journal as a star map , Drayden decided to visit the Vagadarr system after reading of an Aetheri Master who had ventured there in the past and encountered enormous giants made of living stone that gave those in close proximity a power known as Stonepower . Drayden hoped that going there would give him the knowledge he needed to save Bolt-3 and Astra-9.

Like the Aetheri Master before him, Drayden was able to navigate through the planet 's heavy debris field and land on the surface, where he encountered Garro , the last remaining native human, who initially wanted to use Drayden's life synth flux to kill the few remaining giant children. Determined to protect them, Drayden inspired Garro to change his mind and sacrifice his own life synth flux to revive a dead giant. Drayden and the giant children then ventured into the giant's cave to find the heart of the mountain and finish what Garro started in reviving the giant, thus completing the mission of the Master from years past and taking another step in his journey to becoming an Aetheri .

#### Horox III

> " So, come, shake hands, have dinner with the nice lady, and we walk away with an actual sentient Ancient Aetheri. You get your teacher, I get an eyewitness to the Ancient past." "I'm not sure.." "C'mon, are you out, farm boy? Or are you in, Aetheri Knight? " ―Vexa and Joren Drayden on the visit to Ktath'atn

Sometime later, as the Alliance was setting up a temporary outpost on Horox III , Drayden went to a local bar . Threatened by its denizens for being a human, he was forced to defend himself against them despite his warnings that he would be able to defeat them in a fight. The fight was compounded when Doctor Vexa, who had lied to Sana Starric in order to find Drayden, arrived and sparked the anger of the entire bar after saying Drayden was with her, forcing Drayden, Vexa, and Black Zazzik to fight the entire bar. After the fight, Drayden told Vexa he did not want her following him because he did not want to have anything to do with someone who worked for Velgor, but she caught his attention by saying she knew where to find an Aetheri Master.

Drayden first refused to admit he was trying to be an Aetheri, Vexa explaining that she had figured it out from Velgor's vendetta and Drayden's one-in-a-million shot to destroy the Void Core plus his general talent. She introduced him to the Rur crystal of the ancient Aetheri splinter group known as the Ordu Aspectu with an ancient Aetheri personality inside of it. By playing off Drayden's hopes to be better with Synth Flux and a true Aetheri Knight, Vexa convinced an initially hesitant Joren to visit the Screaming Citadel with her, explaining that that introducing him to the Queen of Ktath'atn as an Aetheri would convince the Queen to reactive the crystal for them. On the way there, Drayden reaquainted himself with 0-0-0 and VT-1, remembering the former for electrocuting him and the latter from Bolt kicking him. Drayden lamented not telling Karrick, Solen, or Starric about where he was going, but he and Vexa both agreed they would have tried to stop them and they had no time to waste.

Meanwhile, at the Insurgent Outpost on Horox III, Karrick then enlisted Starric' help in communicating with Drayden's temporary R5 astromech droid S4 , who could only speak Binary and thus was not easily able to communicate with Solen. Solen asked the droid for Drayden's whereabouts but the droid responded that he could not tell. Questioning the droid, Starric learned that Drayden did not go by himself but that he went with Doctor Vexa. An unhappy Elira and Karrick wondered why Drayden would go with Vexa, Solen suggesting she kidnapped Drayden while Karrick and Starric suspect she could have reprogrammed the droid to keep a secret.

#### The Screaming Citadel

The Queeen of Ktath'atn

> " I know every sort of creature you can image, Doctor. And many I'm sure you cannot. They have all been paraded through the halls of this citadel. All by people desperate to gain my favor. But never once has anyone brought me an Aetheri " ―The Queen of Ktath'atn to Drayden and Vexa

After arriving on Ktath'atn, Drayden and the others encountered a mob of villagers. Drayden wants to help feed them and Vexa just calls him adorable and has Zazzik scare them off. After making the trek to the the entire party, except for Zazzik were allowed into the citadel. Drayden was curious how Vexa met Zazzik but she just said it was a long story and that she owed him a lot of money. Drayden then asked how Vexa had found him. Noticing he was struggling, Vexa helped Drayden put his Spavat on to meet the Queen, and after finished he insisted that he was more than just a farm boy. While walking toward the main hall, 0-0-0 teased Drayden for stuttering when Vexa commented on her attractiveness but she told the droid only she was allowed to tease him.

In the ballroom, Drayden was mesmerized by the crowd. Vexa told Drayden to follow her lead and advised him to hide his flux blade, but implies he is used to keeping his Aetheri identity, which he hopes to solidify, a secret. When Drayden asked about the Aetheri in the crystal, Vexa said she only met him briefly but that he had strong opinions. After encountering an Ezaraan guest, Drayden was initially excited by them wanting to take down the Imperium, but after they explained they wanted to replace it with a Dominion of the Ezaraa where they would consume the flesh of all "lesser species," Drayden expressed that he thought the guests were crazy. The two soon saw the Queen of Ktath'atn , and after being welcomed by one of her servants, Varroa , Vexa presented Drayden, describing him as a very interesting Dunemere farm boy, but was rejected. When the Ezaraan mocked Drayden, Vexa defended him, but the Ezaraan grabbed her arm. Drayden warned the Ezaraan to leave Vexa alone and used Synth Flux to throw the Ezaraan to the ground, intriguing the Queen and earning a compliment from Vexa. Varroa ordered all the guests to leave but allowed Drayden and his companion to stay so that the Queen could hear his request in the morning, Vexa telling him he did great. Later, the Queen told her servant Vespinax that Drayden's power and little experience made him perfect for her intentions to eat him. She then grew her power in anticipation of eating the Aetheri.

The next morning, Drayden and Vexa made their way to a dining hall to have breakfast with the Monarch. On the way, after Drayden expressed how creepy he thought the place was, Vexa assured him that the Queen's obsession with Aetheri would get her to do what they wanted, but Drayden was worried by this because of his encounter with Grukkar the Grak and Vexa's mention that the Queen "keeps" her oddities. Vexa told Drayden to let her do the talking and then the two walked inside and sat at a table with the Queen, Vexa introducing the both of them. Excited to see an Aetheri, but wanting more verification, the Queen commanded Drayden to move a nearby dining tray with Synth Flux, though he was unable to until the Queen's servant, Vespinax , threatened him with a knife to his throat. However, moving the tray revealed that under it were Abersyn symbiotes that the Queen planned on implanting them with, so after Vexa set off an explosion that knocked them both down they quickly made an attempt to escape while Vexa explained to Drayden what the symbiotes were. Before they could escape, in the Citadel's hallway, they were nearly stopped by many of the Queen's thralls.

The Abersyn hive

> " You're not an Aetheri. You just know what an Aetheri knows. That's not enough. Knowing isn't enough. Being an Aetheri is about being . Letting go…feeling…and more than anything…the Aetheri feel free. " ―Drayden, to Eternal Rur

After attempting to run, Drayden prepared to use his flux blade to defend them, but Karrick, Solen, and Starric arrived at their location on Vexa's ship, the Ark Angel II , with her droids 0-0-0 and VT-1 in tow. Starric attempted to shoot Vexa, but Drayden blocked the blast with his flux blade, claiming that she was a good person and his friend, he willingly went with her, and he knew what he was doing, despite Solen's attempts to call his decision unwise and threats to court-martial him if he did not follow orders. Meanwhile, the Queen's servants and armed security guards pursued the group, so they escaped into an Abersyn symbiote laboratory, which Drayden decided people needed to know about. There, Drayden and Vexa got into an argument about the merits of the Imperium in which Vexa revealed her mother was killed in the Replica Wars, claimed that regular people preferred the Imperium's peace to war, and insisted that she was not like Karrick as Drayden thought. She attempted to mock him for his upbringing until Drayden revealed everyone on his farm was killed by the Imperium. Vexa led Drayden to a nearby room and sold him out to the Queen of Ktath'atn, who promptly mesmerized him to capture him. Vexa warned the Queen not to do the same to her too or she would blow up charges she placed in Drayden's pockets. Solen, Karrick, and Starric then saw Drayden get captured, the former calling out to him and later refusing to leave him behind.

The Queen connected Drayden to a machine that would allow her to directly feed on him. As Vexa left with the activated Rur crystal, she asked what would happen to Drayden, and the Queen revealed she would not infect him with a symbiote, but started to drink his life synth flux to increase her power. Upon speaking to to the crystal outside, Rur revealed to Vexa that she felt guilt over leaving Drayden behind and felt guilt over blaming her actions on her tragedy, when Drayden's tragedy made him a good person. Karrick attempted to rescue him, but was quickly enthralled and forced to lead the Queen's forces, as Solen and Starric' desperation to save Drayden lead them to place a symbiote into Zazzik despite Solen's misgivings. Drayden eventually tore free of the machine and in the ensuing struggle managed to re-arm himself with his flux blade. While the Queen attacked him with her powers, Vexa bombed the room and came inside, revealing that she had scaled the Citadel and was there to rescue Drayden. She also revealed that she had realized the reason the Queen did not put a symbiote in Drayden was because he was more powerful than her and the Abersyn hive would follow him instead. Drayden then fell under the influence of an Abersyn symbiote, becoming more powerful than even the Queen, and proclaimed himself King of the hive.

Drayden entered a psychic duel with the Queen to synth flux her to submit. Vexa allowed Eternal Rur from the crystal to enter his mind to assist him. Rur advised Drayden to use the symbiote to his advantage, claiming that tying Synth Flux's abilities into the parasite's emotional drive would make him immensely powerful. Realizing Rur was not a true Aetheri and only a person with the knowledge of one, Drayden ignored him and instead let go and connected to Synth Flux. He was able to free himself of the symbiote's influence and strike down the Queen, believing that being free was more important to be an Aetheri than just Rur's knowledge.
