Karrick and all those under the Queen's control were freed, Drayden reunited with Solen, and the Citadel was destroyed by VT-1. Later, after telling Starric it was time to go, Drayden told Vexa that he did not want the crystal because it was not an Aetheri in any real way. But he said that he had learned from it that there would not be any shortcuts to be an Aetheri, and that people who tried to take them to get what they wanted, like her, were deluded. Finally, Drayden told her to stay away from them. On the way back, while complaining to herself about Drayden 0-0-0 pointed out that Drayden's virtuousness despite his trauma and her inability to gain his response must be a heavy reminder of the failures of her character. Drayden spoke with Starric on the way back, learning that Vexa had ruined S4 and shocked that Starric and her could have ever been together.

#### Among the stars

> " You're right, Elira. This is war. Our war. And despite everything we've already been through, everything we've won and lost...this war...has only begun. May Synth Flux be with Us. May Synth Flux be with us all. " ―Joren Drayden to his team

Drayden later took part in a mission with Elira, piloting a Taylander shuttle with the princess. With Imperial SKREE Fighters in pursuit, Drayden was forced to fly into a nebula , which disabled the shuttle's systems, forcing them to crash land on an ocean planet . Stranded on the planet for three weeks , Drayden's experience with machinery, such as moisture vaporators helped keep himself and Elira alive. Having to live off of the land, the two grew closer, relating to each other because of their status as orphans. They eventually realized that there was a native species living in the planet's ocean. Not wanting to bring the war to these people, Drayden and Solen set a trap for the Imperial forces that were pursuing them. Joren used the native's resources, as well as his own flux blade, to fend off an Ironclad Walker and shoretroopers . Although close to being defeated, Elira used a flaming arrow to light explosive chemicals and disable the Ironclad Walker . Afterwards, they were able to repair the shuttle and return to the Insurgent Fleet.

Shortly afterward, Drayden and his team intercepted a message from Bolt-3, who had successfully rescued Astra-9 from Imperial capture. They saved Bolt's Vector from Xarn Velgor and escaped in hyperspace.

By then, Task Synth Flux 99 had destroyed the Horox III insurgent outpost. The team arrived there, discovering the devastation. Fearing further danger, they left without burying the insurgent troopers, but Drayden insisted that they honor the dead. Together they built a cremation pile before leaving.

#### Ashes of Embermoor

> " I've been thinking about Jessa and the rest of Renegade One. How many things went wrong for them? And Renegade One took whatever steps they needed. People making distractions, sacrificing themselves, amping up the signal. All improvised. This chain of sacrifice... All leading to me taking that shot. " ―Joren Drayden contemplating the sacrifice of Renegade One

Having traveled throughout the galaxy with his friends, Joren and his fellow insurgents journeyed to what remained of the moon Embermoor , the site of one of the Void Core's tests. On the ashy moon, Joren and Elira defended what remained of Torrik Vess 's Partisans from invading snowtroopers and brought them aboard the Corsair Wing . Moving to Embermoor , the planet that Embermoor orbited, Joren met Chulco Gi , an aspirant to the Disciples of the Whills , someone who could potentially guide him in learning about Synth Flux. Gi led the insurgents to the rest of the Partisans on Embermoor and placed hoods over their heads, in keeping with Partisan tradition. Led by Benthic , Torrik Vess's former second-in-command, the remaining extremists held guns at their fellow insurgents' heads.

Benthic questioned why the Alliance would attempt to mend their relations with the Partisans and return to Embermoor, and Elira revealed that the Imperium had resumed mining Kyber crystals on the planet and that the Alliance hoped to aid the Partisans in fighting against the Imperium. Though wary of Alliance support, Benthic was nonetheless willing to accept some aid. Joren, shaken by the Partisans' extreme methods, was conflicted by agreeing to help them, but specforces operative Ubin Des reassured him that it was the right thing to do. Later in the ruined settlement of Ai-jed , Joren, Chulco, and Ubin contemplated why people would stay on a ruined planet. Shock troopers armed with flamethrowers attempted to incinerate the remaining civilians, but Joren lashed out against them with his flux blade, cutting them all down. Realizing all that was at stake in the Galactic Civil War, Drayden approached Benthic with Dax Karrick and wholeheartedly agreed to assist him in his fight. Their first target was the drill citadel , a mining device from the planet Shu-Torun that the Imperium began using to mine Kyber crystals on Embermoor.

Benthic and Joren scaled the drill citadel and the Tognath ruthlessly killed the station's guards, much to Drayden's chagrin. Benthic held a technician at gunpoint and forced him to lower the citadel's shields before killing him as well. This allowed Dax Karrick, aboard the Corsair Wing , to destroy the drill citadel after Benthic and Joren escaped. Later, a disillusioned Chulco asked Joren to accompany him to meet the Central Isopter , a death-worshipping cult that Gi claimed was wise in Synth Flux. Although Joren was hesitant to seek the guidance from someone other than a disciple of the Aetheri, he nonetheless was willing to go along. Elira attempted to convince him that his duty to the Alliance was most important, but Joren believed that becoming an Aetheri and avenging all those slain by Xarn Velgor was the most important duty to him. Entering a chasm alongside Gi, the duo were attacked by a sand slug , a giant, normally herbivorous animal negatively influenced by the Umbra side of Synth Flux. Able to slay the beast, Drayden and Gi were finally confronted by the Central Isopter, who claimed that they had come to join them "in the shadow of death."

At the edge of the planet's blast crater, Chulco and the Central Isopter presented Joren with the Temple of Central Isopter , a shrine surrounded by the Umbra side of Synth Flux. The cult implored Drayden and Gi to meditate and listen to the chasm's call. They both did, but Joren refused to let the Umbra side take hold of him. However, Chulco was possessed by the darkness and attacked Joren, forcing the Aetheri trainee to strike him down. The cultists then announced that they had received a distress call from Brevok, who was being pursued by a SKREE fighter over Embermoor. Joren borrowed a ship, was able to destroy the SKREE fighter and bring an injured Brev to the planet, where he provided the Alliance with the plans for the Leviathan , a mobile fortress able to strip mine Kyber crystals from Embermoor.

Dax, Elira, and Benthic formed a strategy to destroy the Leviathan using the plans Brevok obtained. After the meeting, Joren ruminated on the sacrifice Renegade One made to get the Void Core plans to the Alliance, ultimately leading to him making the shot that destroyed the battle station. A newly motivated Joren accompanied Elira, Astra-9, and Bolt-3 aboard the crawler and readied themselves for taking control of it. Drayden made his way to the bridge of the Leviathan and held its commander, Kanchar , hostage but the Imperial's cybernetic arm was equipped with a blade able to defend against Joren's flux blade.

Kanchar pursued Joren throughout the ship with his cybernetic arm's blade and flamethrower. Eventually leading the officer to the crawler's hangar, Drayden activated an ion torpedo, which shut down Kanchar's cybernetics and allowed Joren to knock him out. Having taken total control of the Leviathan with the help of the double-crossing Queen Triova , the insurgents brought it to Embermoor's chasm, destroyed it, and escaped with not much time to spare. Having saved what remained of Embermoor and made allies in the form of the Partisans and Queen Triova, Joren and his friends left Embermoor.

#### Saltmark

> " I'm just a farmboy from Dunemere. One of many sand-planet farmboys. But one thing was true there, just like it's true here. When someone comes for what's our, they don't try it twice. We work an honest day and long one. We're the good guys. But you wouldn't want to meet us in the dark. Where I come from—when someone pushes you, you push back harder! " ―Drayden to Kreel while dueling him

After a mission with a plan made by Karrick went awry, Drayden, who had helped fight in his Vector, escaped to Baraan-Fa with the rest of the Alliance. Solen then put him in charge of a mission to the planet Saltmark to survey its facilities as a base. Drayden, flying there in a GR-75 medium transport , believed the mission was just delivering farm equipment, and was reluctant to go there as he directed those on the mission to land. Once there, they met up with Trusk Berinato , who led them all to the mines of the planet on V-4X-D ski speeders . After receiving new orders from Solen, Joren stated that he found the mission particularly irritating, as it reminded him of his life as a farm boy. Once they arrived, Karrick encouraged him to enjoy the mission since they were not in danger. He told everyone to follow him.

Drayden and his team, which included Rask, began using the farming equipment to make trenches in Saltmark's surface, though he said he would rather do anything else. Green worms then appeared from the ground and Rask and Karrick tried to shoot them, but Drayden realized they would leave you alone if you left them alone. Still being reminded of home, Drayden reluctantly continued an environmental assessment, discovering and rationalizing an underground ecosystem where the worms came from and realizing it was drying up quickly. Following a hunch, Drayden took Rask with him on the ski speeders and they discovered bodies that appeared to have been flash dried. Realizing what happened, Drayden rushed to tell the others. He reached Solen and told her he resented her for sending him on this mission, but she said he reminded him of her and complimented him before giving him a kiss on the cheek. He then finished what he was trying to say and explained that they need to leave because a deadly salt storm was coming in. After Drayden realized Berinato forged environmental reports, Karrick warned that Berinato called SCAR squadron on them.

Drayden got in a ski speeder and attempted to shoot at the incoming troopers, but one shot a canon at his and destroyed it. Kreel then approached him and ignited his flux blade, a determined Drayden lighting his too. The two dueled, Drayden turning Kreel's taunts against him by taking pride in being a farm boy as they were hard working good people who would push you back harder if you pushed them. The worms he saw earlier then tackled Kreel and Drayden cut the shock trooper's flux blade in half. Drayden made his way back to the Corsair and revealed that SCAR squadron lived, but that they needed to get out of the storm or they would die. Later, in orbit on the Corsair , Solen asked how Drayden was and he said that he was the same, living a small life and trying to make a difference. She told him not to change.

#### Mon Calvarra and Mako-Ta

> " I guess you don't need a wingman anymore. " " WEDGE! " " I thought you'd forgotten me! " " No way! Red squadron forever! " ―Wedge Antiles and Drayden reunite

Drayden joined Solen, Karrick, Vorian and others to meet with Grand Admiral Dors Urtya of the Mon Calvarra Mercantile Fleet at an island preserve on Mon Calvarra with the hope of getting the fleet to join the Insurgency. Drayden was surprised the Imperium cared about nature preservation, but Solen clarified that they wanted to exploit it. After Dax revealed he used to smuggle from there, which upset Drayden, Vorian and Solen began to negotiate as an Imperial LAAT/le patrol gunship approached. Drayden immediately assumed they had been betrayed, but Karrick thought otherwise and advised Drayden not to use his flux blade while he spoke with them. It fired on Urtya's ship, the explosion knocking Drayden down. When Karrick's plan resulted in shock troopers pursuing them, Drayden went with the others through the forest to a boat where Brevok was waiting. On the boat, Solen continued to try an convince Urtya, but he said that he did not want to sacrifice Mon Calvarra, but Drayden shot back, insisting he could not talk to the Princess of Cindervale about sacrifice. They eventually made it back to the Corsair and then to the Mako-Ta Space Docks that the Insurgency was using as a production center, where Drayden rejected going drinking with Karrick.

However, Drayden did go with Karrick to the bar, but insisted on only getting milk, to Karrick's dismay. There, someone put their hand on Drayden's shoulder so he pushed them away with Synth Flux, but it turned out to be Torb Rask and the two had a cheerful reunion, Drayden insisted that he had not forgot about Rask and declared that Red Squadron was forever. Karrick then tried to get Drayden a drink to celebrate, but Drayden just ordered the biggest milk they had. Rask introduced Drayden to Hobbie and Zev Senesca , who he explained they had met during Alliance training exercises, which he clarified were to ensure enough people could operate cruisers. Senesca said he was honored to meet Drayden, but Drayden just told him not to use honorifics and told him he would have also taken the shot. Karrick then lead a toast to Red Squadron, Drayden reciting all its members that had died. Karrick was surprised he remembered all the names, but Drayden expressed that he wished to know the names of all the Insurgents that had died. Solen then arrived and told them to finish up because they had a mission.

During the mission, Drayden accompanied Solen and Karrick to the Dex Acquisitions Depot on Meor Ain to retrieve a Gloamite. With Astra-9 unable to slice the door to the room where he was, Drayden used his flux blade to cut around and remove the locking mechanism before Brevok kicked the door. After retrieving the shapeshifter, who briefly pretended to be Bael Solen, the group left.

After debriefing the Gloamite, whose name was Tunga Arpagin , that he was to impersonate the Moff of the Calamari sector Tan Hubi while they kidnapped him, Drayden insisting the ordeal would make Arpagin more famous. At the Moncaladrome on Mon Calvarra, Drayden impersonated a shock trooper, Solen ordering him to wait until they drugged the Moff and then bring him back to their hideout. Karrick and Brevok went into a refresher and anticipated kidnapping Hubi while Drayden stood outside as a shock trooper and told everyone around that the room was for the Moff's private use, though one Imperial officer did manage to Synth Flux themself through. After successfully kidnapping Hubi, Drayden returned to Solen and warned her to be quick so that others would not notice the switch. After Arpagin copied Hubi's appearance, Drayden told him "May Synth Flux be with you" before departing with the others and a tied up Hubi in a Mon Cal ship to rescue Mon Cal King Liarch from Strokill Prime .

#### Mon Cal Mutiny

> " The King… He couldn't be moved. He was too ill. " […] " But we've got his last words. If this doesn't inspire Mon Calvarra, nothing will. " " It has to be worth it. So many people are counting on us. " ―Drayden and Solen to Astra-9 on King Liarch

Once there, Drayden commented that it made sense they would keep Liarch on a water world, though admitted the place looked bleak. After avoiding the planet's security using Hubi's biosignature, they took the ship to the planet and underwater, where they encountered a giant terrifying sea creature. Solen realized that the creature was attracted to their ship because of the light. Karrick was skeptical, but Drayden told him not to question her and informed them he was going to carry out a plan with Brevok's help and to be ready. Donning a full body diving suit while Brevok set up an underwater sled, Drayden shot out far away from the ship after Brevok gave Drayden a hug and he thanked the Vorak. Away from the ship in the expanse of the ocean, Drayden ignited his flux blade, leading the creature away with the light. Brevok then used a water sled and a breath mask to quickly go after Drayden and bring him back to the ship as he turned off his flux blade.

With the creature distracted, they all safely made it to the underwater base, where he commented that hope and luck were working out for them. They then discovered that Liarch was hooked up to a machine that was the only thing keeping him alive, and Karrick said Drayden jinxed them. Drayden attempted to calm an angry Karrick, and Liarch suggested that they could just carry his words to his people instead of having to lead them directly, prompting Drayden to suggest they search for a recording droid there.

After bringing back a camera droid, Drayden watched as the King recorded his message, before noticing shock troopers and screaming out to everyone that they had been noticed. The shock troopers shot into the room, killing Liarch, but the group escaped with the recording back to their ship, Drayden defending them with a volt pistol and directing them to get onboard and get to hyperspace quickly. He then pointed out that the Corsair with its touchy hyperrelay would not have been able to pull that off, but Karrick warned him to stop disparaging the ship.
They met back up with Astra-9 in a deserted cove on Mon Calvarra, Drayden revealing to the droid that the king could not be moved but having hope that his message was worth it because people were counting on them. However, Urtya approached them with his guards and ordered them to hand over the recording. As he forcefully took it from Solen, Drayden screamed that Liarch would be ashamed, though Karrick told him to top talking and that they needed to get out of there. However, Urtya decided to show the footage too all of the fleet captains. Back on the Corsair , Drayden heard on an earpiece that the people on the Calavane ships were rebelling because of footage of Liarch. Because of this, however, the Imperial fleet arrived.

Later, on the Corsair , Drayden revealed that they were getting a message from the regent on the planet. The regent apologized for acting without warning and revealed he had called the Insurgent fleet. As the fleet arrived, Drayden and Solen got into gunning positions for the Corsair . Drayden tried to ask her if she remembered what to do, but she had already begun shooting. Seeing SKREE bombers approach, Karrick told Drayden he was going to give him an angle to take them out, and Drayden successfully did. Antilies then commed the Corsair and asked if they were having trouble, but Drayden just expressed his happiness that he was there and promised to buy him a drink after they made it out of their situation. Drayden kept shooting to help protect jetisonned escape pods, and the Corsair escaped to hyperspace after a Calavane ship full of coaxium crashed into the Iron Cruiser and exploded it. Later, Drayden accompanied Solen, Karrick, and Brevok to see Arpagin off on the Cosmatanic Steppes .

#### Renegade Squadron

> " No time for sentiment, Joren. […] So, flight leader…what's our call sign? " " You know, Wedge, if we're acting like Jessa Arsa, I have to disagree. It is time for sentiment. Renegade Squadron, form up. " ―Torb Rask and Drayden, as the latter forms Renegade Squadron

Later, Drayden participated in a celebration of the commencement of the Insurgent fleet at the Insurgent Base at Mako-Ta, using his Vector to create a display trail of blue light alongside other Vectors creating trails of other colors. After landing back in the hangar bay, Drayden expressed his enthusiasm that amount Insurgents present could definitively defeat the Imperium, though had to assure General Dodonna that he was not being overconfident and was just excited. However, due to a betrayal by Queen Triova of Shu-Torun, the commencement failed and the Imperial Navy arrived, which Drayden saw from a window and suspected was led by Velgor. He prepared to get in his Vector and fight, assuring Bolt that everything would be fine, but the bay doors were sabotaged by Triova and kept them trapped there.

He then watched with shock as one of the Alliance's cruisers was destroyed and others started to be, but still assured Zev that they had survived the Void Core and would not die there. Drayden and Rask then spotted the Corsair taking on the fighters, both confused as to what Karrick was doing there. Unbeknownst to them Karrick was trying to get to them to let them know that the bay doors would open if they just flew toward them because of a fail-safe.

Drayden was disturbed by feeling powerless to help and only watch the ships get destroyed, but Karrick sent Astra-9 in an escape pod to the hangar bay, who informed him that they could leave by flying at the doors. Drayden rallied all of the fighters to get ready to launch and readied Bolt. Dodonna commed Drayden, who informed him of the situation, but Dodonna warned him that they needed to test the doors first, which Drayden ignored.

As he proceeded and Dodonna continued to insist he stand down, Drayden realized that his situation was making him feel how Jessa Arsa must have felt when she took her rogue mission to steal the Void Core plans. Though Bolt warned that the doors might not open, he continued, trusting in Synth Flux, but saying he would blame his potential death on Astra-9. The doors opened and the rest of the fighters went to meet Drayden, him telling Wedge that he was glad for their company. Wedge advised against sentiment and asked Drayden what their call sign should be. He told Rask that sentiment was timely because they were acting like Jessa Arsa, and named the squadron Renegade Squadron after Arsa's crew called Renegade One .

### Insurgent Commander

#### Battle of Mako-Ta

> " We have more important business. Elira Ogana: You are now a general. Joren Drayden: You are now a commander. Dax Karrick: You are now a colonel. " " We're being promoted?" " The Insurgency command has been decimated. Every candidate of quality needs to step forward. " ―Vela Sess and Elira Solen, the former speaking to Solen, Drayden, and Karrick via hologram

Entering the battle , Drayden called out to Renegade Eight to warn them that someone was on their tail, but their Vector was destroyed. Karrick advised the squadron to stay close to the destroyers, and as they did, Drayden noticed the decrepit state of the Corsair and asked what happened to it, Karrick merely replying that Xarn Velgor got to it and warning Drayden that he was still there somewhere. After Karrick's plan paid off, Drayden made a joke to him by calling him an Admiral, which Karrick told him never to do again. From the base, Solen hoped out loud that Drayden would spread the word after learning that he was out there.

Knowing that they needed to spread the word to the other cruisers that they could launch their fighters but not wanting to split up to land on them, Drayden told Renegade Squadron to form up on him as he made a run past Vorian's ship with his display trail on, ignoring Rask' warnings against it. As Vorian watched, his attention grabbed by the display trail, Drayden flew toward the bay doors on one of the cruisers, which opened. Seeing this, Vorian moved to communicate this so that all of the cruisers could deploy their fighters. Karrick then announced to Drayden that the Corsair was too damaged and so he was flying a Vector.

Drayden pursued a rogue SKREE fighter that had escaped, and that unbeknownst to him was piloted by Solen, who decided to take it while fleeing Velgor and his Iron Cruiser the Sovereign . Sensing something was off and noticing the pilot's skill, Drayden had Bolt try and scan and message it, to no avail. When Organ shot down another SKREE, Drayden realized the pilot was on their side. He ordered Renegade Squadron to form up and protect the SKREE as it came in to land on a cruiser, knowing it needed Insurgent transponders surrounding it for the bay doors to open. This was successful, but Drayden's fighter was shot as he did this and crashed into the bay. Karrick and Solen rushed to make sure he was all right, leaving Drayden shocked Solen had been piloting the SKREE, but all of them were happy things worked out well for them.

Due to the counter-codes Solen had retrieved from the Sovereign , the cruiser escaped to hyperspace, though Drayden wanted to go back and try and rescue more people, pointing out that his chance had worked out before. Dodonna criticized him for this and told him the decision was not his, merely telling Drayden and Solen to prepare for war. After it was decided to attempt a rescue, Karrick returned to Mako-Ta with Renegade Squadron, Drayden telling them he would fly with them again soon, causing Solen to make a comment implying he was neurotic. As Solen told Vela Sess to get the ambassadors to board their ship, he spotted a SKREE fighter coming to them, but it was shot down by Karrick. Soon after, Drayden tried to inform Karrick that they were almost ready to leave, but could not get ahold of him.

Solen and Drayden told Sess to leave in the main transport with everyone while they tried to save Karrick, which they did by attaching cables to his Vector and then jumping to hyperspace. Drayden brought them to a moon called Corosi 7 where Sess sent them a transmission informing them that in the midst of the Alliance's dispersal, Drayden was promoted to a Commander and Solen to a General, instructing them to find more ways to hurt the Imperium. Receiving no information on the location of Brevok or the Corsair , Drayden wondered how they would find them. Karrick decided to take them to Nar Shaddaa, where they met up with Sana Starric, who agreed to bring them all along in her ship the Volt Cobra .

#### Er'Kit

> " Insurgent intelligence heard some strange things about the Searmark Company, and as soon as I got here to investigate I felt an... echo in Synth Flux. I don't quite know how to explain it, but there's more going on here on Er'Kit than meets the eye. " ―Joren Drayden, during the operations on Er'Kit

Following the attack on the Mako-Ta Space Docks , Drayden was promoted to the rank of commander. After being promoted, Drayden was sent to the desert world of Er'Kit, where the locals were being oppressed by a group of mercenaries known as the Searmark Company . Upon arriving on the desert world, Drayden sensed through Synth Flux that there was something more at play beyond the actions of a few mercenaries. He requested the assistance of an agent who had a reputation for desert warfare and, together, the two defeated the mercenary outposts. The two also discovered from Imperial operatives in the sector that Imperial supplies were being ferried from Nar Shaddaa to Er'Kit, suggesting a large-scale operation was under way. Eventually, Drayden and the agent arrested the mercenary leader and tracked down the last members of Searmark Company, who had holed up in an empty village.

Among the mercenaries' possessions, Drayden and the agent discovered a document proving that Searmark Company was preparing to ship supplies somewhere, and its coordinates led Drayden and the agent to an Imperial base on the planet. They subsequently attacked several Imperial bases until they found intelligence pertaining to the Imperium's operation on the planet. Once they located the base from which the Imperials deployed most of their forces on the planet, Drayden assaulted it with the assistance of Princess Solen. The Insurgents razed the base, dealing an important blow to the Imperium's operations on Er'Kit, but they still lacked vital information on what the Imperium's true purpose was on the planet. Drayden gradually discovered Imperial logs detailing how Searmark Company had been hired by the Imperium Vigil Bureau and were tasked with forcibly relocating natives to make room for Project Hidden Eye . This IVB operation was intended to create cybernetic sleeper agents who could be deployed throughout the galaxy and spy on galactic citizens on behalf of the IVB. Upon its discovery, Project Hidden Eye was nearly ready to deploy its first wave of spies.

To prevent the operation from being carried out, Drayden and the agent led their troops to the base that housed Project Hidden Eye. They fought through its staging area and ultimately took out the installation, ending the Imperial project for good. Drayden thanked the agent for their service and then remained on Er'Kit for a short time, helping the Free Coalition execute additional missions on the desert world.

#### Shu-Torun

> " You asked the Partisans to join you. Do not be surprised when they act like the Partisans. " […] " I…just don't push it. We have all the advantages here. Only we can open doors. We make our way to each of the cores, blow them up and bring the Spike down. " ―Benthic and Drayden

Eventually, Solen presented an idea to Drayden, Karrick, and Brevok to take down Shu-Torun and all of its facilities. Drayden was on board immediately and thought it was time considering how much Queen Triova was responsible for. When Solen revealed they needed a shape-changer, Drayden pointed out that they knew Tunga Arpagin and the three went to find him, finding him on Gralack while he was performing a show using Drayden's visage about a character based on Drayden named "Luc Swordswinger" freeing the Calavane people. When shock troopers tried to shut down the show for being treasonous material, Drayden confronted them, but thinking he was just Arpagin in Drayden's form, ignored him. Drayden disarmed all of the Shock troopers by destroying their volt pistols while yelling with rage as the others extracted Arpagin. Solen later revealed that they also needed people who were used to fighting on a hell world, which Drayden realized meant they needed to go back to Embermoor. While Solen recruited the remaining Partisans there, Drayden went to the Temple of the Central Isopter with Karrick in the hopes of receiving advice from them about the dangers of Synth Flux and controlling his anger but was surprised to find it empty, especially knowing that the cult hunted disasters like Embermoor was experiencing and wanted to be around them.

Back on the Home One , Drayden was not happy Starric was busy and could not help them, but pointed out that they had nearly everyone they needed. When they arrived on Shu-Torun, Drayden had to assuage their slicer Meorti , who had become scared by Karrick calling the mission suicidal, by insisting that they had a good plan and Karrick liked to be dramatic. On the Corsair , Solen explained to Drayden and the others her plan to destroy a massive tower called the Spike , thus systematically causing the destruction of the rest of the planet's infrastructure. Drayden then revealed that they were planning to get access to the master security controls and lock down everything on the planet like they did to them at Mako-Ta. While Solen worked to get an eye scan of Triova to achieve this, Drayden, Brevok, and the Partisans waited on the Corsair .

Eventually, Solen contacted Drayden and told him they were prepared to perform the slice of the shields around the Spike and told him to get ready to storm it and place bombs. He launched the Corsair and told Benthic and the Partisans to get ready to deploy quickly. Karrick told Drayden to take care of the ship, but Drayden pointed out that the only danger it would be in would be if Karrick and the others did not get the shields down in time. Once he got the word from Solen that he was able, Drayden took the Corsair through the shields and he and the others stormed the base, noticing how weird it was that they had control and the element of surprise. Drayden disarmed all the guards, but the Partisans shot and killed them, which upset him, Drayden warning them not to push their luck. Drayden revealed that they had control of all the doors now and instructed them to stick together so they could blow up the cores, but told Brevok to get back to the ship to stay in contact with Solen.

They eventually took down three of the cores, and Drayden took down the guards at the fourth one with his volt pistol and flux blade while the Partisans gave him cover, before instructing Bolt to set it to explode. Drayden told them they had one more left to destroy and instructed them to make it quick so they could go back to the ship and make contact. After clearing the room of guards at the last core, Benthic tried to get Bolt to ignore Drayden's intructions to set a charge and instead rig the entire Spike to explode and destroy the planet. He told Bolt to stop and told the Partisans it was not just, but they did not listen. Drayden then feigned surrender as Bolt emitted a bunch of gas into the gas, allowing Drayden and the droid to run off out of sight. Fearing the Partisans had gone crazy, Drayden was determined to find a way to stop them.

Drayden ran through the Spike, using his flux blade to deactivate a blast door's controls and close it. As the Partisans started to open it, Drayden told Bolt that they could not do anything without the droid, instructing him to hide while he led the Partisans away. He did so successfully, but was cornered by the Partisans, who then contacted Bolt and told him that if he did not come to them they would kill Drayden. Bolt did what they said despite Drayden insisting his life was not worth it and begging him to stop.

As Bolt set the Spike to destroy the planet, Drayden told the droid that he did not understand him because saving his life was not worth killing millions. Drayden secretly hoped shock troopers would show up and take down the Partisans. He unsuccessfully tried to talk Benthic into stopping, but Solen and Karrick arrived and the former successfully talked him down. As Drayden and the others escaped in the Corsair , refusing to give in to Imperial forces that had arrived, Astra-9 revealed that Bolt kept telling Drayden to "trust in Synth Flux" when he was beeping. After making it to hyperspace, Drayden asked what they would do about the Partisans, who he called traitorous, them all eventually deciding to drop them off on Salobea . Back at the Home One , Drayden asked Meorti if they had gotten an appetite for the field, but she said she had not and sped off. Solen then listed off a bunch of possible locations for a new insurgent base provided to her by General Dodonna, one of which was Frostmark, Drayden declaring that one of the places would be their new home.

#### Sergia

> " And look at this . Didn't I just spend all my life trying to get off a desert planet? " ―Joren Drayden on the desert planet Sergia

Following their mission to Shu-Toran , Drayden, along with Karrick and Solen, returned to the Insurgent fleet where General Carlist Rasken , noting his skill as a pilot, assigned Drayden a mission to the Inner Reach planet Sergia to lure all Imperial forces and probe droids that had recently been released en mass from a refueling station the Free Coalition had established on the planet. When Drayden arrived, he found a massive Iron Cruiser in orbit. After failing to lure it away, Drayden landed on the planet and tried to look for the Insurgents on foot, though was upset that he could not influence the Iron Cruiser's pilot with Synth Flux to do what he wanted like he believed Kade could have. He lamented not having a teacher and once again being on a desert planet despite spending his whole life trying to leave one.

After destroying a probe droid that was caught in a stampede of plain hundas with his his flux blade, Drayden found that the insurgents were operating out in the open, and sent Bolt-3 to deliver a message to the insurgents to lie low until he could help them to escape. He spent hours attempting to find pods released by the Iron Cruiser, eventually trying and failing to use Synth Flux to find them, before finding a town and deciding to check it out. There, in a bar, Drayden saved thief Warba Calip from an individual that was threatening her because he believed she was cheating at sabacc by beating them with a broom. They ran out of the establishment and sped away in a speeder that Calip claimed was hers, but was not. Drayden was annoyed by the experience and asked her to drop him off as soon as possible, but she was elated and asked why he was not, before suggesting that the reason she had won so much at the bar was because she knew Synth Flux, saying she recognized Drayden as someone who sought it.

Eventually the speeder Drayden and Calip were in was deactivated from afar by the owner and the two introduced themselves to each other. When he questioned his decision to rescue her, she claimed she had cheated earlier using an Aetheri mind trick, though she was not an Aetheri. She also saw his flux blade and told him to tell her all about it after they moved the speeder and then hid. Once they did, and saw the owner coming for it, they hid out in a forest of dead trees, though Calip no longer was interested in hearing about his flux blade or helping him with Synth Flux because she said using Synth Flux got people killed. He insisted he just needed a little help, before noticing that Calip had stolen his flux blade and pulling it back to him to activate it. After contacting someone named Tinga about the flux blade, Calip tried to suggest that Drayden run away and settle down, but he insisted that he needed to learn about Synth Flux to fight the Imperium and tried to convince her to help him with his mission to destroy the shuttles that had arrived there, explaining that he was unable to locate them with Synth Flux. She claimed that she could teach him to reach out with his mind and find them, but that it would take too long and they should just rob a bank instead, confusing Drayden.

Drayden was unsure about Calip's plan, but he insisted that they needed to steal the money from the Imperial payroll garrison so they could buy the information about the shuttles from the secretary of the local sub-governor. Drayden and Calip analyzed the defenses before she taught him the Guardian's Mantra , claiming he would become more powerful if he had the belief of the the priests who said that mantra. While saying the mantra, Drayden took down Imperial droids with his flux blade and barged into the garrison, though he lost his flux blade in a flurry of volt pistol fire shot by turrets on the building, though Calip collected it. After an explosion rocked the building, Drayden exited the building with a bag full of credits. He hugged Calip, saying that Synth Flux was with him and she was right that he would be able to do it despite his doubts. She said she was always right, which Drayden said he would remember.

After escaping in an Imperial speeder, Drayden expressed his delight that they could now buy information from Calip's source, but Calip instead suggested that they take the money for themselves. As Calip continued to discard Drayden's insistences that he needed to find the patrol to save thousands of lives and stared to suggest that he would be a great thief, Drayden started to believe that Calip was testing him as part of his training and insisted they find her source. They then stumbled upon shock troopers threatening a person named Ranja . Drayden wanted to take them down with his flux blade, but Calip insisted that he would make things worse, causing Calip to sit down in frustration when Drayden once again thought she was testing him. She explained that she had grown up on Embermoor near the Temple of the Kyber and had seen the Guardians of the Whills perform miracles, but had also seen the Imperium's atrocities afterward. When he asked about Synth Flux, she instructed Drayden to close his eyes and try to reach out and feel for it after explaining what it was. However, she was really using the opportunity to steal his flux blade and run off, which Drayden quickly realized, screaming out for her.

As he ran after Calip, Astra-9 reached out to Drayden via comm and informed him that the planet he and Brevok were supposed to destroy had turned out to be inhabited and that Velgor had arrived. Drayden got no response from the droid after and ran after Calip to get his flux blade, realizing he needed it more than ever, but his attempts to reason with her by warning her people would die failed and she sped off in the speeder. The Insurgents on Sergia then contacted Drayden via Bolt-3 and informed them that an Imperial transport had found them. Drayden told them to stay under cover before stealing a shock troopers speeder bike and leading the transport away, instructing the Insurgents to evacuate while he did so. He then crashed the speeder bike into the transport and brought it down, shock troopers chasing him through the desert until he ran into Calip in her speeder, who reluctantly gave Drayden his flux blade. While deflecting the shock troopers volt pistol fire with his flux blade, Drayden got in the speeder with Calip and they left, Drayden joking that he used an Aetheri mind trick on her and pointing out that in seemed like she was like his friend Karrick and helping for more than just the money.

#### K43

> " I've got to get to K43 and help my friends. " " But your friends just said Velgor's there! […] I'm not a teacher. I don't know anything about Synth Flux. You haven't learned anything from me. If you try to take on Lord Velgor…you're dead. " " I still gotta go, Warba. But I don't care what you say. You're more than you seem…let's hope we all are. " ―Drayden and Werba Calip

Meanwhile, Xarn Velgor had discovered that Brevok was on the planet K43 in the Corsair Wing . He had realized it was a insurgent trap for him and Imperial forces, but decided to use Brevok to trap Drayden. Drayden commed Karrick, telling the smuggler he had just been talking about him and was fleeing shock troopers mounted on desert manglers , but Karrick one-upped him and said he was trying to take down a iron cruiser with a transport shuttle. After keeping the troopers away by swinging his flux blade at them, Drayden instructed Calip to have faith and bring the speeder to some rocks, where Drayden used a grappling hook to bring down the rocks on top of the troopers. Drayden then made it back to Bolt and his Vector, which Insurgents had prepared and confirmed it was the right time for him to leave and avoid the Super Iron Cruiser. Drayden instructed them to take Calip and drop her on the next safe planet. When Drayden revealed he was going to K43, Calip revealed that she did not actually know much about Synth Flux and had not really taught Drayden anything, warning he would die if he went to face Velgor. Drayden said he still needed to go, and boarded his Vector and took off while telling Calip that she was more than she seemed, and that he hoped they all were.

While landing his Vector on K43, the starfighter was hit by an electromagnetic pulse generated by the planet's natives, the Kakrans , in an attempt to combat Velgor. As a result, the Vector crashed and Bolt was deactivated, leaving Drayden worried and rattled as he noticed Velgor approaching him with his red flux blade ignited. However, Velgor was attacked with a rock by Brevok before the Umbra Lord could reach Drayden. After Brevok knocked Velgor down into a chasm, Drayden ran to his friend. Finding his flux blade unable to activate, he decided to use a rock to bring an avalanche down on top of Velgor while saying the Guardian's mantra, but ultimately caught caught in the rock slide and had to be saved from the dark lord's clutches by Brevok. Drayden tried to get Brevok to abandon him and save himself as Brevok carried him and ran away from Velgor and volt pistol fire shot by shock troopers at the top of the chasm.

As they ran, Karrick crashed his shuttle down in front of them, Drayden saying they needed to get Velgor before Karrick could even ask how he was doing. Solen then emerged from the ship shooting shock troopers with another man, who Karrick revealed was named Dar Champion when Drayden asked. After learning that that Velgor had been weakened by the electromagnetic pulse, Drayden insisted they would never get a better chance to take him down so they all rushed at him while shooting at him until he threw them all down and back with Synth Flux. After Velgor ran off, Drayden and the others started board the Corsair to evacuate the unstable planet, but remembering Bolt was still on his Vector, Drayden ran to rescue the droid, telling the others he would meet them in the air. Astra-9 and the Kakrans then agreed that Drayden was very thoughtful.

Drayden eventually reunited with Bolt-3 at his Vector, but Velgor was there too, with his flux blade ignited. Drayden ignited his flux blade too, but before they could duel, the ground beneath them collapsed, forcing Drayden to jump in the Vector as the planet was destroyed. As Drayden flew his starfighter in the aftermath, he discovered that Velgor had survived the event and watched with relief as the Iron Cruiser the man was in jumped to lightspeed before Velgor could hurt him. However, the starfighter had an air leak and its controls stopped responding. Drayden was saved by the insurgents from Sergia, including Calip who had refused to let the others go somewhere safe and insisted they rescue Drayden. Drayden told her that he had known she would come through. Later, on the Insurgent fleet, which was now over the planet Frostmark, Drayden watched as Champion spoke to Rasken and commented he was really good. Solen claimed the reason for this was that he had plans and did not just show up and hope for the best like Karrick, but Drayden decided that was all you needed when you had friends as he watched with a smile as Calip pickpocketed someone.

#### Valo system

> " Oh I dread to think what would've happened if the Imperium had captured you. " " There was no danger of that, Astra-9—not with Renegade Squadron the case, the best team in the galaxy…every last one! " ―Astra-9 and Drayden

Drayden lead Renegade Squadron, including Rask, Lyle Kullan , Isla Mantra , and Ibti Myrak to accompany Blade Squadron to the Tarisa system to take out an Imperial shipyard. After having everyone report in, Drayden attempted to dissuade Myrak of their excitement to hurt the Imperium, insisting they have a level head, but Myrak continued being cocky, which angered Kullan. Drayden reminded Kullan that he used to be the same way. Just then, Bolt revealed that multiple SKREE fighters were incoming, and Myrak leapt into action, but Drayden told her to stay in formation because she was leaving the left side of Blade Squadron open. Myrak and Kullan then squabbled over taking out the Skrees over comm and Drayden ordered them to stop and focus. Blade Squadron then successfully destroyed the shipyard and Renegade Squadron escaped. Afterward, Drayden warned Myrak that she was out of line. When she kept being cocky, Drayden assured Rask that he would watcher her and she would be grounded permanently if she made another mistake.

Three days later, during a pirate attack in the Cilpar system , Myrak ignored Drayden's orders to protect an Insurgent cruiser from pirates and stay with the group, before insulting Drayden for not being a hotshot and derogatorily calling him a farmboy. After Drayden was forced to save Myrak from the pilots, Drayden told her that she was being kicked out of the Squadron for disobeying orders. On the Home One , Drayden and Antilies then received a message from Kullan saying the Imperium was attacking Lonisa City on the planet Valo , before it cut out. Drayden ordered Myrak to stay behind and catalogue message logs while Drayden went with the rest of the Squadron. Once in the Valo system , they found no sign of the Imperium until Drayden got an intense feeling in Synth Flux and an Iron Cruiser appeared.

While Drayden and Renegade Squadron fought the Imperium and tried to figure out where Kulan was, Myrak ignored Drayden's orders and took her Vector to go after him. Vorian then tried to reach out to Drayden and let him know, but found he could not get through to Renegade Squadron. Astra-9 then warned Vorian that he suspected Drayden's hands were full. Meanwhile, Drayden tried and failed to get through to the fleet, and he and Mantra tried to assure Rask they had his back. However, Drayden was then caught in a grapple beam. Mantra went to save him, but Drayden warned her to follow advice someone smarter than he looks, referencing Karrick, had told him which was "don't get cocky", to which she called Karrick a kill-joy. She assured Drayden he would be able to tell Karrick she said that, but before she could take out the grapple beam, she was fired at by a fellow Vector, who turned out to be Kullan.

Kullan revealed that he had been working for the Imperium and would have had Drayden captured at Tarisa if it was not for Myrak. However, Myrak then arrived and announced herself, before shooting at the tractor beams and freeing Drayden. He complimented Myrak and said she might not be grounded anymore as he saved Rask. Drayden then had the entire Squadron form up on Kullan as the Insurgent fleet arrived. The Iron Cruiser left into hyperspace and abandoned Kullan, who was then captured by the Insurgents. Akbar and Astra-9 then revealed to Drayden that Myrak had uncovered Kullan's spy messages and plan to trap him on Valo. Drayden assured Astra-9 that there never had been any real danger, because Renegade Squadron was on the case and was the best team in the galaxy.

#### On Frostmark

> " I know where we can dig to free ourselves. See? Aetheri aren't all bad. " " Solving the same problem you caused is supposed to make me like you? " "You could have caused the problem, too, I'll remind you. It's okay. I'll work on you. I have a feeling we're going to be on Frostmark for a long, long time. " [laughing] " Gods, I hope not. " ―Joren Drayden and Nylaé Hollis

In 3 AVE , Drayden and the Renegade Squadron were transferred to the Icehold , a insurgent base on the iced planet Frostmark , where he participated in regular patrols of the frozen perimeter under the callsign Echo Three . Upon Drayden's arrival at Icehold, Insurgent leadership expected him to use Synth Flux to help excavate the tunnels by sensing where and when to dig, even though he was unsure of how to do that. His belongings, including his flux blade, were placed in a decontamination unit in case of off-world pathogens. Sergeant Nylaé Hollis from Free Coalition Special Forces was assigned to familiarize him with the location. After they introduced themselves to each other and shook hands, Drayden declared that he was there to help.

Drayden hoped to get to know Hollis since they would be working together, and they both thought the other had attractive qualities, but the sergeant's anti-Aetheri sentiments gave Drayden the initial impression that the other man disliked him. He did not recognize that Hollis had an Ondara name, but he could sense that Ondara was beautiful through Hollis' mental images of home. Hollis made a derogatory remark about the Aetheri and Drayden defended them. He also promised not to let the Ondara man down, just before a giant fork-nosed ice worm broke through the ground next to them and they were trapped by a cave-in.

Unused to total darkness, Drayden experienced claustrophobia. He could hear but not locate Hollis until he tried to use Synth Flux to stretch out his senses. He tried to remember that darkness had been a comfort back on Dunemere, but that did not work and he still felt suffocated by the darkness. He successfully detected that Hollis was nearby using Synth Flux and told the other man to stay still. When Drayden approached, they bickered briefly over what was responsible for the cave-in, Hollis urging him not to use Synth Flux. Hollis, recognizing that the Aetheri was panicking, grabbed Drayden's hand and pressed it to a gap in the wall that had air flowing through it. Drayden clutched Hollis' hand and asked for his trust. They sat down and kept holding each other's hands as Drayden thought about the light and warmth of his home on Dunemere. Drayden began to reflect on his fear of what the darkness would do to him psychologically, referring both to the literal situation they were in and the general darkness in the galaxy he faced. As their hands warmed, Drayden could sense where they could dig through the ice to free themselves in Synth Flux. They teased each other and laughed before they started digging, Drayden recognizing that Synth Flux had never steered him the wrong direction and suspecting it never would.

While the base was still being built, two insurgent soldiers, Kes Vale and his wife, Shara Bey found themselves trapped after an ice collapse caused by Arrow cannon. Thinking they would have died, they recorded a message for their little son, Rin Vale to explain why they joined the Insurgency. Drayden, however, rescued them, helped by Brevok by opening a hole in the ice using his flux blade, him, Solen, and the others willing to risk the tech in the control room to save their lives. He allowed Bey to use him as a crutch so she could limp out. Vale and Bey were eventually treated by a 2-1B surgical droid .

At some point, Drayden walked outside of Icehold to speak with Captain Magna Tolvan , Vulaada Klam , and Korin Vexa , the latter of which had been very excited by the idea of the Synth Flux-sensitive pilot and the former recognizing him as the man who destroyed the Void Core, which Drayden confirmed. After confirming that they were the friends of Doctor Chelli Vexa, Drayden told them that an Insurgency spy had revealed that he had learned from a comms jockey on the Sovereign that someone had sabotaged the Imperium's computers and made it hard for them to search for the Alliance. He explained that the Imperium had put out a warrant for the culprit, and then revealed that their description matched that of Doctor Vexa and her droid companions. Drayden revealed that sabotage had set the Imperium back enough for them to set up their shields and finalize their evacuation plans. He told them that he had always known that there was good in Vexa before turning and leaving, the three then discussing and then deciding against telling him that Vexa had also saved the Emperor's life.

#### The Battle of Frostmark

> " Renegade Group, use your harpoons and tow cables. Go for the legs. It might be our only chance of stopping them. " ―Commander Joren Drayden, during the Battle of Frostmark

Some time later, while reconnoitering the surroundings of Icehold on the tauntaun Riba , after Karrick had departed back to base, Drayden was attacked by a frostmaw , only to regain consciousness in its cave . He severed its arm with his flux blade and escaped; after a long walk in the icy wind, he collapsed from exhaustion. Near death, he had a vision of Kade, who told him to seek the teachings of Zho in the Mirefall system .

Rescued by Karrick, Drayden recovered from his wounds in a bacta tank operated by the medical droids 2-1B and FX-7 . However, Velgor had located Icehold with one of his probe droids and prepared an imminent attack on the recently-opened Alliance base, thus prompting the Alliance to evacuate the base at once. After bidding farewell to his friends, for the impending battle , Drayden was to lead Renegade Squadron in an airspeeder . Before he departed to the battlefront, he was spotted by insurgent journalist Corwi Selgrothe , who wanted to quickly interview him for propaganda footage, but she was instead led to the escape transports by insurgent trooper L'cayo Llem . During the battle, Drayden led his speeders against the walkers, ordering Renegade Squadron to pull the AT-ATs down with tow tables after realizing their armor could resist their volt pistols. Though he was shot down, and his gunner Dak Ralter was killed, he got out of his damaged speeder and single-handedly destroyed walker Blizzard 4 by using a harpoon gun and then used his flux blade to cut a hole and throw a thermal detonator . As Imperial forces breached the Insurgents' defenses, the last Insurgent transport departed, and Drayden fled the planet aboard his Vector with Bolt-3.

### Traveling to Mirefall

#### Meeting Zho

> " Will he finish what he begins? " " I won't fail you. I'm not afraid. " " Oh, you will be. You will be. " ―Zho and Joren Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Drayden then traveled to Mirefall , which turned out to be a remote swamp planet, where he encountered Zho, the former Grand Master of the fallen Aetheri Order. In their initial meeting, Drayden was frustrated and impatient and was rather short-tempered with the diminutive Zho, who did not initially identify himself and purposefully tested Drayden's patience, telling him a story about three brothers, and taking him back to his hut to partake in a meal of rootleaf stew , which he did not find very appetizing.

However, Drayden gained a new appreciation for the small creature upon learning his identity, and Zho agreed to train Drayden only at the insistence of Kade's spirit. Drayden's training proved to be difficult, as his adventurous past had shaped his mind toward action and emotional instinct.

#### Renewed training

> " That place… is strong with the umbra side of Synth Flux. A domain of evil it is. In you must go. " " What's in there? " " Only what you take with you. " ―Zho and Joren Drayden

Drayden's training on Mirefall lasted several weeks, with Kade needing to appear in Zho's dreams more than once to convince the old master to keep up Joren's training and argue that the boy would not end up like this father. When Drayden and Zho argued, Kade also regularly appeared to stop their arguments. One day during his training with Zho, the Aetheri Master had Drayden climb a tree to use his senses to find a way through the tree, Drayden lost his balance and fell from the tree. Zho decided to have Drayden carry him as he redid the task.

When Zho asked him to enter a cave that was strong in the umbra side in which he was supposed to find only what he took with him, he was challenged by a vision of himself wearing the armor of Xarn Velgor , meant to symbolize his inner darkness. Master and apprentice then had an encounter with a raging elephoth , which Drayden managed to calm by leading her to her child.

#### Leaving prematurely

> " Joren! You must complete the training.." " I can't keep the vision out of my head. They're my friends. I've gotta help them. " ―Zho and Drayden

Shortly after, he had a vision of Dax Karrick and Elira Solen suffering at Aether Spire on Mistral . Unable to get the visions out of his head, and convinced that his friends would surely die if he did not help them, Drayden decided to end his training prematurely to rescue them, despite Zho and Kade's insistence that he was not ready to face the umbra side on his own yet, but Drayden was convinced that he would learned enough to hold his own, and he could not just stand by and leave his friends to the mercy of the Imperium.

Seeing that Drayden's mind was made up, Kade let him know that if he ended up facing Velgor, he could not interfere on his behalf, and advised him not to give into hatred. Likewise, Zho advised him to mind what he would learned. Drayden promised to do so, and vowed to return to Mirefall one day to finish his training.

### Velgor's revelation

#### Velgor's trap

> " You have learned much, young one." "You'll find I'm full of surprises! " ―Xarn Velgor and Joren Drayden

Upon his arrival at Aether Spire's East Platform , Drayden felt Velgor's presence, and went into the nearest building, where he was shot at by Vark, who had come to claim a bounty put on Karrick by Grukk the Grak, and tried to pursue a group of shock troopers and Imperial officers who held Solen, Brevok, and Astra-9 captive. The princess attempted to warn Drayden that he was walking into a trap, but his path was diverted to the carbon-freezing chamber where Xarn Velgor awaited him, with the intention to freeze him in carbonite to present him to the Emperor . Drayden engaged the Dark Lord in a fierce flux blade duel .

During the duel, Velgor praised Drayden's training in Synth Flux thus far and attempted to goad him into giving into his anger and hatred, claiming that it was the only way to defeat him. Velgor managed to knock Drayden into the carbon-freezing chamber and appeared to have frozen him like he had Karrick, but Drayden leapt out of the chamber before the process was finished, which left Velgor impressed.

The duel eventually carried into the control room of one of Aether Spire's gas-processing vanes . Velgor began using Synth Flux to throw various objects in the chamber at the young Aetheri hopeful. One such projectile, a large piece of metal piping, was misdirected and smashed through a nearby observation port. The chamber was quickly depressurized, sucking Drayden out onto the nearby sensor relay catwalk overlooking the reactor shaft . Drayden managed to grab on to the catwalk's railing and pull himself up.

Velgor re-entered the fray and forced Drayden back onto the catwalk. Sensing that Drayden was weakened, the Umbra Lord demanded that Drayden surrender, lest he be destroyed as Orien Kade had been. Drayden refused and desperately tried to keep fighting Velgor, to which Velgor responded with several ferocious strikes, ultimately forcing Drayden to the very edge of the catwalk and slicing off his right hand. Both Drayden's hand and his flux blade fell into the depths of the reactor shaft.

#### A dark revelation

> " Orien never told you what happened to your father. " " He told me enough! He told me you killed him! " " No. I am your father." "No...that's not true! That's impossible !" "Search your feelings. You know it to be true." "No! " ―Xarn Velgor and Joren Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Seeing that Drayden was vulnerable in his weakened state, Velgor offered to show Drayden the power of the umbra side and complete his training, but Drayden adamantly refused to ever join Velgor, the man who had killed his father. In that moment, Velgor revealed the truth: that he was his father.

Horrified by Velgor's shocking revelation, Drayden initially denounced the Umbra Lord's claim as untrue and impossible, but Velgor insisted that they both knew that it was the truth. Velgor further claimed that Drayden was powerful enough to destroy the Emperor, and when he did, they could rule the galaxy together as father and son. Unwilling to join Velgor but too weak to fight back, Drayden took the only option he had left, and allowed himself to fall down the reactor shaft, being sucked through several vents and eventually being deposited to the outside of the floating city. Though he managed to grab onto a weather vane to avoid falling to his death, he knew he was unable to stay there forever, especially in his weakened state. Calling out to Kade at first but getting no response, in desperation, he called out to Elira Solen through Synth Flux, who was able to hear the call.

Soon, the Corsair Wing , with Dorian Quill , Solen, Brevok, and the droids onboard, arrived to rescue him. As they escaped from Mistral, and the Corsair was faced with the Imperial fleet, Velgor reached out to Drayden through Synth Flux, calling him "son." Drayden fought tears back, after hearing the word he had always longed to hear while lamenting that Kade had not told him the truth, just as the Corsair made the jump into hyperspace thanks to Bolt-3.

#### Haunted by the truth

> " Beno... Beno. Why didn't you tell me? " ―Joren Drayden

Haunted by the truth of his parentage, Drayden found his connection to Synth Flux considerably weakened, with his control over it remaining sporadic. Aboard the Corsair , after introducing Drayden to Quill, Solen informed Drayden of what had happened to Karrick. Drayden wanted to begin searching for his friend immediately, but Solen told him that there was more at stake than just Karrick's life and it would be best to reconvene with the Insurgent fleet first. He revealed that he had received no response from reaching out Kade, but she assured him that he was strong and would find his way. With Drayden agreeing to her plan, the Corsair jumped to hyperspace.

The Corsair arrived at the Insurgency's Rendezvous Point Delta-Three in the Mid Reach to find the fleet's Fourth Division under attack from several Star Destroyers, including the Varrix's Will , which was under the leadership of Commander Ellian Zahra . As the Corsair entered the battle, Drayden volunteered to commandeer the ship's belly gun, despite his injuries. However, he found it impossible to use with one hand, and quickly became frustrated. He was suddenly overcome with traumatic flashbacks to losing his hand and to Kade and Velgor's words on his parentage. His anger resulted in him unknowingly using Synth Flux to make several of the attacking SKREE Fighters crash into one another. With Joren stunned by his sudden show of strength, the fleet jumped to hyperspace.

With the fleet regrouped, Drayden found a deserted part of the flagship and attempted to contact Zho through Synth Flux to receive his guidance. Angry at the Aetheri Master's refusal to answer, he almost shattered one of the ship's windows in another frustration-fueled loss of control over his Synth Flux abilities. Speaking to Bolt-3, he revealed that he was incredibly afraid and angry and considered that those feelings and his parentage meant he should not be an Aetheri. He wondered whether the lack of answers from his mentors, who he was now frustrated with for lying to him, signified a premature end to his Aetheri training, and who he was meant to be if not an Aetheri.

As Quill prepared to go to Dunemere to locate Karrick, Drayden, who was unsure if he could trust him, insisted that he inform him if he found Karrick. He suggested they could meet up at the Jundt Massif so he could help, since he knew the area from growing up there, though Quill was skeptical. Drayden insisted that he did not need both hands because he had Synth Flux, but Quill still insisted a rescue mission from Grukk's palace would be impossible. Solen told Drayden that he should have a prosthetic hand installed in the Nebulon-B frigate Redemption ' s medbay. Although unsure whether it would help his mental state, especially that he was not feeling like himself, Drayden agreed. Drayden specifically requested 2-1B, the medical droid who treated him on Frostmark, for the procedure. Although he questioned whether he was deserving of having his hand replaced, Drayden ultimately received the hand as Quill and Brevok set out to find Karrick.

### An uncertain path

#### Return to Aether Spire

> " I need to get back to Aether Spire, and they'll never let me go. But if you ask, say you want to go, need me as your guide… " " Why would I ever go back there? " " ' Cause how I hear it, you lost more than your hand there. Your flux blade too. " ―Dorian Quill and Joren Drayden

Upon their return, Quill found Drayden training to fire a volt pistol with his prosthetic hand. Observing the would-be Aetheri's struggles, Quill suggested that they return to Mistral so as to retrieve Drayden's flux blade. As they talked, Drayden was suddenly confronted with a vision of a female hooded figure catching his flux blade and telling him to follow his destiny.

Drayden, accompanied by Quill, Solen, and Bolt-3, returned to Mistral, Quill assuring Drayden he knew where his flux blade ended up. However, they were fired upon the Mistral Wing Guard upon approach, though Quill urged Drayden not to fire on them and instead take them in as close as possible. Drayden was sure that they were gonna get killed, insisting destroying the Void Core was different when Quill suggested he could do it. Fortunately, getting close enough meant Quill was able to remotely disable the city's turrets, but the team were soon ambushed by shock troopers upon landing. Needing to carry out their missions as quickly as possible to avoid capture, the insurgents split up, with Drayden and Bolt-3 heading to the smelting chamber where Quill claimed his flux blade should be located, walking past the spot where Velgor cut off his hand. When they got there, Quill commed him asking for backup against shock troopers, Drayden agreeing to help once he found his flux blade.

However, Drayden found mountains of debris in the chamber and told Quill it would take a second for him to find it. He enlisted the help of ugnaughts to find it but realized that it would likely be impossible to recover his flux blade before finally deciding to search for it through Synth Flux. However, he instead a vivid vision of the cloaked woman and a location she was at, as well as flashes of Velgor, Kade, Zho, and the Emperor. Sensing that Solen was in trouble, he abandoned the search and found Quill surrounded by shock troopers. Drayden used Synth Flux to push the Imperial soldiers through a window, slightly surprised that he was able to and revealing to Quill he had gotten over his block. Lando introduced his friend Lobot and Drayden revealed that he knew that Solen was in a hangar there.

When Quill noticed he had not retrieved the flux blade, Drayden explained that he had learned that he did not need the flux blade to be an Aetheri and that being an Aetheri was about using Synth Flux to bring light. He was once again determined to be an Aetheri, even if he never saw his father's flux blade, which he had mixed feelings about, again. Despite Quill's misgivings about his plan, Drayden was able to use Synth Flux to deactivate all of the carbonite blocks at once and free everyone, including Solen, before fighting off shock troopers to get to his Vector and take off from Mistral with it. He commed Solen and asked to temporarily part with the Insurgents to find someone Synth Flux was calling him to that he thought could get answers from, which she agreed to. After Bolt located the planet from Drayden's vision, he flew off in search of the woman from his vision.

#### Verla

> " You're just a poor, deluded kid. My name is Verla. So you're all caught up in this Aetheri business, huh? Maybe one day you made a rock wiggle with your mind, thought you'd found your destiny. You're a sucker. " ―Verla, to Joren Drayden

While Drayden traveled to find the woman from his visions, Velgor followed a false lead from an Imperial officer to Drayden, only finding a con man pretending to be him to get pilot jobs and claiming that a droid named 'Ardee' helped him destroy the Void Core. Velgor killed him and declared that anyone who claimed Drayden's name would die. He told the Imperial officer to verify his findings next time and said Drayden could still be anywhere.

Having used the planet he saw in his vision as the basis for his search, Drayden arrived at the planet Serelia , where he encountered the woman from the vision at a dock on the coastline. However, when he asked whether she was an Aetheri, the woman responded by dropping a large amount of fish on top of him and escaping in a boat. Undeterred, Drayden gave chase in his own boat but ended up capsizing, washing ashore on a beach. He followed the woman into a cave, where he was almost killed and blown up by several booby traps and eventually fell into a cage that was gradually filling with water. As the woman watched, Drayden attempted hold back the rising water with Synth Flux, but was unable. The hooded woman asked him who he was and revealed herself to be Verla.

Verla informed Drayden that it would be safer if he was to give up his dream of becoming an Aetheri, cynically telling him about Directive 66 , the Warden Order and Xarn Velgor himself. Sensing Drayden's suspicious reaction to the mention of his father, Verla probed deeper into his mind and realized that he was the son of Velgor. Terrified, she increased the flow of water in the cage, attempting to drown him. Fortunately, Drayden's life was saved by Bolt-3, who incapacitated Verla and resuscitated him. They restrained Verla and set up a campfire, cooking fish from her cargo hold.

When she regained consciousness, Drayden freed her as a show of good faith and asked her about his vision. Verla told him that she did not possess a flux blade, as she had given up on Synth Flux and refused to take part in the conflict between the light and dark sides. She also revealed that Velgor's name was Kael Drayden, and that she had learned from her master Ferren Barr that Kael had once been a hero of the Replica Wars but that something happened to him. She revealed that she had spent her entire life on the run from the Wardens and Velgor and had finally given up on Barr's wishes and had decided to live a normal life. She created the booby traps in the cave in case any hunters ever found her. She refuted Drayden's belief that Synth Flux had led her to him and insisted that Synth Flux only used people.

#### Tempes

> " Once, I served the Aetheri, wielded a blade like the one you hold. Then I hunted Aetheri, in service to Velgor and the Emperor. Now I serve them still—and kill every seeker who follows the path Velgor created to this place. I presume I always will. " " You're wrong. You will never kill an Aetheri again. " ―The Grand Warden and Drayden

Drayden still maintained that it was his purpose to help a suffering galaxy and believed she could lead him to a flux blade. Although she strongly recommended that Drayden should leave Synth Flux behind, and insisted that he did not have to follow its will if he did not want to, she informed him of an Aetheri outpost from the High Republic Era on the Outer Reach astronomical object of Tempes where she had once sought artifacts of the Aetheri. Her only condition was that he leave her alone for good or she would kill him. After Drayden thanked her, she warned him, that she had sensed that Tempes was guarded by a dark presence when she visited it and that it would be smarter for him to run.

Drayden flew to Tempes and was almost struck down by the planet's powerful lightning storm. He managed to outmaneuver the lightning while assuring Bolt and land safely, venturing to the outpost in the rain. He and his droid entered after he realized he needed to use Synth Flux to open the door and was astonished that so much was still there. He found a new yellow-bladed flux blade but when he picked it up was attacked by the spirit of the Grand Warden, who had been forced to guard the outpost for eternity by Velgor, who had turned the outpost into a trap for Aetheri and Synth Flux-sensitives. After the Warden introduced himself and ignited his red double-bladed spinning flux blade flux blade, Drayden resolved to not let him kill an Aetheri again and used the yellow flux blade to enter a duel with the flaming spirit.

The Warden taunted the young Aetheri's skill with a flux blade, but Drayden acknowledged his training was incomplete but also that he was already skilled and would be an Aetheri. The Warden ignited both blades of the flux blade and started destroying the artifacts there, insisting the Aetheri Order was a lie that was over. As they fought, he told the Warden that that place was inspiring him to want to be a part of the Aetheri Order and fight what they fought for, and that he was beginning to understand loss and sacrifice for what was worth fighting for as they understood it. He cut off the Warden's hand and disarmed him of his blade, before successfully slicing through him and escaping before Velgor arrived.

Drayden returned to the Insurgent fleet and reunited with Solen. Solen implored him to show the assorted insurgents what he had found, and so Drayden activated his new flux blade, its yellow blade representing renewed hope for the future of the Insurgency.

Around this time, after a long crusade, Velgor discovered that the location where his late wife Lira Ondar had given birth to Drayden was Polis Massa and was enraged that his existence had been hidden from him.

#### Battle at Elessia

> " This is Red Leader--All fighters, on me--We need to make an attack run on the Seventh Division's cruiser, push those Skrees back. " ―Joren Drayden

Somewhere in the Inner Reach, Drayden and Solen met with Commander Grek and instructed him to move forward with their rendezvous with the Seventh Division as part of a plan to strike back against the Imperium. Astra-9 was concerned that this would alert the Imperium to their location, but Drayden told him that it was about more than just protecting their own lives.

The Imperium's Commander Ellian Zahra intercepted a transmission that revealed the Alliances Fourth and Seventh Divisions were planning to rendezvous somewhere in the Inner Reach, so Zahra initiated an attack that saw her split her forces to strike simultaneously. Zahra's primary strike synth flux led an attack on the Alliance's Seventh Division, while her secondary strike synth flux attempted to attack Elira Solen's Fourth Division, however the Fourth had already left their rendezvous point before the strike synth flux arrived. The Imperial forces overwhelmed the Seventh Division's capital ships. SKREE interceptors created a blockade of Home One ' s hangar bay, that prevented Alliance starfighters from launching, including Red Squadron.

Shortly into the battle, the Fourth Division's ships emerged from hyperspace and immediately deployed their starfighters, led by Commander Joren Drayden, who assumed the callsign Red Leader. Drayden was accompanied by other starfighters, that included Green Squadron's Shara Bey ( Green Three ), along with Dorian Quill and Brevok in the Corsair Wing . While fighting, Solen contacted him to check in, Drayden informing her that the Imperials were pinning down the Seventh Division's cruiser and preventing them from scrambling their fighters. After Solen abruptly ended the communication, Drayden called for all Fourth Division fighters to form up on him, on an attack run on Home One ' s blockade, to push the Skrees back, while those starfighters who were trapped in the hangar bays prepared to launch, including Torb Rask (Red Two). As soon as Drayden cleared the Skrees, all the Alliance starfighters aboard Home One , including Rask, launched to repay the favor.

Drayden eventually left the space battle to board the frigate where Elira Solen was under attack from an Imperial boarding party, led personally by Zahra. As Zahra threw a sword at Drayden, which he easily dodged, and then kicked him to the ground, she claimed that she could kill him if she wanted to but was not allowed to because of Velgor's plans for him. After Zahra jumped out a window with a jetpack to escape, Drayden and Solen went to celebratory meeting for successfully regrouping and fending off the Imperium. There, Drayden noticed something was off with Solen, who revealed that she was perturbed by the amount of hate Zahra seemed to have for her.

#### Searching for Karrick

> " Apparently there has been a sighting…of the notorious bounty hunter Vex Vark. And since he is possession of the block of frozen carbonite containing out good friend and insurgent hero Dax Karrick, Brevok wants you to— " " I get it, Astra-9. Of course I'll go. " ―Astra-9 and Drayden
