Around this time, Velgor had his armor damaged during a mission . Reflecting on the pain it caused him to repair, Velgor wondered if Drayden's pain, including losing his hand, had made him stronger. Velgor suspected it had not and considered Drayden weak, believing the fledgling Aetheri had not accomplished anything without the help of his friends, who Velgor believed he allowed to survive. No longer wishing to turn Drayden he resolved to kill him, and began his path to do so by searching for Dax Karrick. In killing Drayden Velgor believed he would kill Orien Kade's last hope and prove those who thought Drayden would be his end wrong. While tracking Karrick, Velgor's partner, an Umbra assassin named Orrin , figured out that Velgor planned to lure Drayden to him with Karrick.

While practicing his flux blade and Synth Flux skills by fighting training remotes, during which he recalled Zho's training, Drayden was interrupted by Astra-9, insisting he did not mind the distraction. Astra-9 said that Bolt-3 wanted to speak with him about something related to Tempes, where he found his new flux blade. Before Bolt-3 could say anything, Brevok arrived and revealed, via Astra-9 translating, that Vex Vark had been spotted on Nar Shaddaa and urged Drayden to come with him to rescue Karrick from him. Brevok shot down Drayden's suggestion that they bring Quill, saying he was not to be trusted and that Karrick would not want to see him, but Drayden agreed. Drayden, Astra-9, Bolt-3, and Brevok then took the Corsair to Nar Shaddaa, though Bolt complained to Astra-9 on the way that he had not been able to speak to Drayden. Drayden was annoyed that Solen had to stay behind to coordinate Operation Starlight and urged Brevok to ensure the mission would be quick, recalling how awful he found the planet and what had happened with Grukkar the Grak.

On the planet, Drayden watched as Brevok met with his contact Sagor , Astra-9 explaining that he and Brevok used to be old friends and translating that Sagor believed a new victor in the Garkata Fighting Arena was Vex Vark. Looking at a hologram of the winner, Drayden also thought they looked like Vex but noticed it was labeled "Garrick." Drayden spoke with a Pa'lowick attendant at the Arena to get information on "Garrick" but was annoyed by both the Pa'lowick 's refusal to answer questions without payment and Astra-9 spouting the mottos of Nar Shaddaa. The attendant, suspecting they were friends of "Garrick," used an alarm to call the Kanji gang, who were upset that "Garrick" had defeated their fighter in the arena. Using his flux blade to fight back against the thugs, who attacked despite Drayden's warnings, Drayden and the others fled, though Brevok was reluctant to because they had not found much information. Following Sagor through the streets before stealing and fleeing on a speeder, they encountered many more groups of Kanji that Drayden fended off with Synth Flux and his flux blade until Bolt-3 picked them up in the Corsair . However, some of the Kanji planned to report seeing Drayden to Imperials on Vandor .

Afterward, Bolt finally gave his message to Drayden via Astra-9, revealing that he had found a list of former Aetheri outposts across the galaxy in his databanks that he seemingly acquired on the Void Core. This greatly excited Drayden, who eagerly wanted to learn more, feeling like Synth Flux was providing for him and making Bolt list the locations for him immediately. Sagor then entered with a message, Astra-9 translating, in which he revealed that Solen had received a message from an unknown party claiming to be in possession of Karrick. Though Solen was starting to lose hope,

#### Ab Dalis

> " Approaching the target. Looks like a… vent of some kind, maybe. I see them, Bolt-3. Skrees on our six. Trying to hit a target the size of a strider's eye…feels like old times. " ―Drayden, to Starlight Squadron and Bolt-3

Drayden and the others met up with Solen, who told them that Vark did not have Karrick anymore and that he was going to be sold in a auction in the attendance of most of the galaxy's crime syndicates on Jekara . Though Solen was starting to lose hope, Drayden assured her that they would find him. After Solen revealed Karrick was in the possession of Carmine Dawn , who Brevok remembered was an extremely powerful criminal organization he had dealt with before, Drayden expressed his concerns to Solen about her taking on this mission, claiming that she had not been herself recently and had not been acting from the heart. Solen agreed but insisted that was why she needed to take the mission to get Karrick back. Later Drayden joined Rask and Starlight Squadron in his Vector, having promised he would join them for a patrol, but assured Solen he would join her on Jekara soon.

Eager to get the fleet back together, and knowing recon missions were dicey, Drayden told Rask that he was happy to join the mission to find and recover a fleet division. Hoping to boost pilot L'ulo L'ampar 's morale, he also revealed to the squadron that the Alliance was close to recovering Captain Karrick. After traveling through hyperspace to Ab Dalis , Freyta Smyth and Rask explained that it was chosen as a insurgent rendezvous point because it was abandoned after a cataclysmic impact make it tectonically unstable hundreds of years earlier. Mart Mattin and Drayden then received a broken transmission, which Bolt unscrambled, revealing it was from the Eleventh Division of the Rendezvous Point Vectis-Four at Ab Dalis who were being attacked by Imperial forces. The insurgents on the surface, learning that heroes of Cinderfall Drayden and Rask were above them, became desperate for the squadron to come down and help them.

Approaching the planet, Rask advised them all to target ground targets and Drayden relayed a message from Bolt that SKREE fighters were approaching them. Hearing that Drayden, who had made the trench run on the Void Core was among the fighters, one Insurgent who had conducted a geological survey of the planet sent coordinates to the squadron for a ground target beneath the Iron Cruiser. They were unsure about what the target was, but after scanning and determining it was a tunnel that could have been hiding someone important, Rask sent Drayden to hit it, comparing it to making the Void Core run. Drayden started to make the run while the others kept Skrees off of him, feeling like he was making the Void Core run again, but he started to have a flashback to the battle of Cinderfall that instead ended with Velgor taking down the Corsair and saying he would claim Karrick for himself, disturbing Drayden and causing him to miss the target. However, the rest of Starlight Squadron were on the same vector and were able to hit it, triggering a volcanic explosion that destroyed the Iron Cruiser that attacked the Eleventh Division. Afterward, Drayden apologized for missing the shot, before the others realized they had rescued Vela Sess. Drayden then told Rask that he was going to Jekara to help Solen.

#### Auction of Dax Karrick

Haunted

> " Elira, I don't know if you're receiving this, but I had…a vision, I guess. I think Synth Flux was trying to tell me something. I think…I think Velgor's coming after Dax. " ―Drayden, in a message to Solen

Drayden contacted Oragna before he left for Jekara but only received a message from her saying things got complicated before losing signal. Unsure whether Solen could still hear him, he warned that Velgor was potentially coming after Karrick, having realized that his vision on Ab Dalis might have been a warning and a premonition. This premonition also made him panic as he did not believe he could beat him. In hyperspace on the way to Jekara, Drayden recalled fighting Velgor on Aether Spire and the Umbra Lord's utter brutality, strength, and ineffable power, likening it to a gravel storm . Imagining himself as he was at that moment fighting Velgor on Aether Spire with his yellow flux blade, Drayden began to believe he could be a storm too, but then just thought he was kidding himself and that Zho and Velgor had been right about him. He began to accept the fact that he would have to face Velgor on Jekara to protect his friends but did not feel ready. Desperate for guidance, he tried to reach out to Synth Flux to speak to Kade, but his fear made it difficult to access.

Just as he was about to exit hyperspace, Solen asked Drayden for help in distracting Velgor because the Umbra Lord arrived at the auction, but he was quickly occupied dealing with Velgor's Super Iron Cruiser the Sovereign that was over Jekara. Overwhelmed, Drayden fought off various SKREE fighters before leading them close to and getting their shots to hit the ship of one of the crime syndicates present at the auction, the Son-tuul Pride , which made the syndicate retaliate against the Imperium as Drayden hoped it would. Finally given a window to approach the planet, he told Solen he was approaching as she told him to hurry. However, the Imperials established a secure channel between him and Velgor, who had sensed that he was there in Synth Flux. Velgor threatened to kill the frozen Dax if he did not arrive to face him, while imagining fighting Drayden and thinking that he would not be a father to him or let him hide behind his friends. After hearing the message, Drayden, haunted by the image of his current self being killed by Velgor on Aether Spire, decided he was not ready to face him and turned his Vector around.

The chase

> " He's not trying to kill us. We'll lead him out into space and— All right…M-maybe he is trying to kill us! " ―Drayden, to Bolt-3

As he left, Drayden explained to Solen his belief that Velgor wanted to turn him, and that if he fought Velgor, he would kill them too. He told her he would help from his ship and then taunted Velgor, urging the Umbra Lord to pursue him. Solen revealed that his stunt had worked and Velgor was coming for him, Drayden telling her that he hoped to keep him busy and imploring her to save Karrick. As he assured Solen he would be fine, the starship of a criminal group harpooned his fighter and started to pull him in. He was freed by laser blast's from Velgor's SKREE fighter. As the two engaged in a dogfight, Solen contacted Drayden again, who simply repeated his earlier sentiments and said he needed to focus. He then tried to lure Velgor into open space, believing the Umbra Lord did not want to kill him, but Velgor shot his Vector anyway, Drayden realizing that Velgor did want to kill him as he crash-landed on Jekara.

After urging Bolt-3 to repair the ship quickly, he used a volt pistol to shoot at Velgor's SKREE fighter as it approached. However, Imperial Administrator Sly Moore , who had been tasked with killing Velgor dropped a droid gang she hired called the Droid Crush Pirates of Bestoon onto Velgor's SKREE and near Drayden. Drayden initally ignited his flux blade and acted defensively, but the droids instead fixed his fighter, leaving Drayden confused. Drayden, accepting of the help, flew away with Droid Crush members riding his wing and shooting at Velgor.

Opting to fly away to the planet's Polar Sea to get Velgor to follow him, Drayden briefly thought he had lost Velgor until Velgor's SKREE fighter emerged from the water beneath him. Velgor taunted him, saying his increased strength in Synth Flux would not save him and that he had Karrick, so he would not be saving Drayden either. Noticing how quickly the sea's water was freezing, Drayden trapped Velgor in the ice by making a giant wave of water with his Vector and leading Velgor into it when it quickly froze. Despite having the perfect opportunity to shoot at and kill Velgor, Drayden could not do it after what he had revealed at Aether Spire, but knew he would eventually need to face him. Drayden reached out to Solen on the Corsair who explained that they and the Imperial shuttle had been hit by a blast that had immobilized them, meaning they could not reach Karrick but Karrick could not be taken to the flagship. Drayden said he was on his way and assured them Velgor would not be a threat for a while. With Grak fighters now chasing the shuttle with Karrick on it, Solen tried to get Drayden to help, but Velgor emerged from the ice in his SKREE fighter and dragged Drayden back into a fight, so Drayden told her he could not help.

Velgor soon chased Drayden back into space. With Velgor reveling in making Drayden feel fear due to his friends lives being put in danger, Drayden cried out as the shuttle containing Karrick was fired at by Grak and Imperial fighters. Thankfully, Velgor was soon ordered by the Emperor to assist with a Grak attack that was happening against the Imperium, which forced the Umbra Lord to leave his son behind, not before reminding Drayden that he was no Aetheri. Drayden insisted that when they met again he would be an Aetheri. Velgor resented having to leave but was proud of terrifying Drayden, recognizing that the Emperor wanted Drayden to live and was orchestrating things so that it could be the case. During his confrontation with Velgor, Solen and the rest failed to rescue Dax Karrick, who was captured by Vex Vark once again after the auction.

#### In search of Aetheri secrets

> " I know I'm needed here, Elira. But I can't help but think I should be following that path, trying to grow as an Aetheri. I'll have to face Velgor eventually. […] I need to find a way to finish my training…and soon. " ―Joren Drayden to Elira Solen

After the breakout of the Syndicate War , the Free Coalition had a meeting with many of its leaders that Joren Drayden was present for. As Admiral Vorian discussed the Alliance's next moves in its rebuilding effort, Elira Solen noticed that Drayden was distant. He explained that Bolt-3 had given him a list of possible Aetheri sites across the galaxy from an Imperial archive. He expressed that he knew he was needed by the Alliance, but that he felt like he should be trying to continue growing as an Aetheri. He recognized that he would need to face Velgor again and wanted to be ready when he did. Solen told him that it was okay and that he should trust what Synth Flux was telling him, offering to smooth things over with Vela Sess and telling him that they could fight the battles without him for now.

Drayden consulted Bolt-3 for the list of Aetheri sites and worried that since it was an imperial list all of the places would have been destroyed or looted. But he recognized that he needed to keep going if he was going to face Velgor, though he wished Master Zho would talk to him. Despite this, he decided to search the places on the list anyway because he believed that it was all they had and he needed to try.

He visited the planet Ilum first and discovered the mass imperial blockade and presence there and the Imperium's exploitation of the planet. He felt in Synth Flux that the planet was important, but also felt that now only held pain as the Imperium was killing it more and more every day. He believed there was nothing for him there and he and Bolt-3 used the weather of the planet to leave and slip past the blockade. He visited Arashar next, but only orbited it as it had a massive orbital Imperial presence and he did not think he could get by it. He then visited Lorath , and traveled to the former location of the Lorath Aetheri Sanctum . He could feel that it needed to be opened with Synth Flux, but it did not respond to him and felt like it had been buried or locked. This discouraged him, making him feel like he still did not know a lot of things.

#### A legacy on Al'doleem

> " A Aetheri saved my life once. Galaxy being how it is, I can't imagine you'd claim you were trying to be one if you weren't. I've wanted to repay that debt my whole life. You're probably as close as I'll ever get. " ―Colli to Joren Drayden

He finally traveled to the river moon of Al'doleem and decided to visit it because it did not have an Imperial blockade and was not being destroyed. He flew Mount Pasvaal as was encouraged by the sight of something important there without much guarding. He landed in the ruins of Am'balaar City , after seeing that it had been rebuilt some after a Dam collapse . He told Bolt-3 that they would head into town, but he did not want to draw attention as one of the Imperium's biggest targets. He walked up to two shock troopers and tried to perform an Aetheri mind trick on them that Zho had taught him and he had seen Orien Kade do, asking the troopers to tell him what the Imperium was doing at the top of the mountain, but the mind trick failed and the troopers merely told him to move along.

Drayden and Bolt-3 aimlessly wandered the city until Bolt noticed a hull panel from a Delta-7 Aethersprite-class light interceptor and stopped him. Drayden did not understand until a man named Colli walked out and told Drayden that he was surprised he did not know but bet he had other people fix his starships. Colli explained that the Aetheri flew Delta-7 Aethersprites in the Replica Wars. Colli told Drayden to come inside, as he had noticed the flux blade on his belt , and warned that if he had others might have too. Colli asked him if he was really an Aetheri, to which Drayden explained that he was trying to be. Colli told him that he had known an Aetheri named Kirak Infil'a who had worked in the monastery on top of Mount Pasvaal and had allowed him and his sister to escape in his Aetherspire when the city was attacked and destroyed. He explained that both Infil'a and his parents had died. Colli asked Drayden about his story, but Bolt-3 tried to stop him from saying anything. Colli insisted that he was not going to turn them into the Imperials and that helping Drayden would probably be the closest he could get to repaying his debt to the Aetheri that saved him. Drayden said that he needed to learn as much about the Aetheri as he could from what of their legacy remained and that he believed the mountain that Infil'a had come from was connected to the Order and that he wanted to figure out what the Imperium was looking for there.

Colli took Drayden to the Monastery at the top of the mountain, but explained that there were also tunnels and chambers in the mountain that the Aetheri had used and that the Imperium was bringing artifacts out of it all the time. Drayden considered using his flux blade and Synth Flux to let go and cut right through the Imperial forces in his way but could not bring himself to do it and instead gave his flux blade to Bolt for safekeeping. He went and meditated with Synth Flux on the side of the mountain before returning to the warehouse where Colli had told him the Imperium stored the artifacts they found in the mountain. He successfully performed a mind trick on two shock troopers stationed outside, having them bring him the most important artifact that was stored there. They brought him a reliquary, which Joren recognized from his experience with Grukkar the Grak. He reached out with Synth Flux to unlock it as he had learned how to do and found a recording of Master Zho teaching lessons. Drayden was disappointed, however, that the lesson Zho taught in the reliquary was one that he had already taught him back on Mirefall. Burdened by the series of setbacks he had faced recently, Drayden considered dropping the reliquary off of a cliff, but was stopped by Bolt. Drayden opened the reliquary again, and this time Zho discussed Vergences in Synth Flux, listing the Aetheri Sanctum on Vantor, Embermoor, and the Living Sea of Gazian as examples. Joren had never heard of the Living Sea of Gazian and decided to go to the planet to check it out.

#### The Living Sea of Gazian

> " I'm going to tell you what the other Aetheri you've met haven't. Even Zho. You don't have to be an Aetheri, friend. […] There are many other paths. The important thing is to make choices that feel right, that are true to you and the person you want to be. But if you want it, we're here for you. " ―Elzar Mann to Joren Drayden

Arriving in the Gazian system , Joren was excited by the possibility of finding an Aetheri-related site that the Imperium did not know about. He landed on a platform he found on the planet, but was disappointed to only find a sea of mushrooms. However he could feel the strength of Synth Flux there and decided to look around, but when he walked onto the surface, he fell into a mushroom-filled cavity below. His breath mask was pulled off of him and he went unconscious, starting to have a vision where he was wearing the clothes he wore during the Duel on Aether Spire and was in possession of the Drayden flux blade. He then saw a man in High Republic Era Aetheri robes sitting next to him, who then took the Drayden flux blade with Synth Flux and admired it, comparing it to his own. Drayden held up a volt pistol and told the man to give it back. He acquiesced but commented that he was not scared of the volt pistol. The man introduced himself as Aetheri Master Elzar Mann . He asked Drayden if he was an Aetheri, to which Drayden said that he was trying to be but was still learning about Synth Flux and trying to understand it. Mann told him that that never really stops, as the Aetheri Order goes back a long way and there was a lot to learn.

Just then, their surroundings shifted to Tidehollow , the birthplace of the Aetheri. Drayden asked Mann what exactly was going on. Mann explained that Gazian was a gigantic living organism that takes an imprint of the minds that visit it and reproduces them exactly as they were at the moment they were there. Mann added that he had come to the planet to get answers after having a vision, and that was why his imprint was there. Drayden explained that the Republic had fallen and an Imperium had taken over. He said that he had never met an Aetheri like Mann, and had only ever met Orien Kade and Zho, the latter of whom Mann remembered and commented was very old and very wise. Drayden had also said that he had met an Aetheri named Kael Drayden, but that he had fallen to the Umbra side, something that Mann knew happened occasionally.

Mann then showed Drayden an imprint of what the High Republic looked like, telling him that it was a Golden Age where they were all connected eight centuries after the defeat of Vael Vorek . The mention of Vael Vorek made Drayden confused and comment that it was all too much. Drayden elaborated that when he started his journey it was exciting and he had been a hero, then he was supposed to save the galaxy by learning from Zho, but then it all fell apart and now he felt that if he did it wrong he would become a monster. But Mann said that Synth Flux was so powerful and boundless so it should not be easy to learn how to use it. Mann recalled when he had trained to be an Aetheri and how even with help and support how impossible everything felt. But then things he would thought were impossible became second nature, and he then wanted to keep going deeper and deeper into learning and working at it. But Drayden was alone, and felt that it was too much. Mann said that the Aetheri tend to be what they need to be, whether that be warriors fighting evil, teachers teaching the galaxy, and sometimes they were almost nothing, left with the responsibility of being the only light in the dark of the galaxy. Suddenly they were underwater, floating in an expansive ocean. Mann then told Drayden that he knew the kind of pressure that Synth Flux choosing you could bring, and decided to tell him a piece of advice that he did not think many other Aetheri would tell him: He did not have to be an Aetheri. Mann told him that there were other ways to use Synth Flux safely and protect life and light, and that there are many paths and choices that could be true to the person he wanted to be. But he also told Drayden that if he wanted it, the Aetheri were there for him. Other past Aetheri then appeared in the water and floated around them until they found themselves back in the void-like vision of the Living Sea. He asked Drayden if he wanted to stay there and speak to him and the many minds in the Living Sea, but Drayden told him that he thought he should leave. Mann was disappointed, but reminded Drayden that a part of him would remain there to talk to them as Drayden saw an imprint of himself appear in front of him.

Drayden came to, ignited his flux blade, and put his breath mask back on. He then saw many bodies of Aetheri that had stayed in the Living Sea. He found one Aetheri skeleton holding a sacred Aetheri text , which he brought to the surface and recognized was an invitation.

### Aetheri of the Insurgency

#### Unity Day attack

> " This is amazing." "We've earned it. It's been a long time since we had a solid win. Not a single casualty. You planned one heck of mission, Elira. " ―Solen and Drayden during the celebration after the Unity Day attack

While Drayden was away, Zahra referenced her meeting with him to Bey, as she believed Bey to the most arrogant insurgent she had ever met next to Drayden. After he returned to the Alliance, Drayden took part in an attack on the Imperial Unity Day Parade on Bar'leth , one of the Insurgents' first large scale operations since the Battle of Frostmark. Solen and the rest of the Alliance hoped it would announce to people that they still existed and could still strike back against the Imperium, hoping to do so without harming civilians as well. Drayden flew in his Vector as part of Red Squadron during the attack. After destroying the Imperium's vast artillery on display in the parade, Drayden told the others over comm that Bolt-3 had detected a wave of SKREE fighters launching from a nearby Imperial airbase outside of the city and suggested they leave. Torb Rask, flying as Red Leader, acknowledged Drayden and told everyone to follow him, leading all of the Alliance fighters to a successful escape.

Afterward, Drayden participated in a toast to the Free Coalition lead by Rask before having a drink and sitting down. He spoke with Solen, telling her that they had earned the celebration because the attack had been their first solid win in some time and was done without casualties. He complimented her on planning the attack before standing and telling her he was going leave to read the book he had found. He explained that it was full of incredible things and that he could not stop thinking about it, adding that even holding it made him feel more connected to Synth Flux. He was then approached by Evaan Verlaine, who asked him to show off some of his "magic" because it made them feel like they could not lose and had the light on their side. He was reluctant to do so because it made him feel like a show-off, but he agreed, igniting his yellow flux blade. Verlaine called it magic while many looked on in awe.

#### The second Void Core revealed

> " Joren…what is it? What do you need to tell us? " " The Imperium's building another Void Core. " " If we could do it once…we can do it again. Let's get to work. " ―Vela Sess and Joren Drayden

Meanwhile, Carmine Dawn agents Bevelyn and Jon Melton received a message from Lady Vessa instructing them to cause chaos for the Imperium. Because they were deep undercover at the Imperium's secret Void Core II , they decided that they should expose the facility to the Free Coalition. They managed to escape with their children and get a message to the Insurgents telling them that they had top secret Imperial weapons information they would give in exchange for protection, but they were not taken seriously until it was presented by General Lena Kryll at an intelligence briefing that Drayden was present at among many other intelligence hits. Drayden asked about it and Kryll explained to him what it was, though she wondered if something about it stood out to him. While Drayden was unsure, he said had good feeling about it.

Later, in a separate room, Kryll asked Drayden if he truly believed there was something significant about the defectors and wondered if he was sensing something due to his connection with Synth Flux. Joren said that he has learned everything is connected to Synth Flux, Kryll responding that he sounded like Brenn Aldric , her late Aetheri lover. Drayden asked if that was a good thing, and she said it usually was. Commodore Grek decided that anything that Drayden thought was worth looking into was worth looking into and had someone look up information about the case to walk Drayden through it. While they only had vague information, the aide informed Drayden that a relay station, one of many they had in out-of-the-way spots where the fleet could send communications safely, received a signal from two individuals who claimed to be imperial defectors and asked for protection and a safe place to go in return for information about a classified weapons site, though the Alliance lost connection after they told them to wait for authorization and had disregarded it because many information hits turn out to be imperial traps and are therefore not worth the risk.

They had also taken the shuttle's signature and learned that the defectors had escaped in an Imperial shuttle called the Scopium and discovered right then while checking the databases that the Imperium had put a system-wide alert on the shuttle, suggesting the defectors' legitimacy. Grek asked Drayden if he could give them more specifics about what he was sensing, but Drayden could not, explaining that it was not like tuning into a comms signal but that he could tell that something about them was important and they needed to pay attention and find the shuttle. They discovered that the last place the shuttle's signal had popped up was on Vantor, and so Drayden suggested he go there. Grek and Kryll told him it was a bad idea, the former reminding him that the Emperor lives there and the latter suggesting that they send pathfinders to mount a rescue mission but not him because he was too valuable, there were bounties for him across the galaxy, and because he was the only Aetheri they had.

Drayden understood but reminded them that their spies had confirmed that the Emperor and Velgor were off-world, even confirming it with the aide who said they were on Velgor's flagship in the Mid Reach and that he could do things that others could not, so what was risky for others was not risky for him, demonstrating his point by performing a mind trick on Grek and getting him to repeat what he said. Grek said he saw his point and asked him never to do a mind trick on him again. Kryll emphasized that Vantor was the most dangerous place for an Insurgent to be in the galaxy and asked if he had a plan. Drayden said he had the start of a plan which was to hide the fact that he was a insurgent by dressing as an Imperial.

Drayden eventually arrived in the Works District of Vantor, two Insurgent agents seeing him off. They told him that he was lucky they had an Imperial Officer uniform that fit him very well, one of the Insurgents telling him he looked good in black, which surprised him and prompted to look at the outfit. They both told him that they thought what he was doing was crazy and not a good idea, but Drayden assured them that they did not need to worry because he would be fine. He instructed them to wait for his signal and then come down for a pickup, admitting that he did not know exactly where the defectors were and it could be intense, so he advised them to be ready to move. He then gave them his flux blade to hold on to, which surprised them, clarifying that it was better to leave it with them because he would be tempted to use it if he had it and and if people saw it they would report the Aetheri bounty and he would be swarmed with Imperials. When they asked him, Drayden revealed that he was going to track down the defectors by speaking to shock troopers.

Eventually Drayden tracked the Meltons to a sewer, but they immediately started shooting at him until he told them he was there to help and was with was with the Alliance, forcing him to use Synth Flux to jump around the sewer to dodge the blasts. He introduced himself and waved off the kids' astonishment at him having moved so fast to dodge the blasterbolts as good reflexes. He explained that he found them without incident by asking shock troopers where the search was concentrated. He checked in with the kids and asked if they were okay and they said that their parents were keeping them safe, which did not surprise Drayden. Vantor Police then arrived, and Drayden told them to stand down and that he would take it from there, but they questioned this as they had not been told any officers would be down there and asked him for authorization. Believing Drayden could not take on the troopers on his own, Bevelyn and Jon made a plan for her to take the kids and run while he held them off, but Drayden overheard them and assured them that it would not be necessary. As the police pointed their volt pistols at him and he raised his hand to reach out with Synth Flux, he admitted that there were too many of them for him to fight or mind trick, but told them not to worry because he could sense help all around them. He then used Synth Flux to call down many Corridor ghouls , beastly creatures that lived beneath Vantor, down through the ceiling of the sewer and had them attack the Police, Drayden saying "told you" to the Meltons as they took the opportunity to run away and escape.

Drayden got the Meltons back safely for them to debrief their intelligence people, but they talked to him on the way. He gathered Insurgent leaders Vorian, Kryll, and Sess and told them that he needed to tell them what the Meltons told him. He told them that Solen should hear it too, but they informed him that she could not be there there because of a new policy to not gather too much of the fleet at once, assuring him they would pass it along. Drayden revealed that the defectors were Carmine Dawn agents that were deep undercover as a cargo specialist and aide to a commander of a secret weapons facility. How deeply entrenched Vessa's spies were concerned Vorian and Kryll about the alliance, but Sess redirected the topic to ask him what he needed to tell them. Drayden revealed that the Imperium was building another Void Core. The leaders were shocked, but Sess said that if they had defeated it once, they could do it again.

At some point after this, Drayden sensed the activation and attempted opening of the Fermata Vault , an ancient Umbra device, by members of Carmine Dawn.

#### Search for the Kezarat convoy

Spira

> " The Kezarat was the command ship in a hundred-tanker Byne Guild convoy bringing fuel to the Outer Reach Territories some two hundred years back. It just…vanished. […] " " Probably got hit by raiders, right? That much fuel had to be a tempting target." "Some people say that, Joren. But I think it was something different. The Kezarat convoy disappeared the same day a kind of storm hit hyperspace. " ―Amilyn Holdra and Joren Drayden

Later, Drayden took a break with Solen, Quill, Lobot, Brevok, and Amilyn Holdra at the latter's friend's house on the planet Spira . Sitting by the pool, Drayden read the Aetheri text he had recovered from Gazian. Quill, who was playing Sabacc with Lobot and Brevok tried to convince Drayden to stop reading and join their game, but Drayden refused, explaining that he did not often get the opportunity to study and wanted to use the time to focus since he was learning so much about Synth Flux and the Aetheri from the text. Eventually, Quill pushed Brevok into the pool, sending a wave toward Drayden that he held back and redirected with Synth Flux while still reading with a smile. Meanwhile, Holdra tried to convince Solen to relax especially since they were by handsome men. Realizing she was referring to Drayden and Quill, Solen was unimpressed, but Holdra said that she would look at them if Solen would not.

Later, Drayden, Solen, and Holdra attended an auction on Spira for an ancient Nullkin strikeship , the real reason Holdra had wanted them to come to the planet. They were all dressed in elegant masks to hide their identities. As Holdra explained to Solen that they needed the strikeship to find a set of tankerships full of fuel to solve the Insurgents' fuel problems, Drayden felt something that made him groan and put his head in his hands. Holdra was losing the auction to the curator of the Imperial Museum , so she asked Drayden if he could use Synth Flux to get the man to stop bidding. Drayden said that he could not perform a mind trick because he was feeling off, believing something was happening with the umbra side of Synth Flux that was distracting him, which unbeknownst to Drayden was a disturbance in Synth Flux caused by the opening of the Fermata Vault . Drayden agreed to try the trick anyway, but Quill commed them and told him not to. Solen, holding Drayden's arm to stop him from doing anything, listened as Quill explained that the mind trick would draw too much attention to them, and that the man might know about Synth Flux and figure out what happened since he ran the Imperial Museum. Quill suggested they instead let the man win.

Later, in a hangar where the man who won the strikeship was keeping it, Drayden ignited his flux blade and drew a bunch of shock troopers that were guarding the ship to him, deflecting their volt pistol shots back at them. While they were distracted by Drayden, Quill and Holdra fired on them from above and Brevok stopped some of the troopers from locking off the ship. Once all the shock troopers were taken down, Quill said that he knew his plan would work because all the shock troopers would be so caught up in taking down the Aetheri to get a reward that they would not notice anything else. Drayden reminded Quill that it might not have ended well for him, but Quill claimed he knew he would be able to handle it because of the book he had been studying. Holdra asked Drayden to cut open the side of the strikeship with his flux blade and make a circular hole not too deep. After Drayden did, they retrieved the Path engine from inside the ship.

Later, back at Holdra's friend's house, Holdra explained that she was planning to go after the Kezarat convoy , which Solen had suspected. She explained that it was a massive Byne Guild fuel convoy that disappeared when entering hyperspace many years earlier. Drayden asked if it was because it got hit by raiders because it was such a sought-after target, but Holdra revealed it actually disappeared during a storm in hyperspace caused by the Nullkin. She hoped that the last message sent by the convoy that was thought to be nonsense was actually navigational data designed only to communicate with a Path engine and that both together could take them to the convoy. After Brevok finished installing the Path engine into their ship the Skyfarer , a group of SKREE fighters found them and started going after them. Holdra attempted to pilot the ship out of the atmosphere so they could jump to hyperspace and escape, but the Path engine activated on its own and sent the ship through hyperspace into No-Space , near the Great Hall of the Nullkin .

The lost colony

> " Sometimes people find their way to No-Space, just like you all did. […] One of those was an Aetheri. Alareen Xie. She joined us, helped us fight off the Nullkin. We wouldn't have survived without her. She died in time, as most people do. But after she did, a sort of belief rose up. That someday an Aetheri would return and take us out of this place. Would take us home. " ―Blythe, to Drayden

Drayden and his friends did not recognize No-Space and had no idea where they were, but Drayden expressed that while he had not traveled as much as they had, he knew the galaxy had strange things in it. He added that Synth Flux seemed off and distant, as if there was not much around that was alive. After Holdra tried and failed to take the ship to hyperspace again, Drayden suggested that they needed to disconnect the Path engine to see if it would fix the problem, but Holdra revealed they would need to land somewhere first. After landing on the Great Hall, Lando lamented the dangers of falling off the edge there and said it was worse than Aether Spire, but Drayden insisted that Aether Spire had been really bad. A child in a spacesuit then appeared and asked them if they had 'flavors', which Quill interpreted to mean the kid wanted to eat them. Drayden said he was just a kid, but Quill asked him to remind him to tell Drayden about the White Worms gang's savage children. The kid eventually advised them to hide and ran off, and Drayden sensed with Synth Flux that the kid was terrified and did not know where he was going. The kid then jumped off the edge of the Hall, which shocked Drayden, but he just activated a jetpack and flew away.

Lando suggested they get out of there, which Drayden agreed with, suggesting they go back to their ship and try to find the other place there that the boy mentioned. He then noticed that a bunch of Nullkin killdroids had surrounded their ship, which was what the boy was afraid of. Drayden began to fight them with his flux blade, but quickly realized that they were flux blade resistant. A ship then pulled up next to the hall, and Holdra suggested that they board it to escape the droids, but was concerned by having to jump across open space to it. Drayden insisted that it was not that bad of a jump and because the ramp had a magnetic shield they would not be in the vacuum very long, but Quill implied that easy for him to say because he was an Aetheri. They all jumped over to the ship without issue, except for Quill, who tripped and fell, prompting Drayden to use Synth Flux to pull him back up while muttering "I can do this." Once Quill was safe he thanked Drayden for saving his life and assured him he would not bother him about studying his book ever again. Drayden revealed that he could have gotten him to safety faster but could not because there was still something going on with Synth Flux that he described as ripples messing with his concentration. Once the ship they were on docked with a larger ship, a voice over a speaker ordered them to throw their weapons out into space or they would vent them out into space. Drayden considered using his lightaber to just cut through the door into the bigger ship but realized they would just vent them anyway. He threw his flux blade out into space, which he said hurt because they were hard to find, but the kid with the jetpack just flew over, picked it up, and ignited it before flying away, Drayden warning him that he needed to be careful with it or he would hurt himself.

The voice over the speaker welcomed them to the Colony of Kezarat . Holdra yelled that they just needed to go home, but an Abyssin left the cockpit and told them they were home. After Solen said they would not attack and were listening, the individual introduced himself as Captain Blythe , and Holdra introduced herself, Drayden, and the others to him. Blythe then began a tour of the colony, confirming that it was the old convoy and the fuel was still there. The kid, who Blythe revealed was named Forvan , then ran up excitedly, and Blythe explained that he had gotten excited from seeing a flux blade. Drayden asked if it was because they had Aetheri there and asked for his blade back, but Blythe said they did not have Aetheri there and he would get his flux blade back in due time. After Blythe explained the colony's history of clashing with the Nullkin before the organization disappeared from No-Space, Forvan started to lead them over somewhere else. Blythe told Drayden to let Forvan down easy, and when he said he did not understand, Blythe said he would.

Forvan took them to mural depicting an Aetheri. Drayden was shocked that they had an Aetheri with the convoy, but Blythe said she had not been there from the beginning and just found her way to No-Space. Blythe explained that her name was Alareen Xie , and that after she died a belief spread within the colony that an Aetheri would someday lead them home. Blythe once again reiterated for Drayden to let Forvan down easy, so he bent down to speak with Forvan and told him that while the Aetheri could do incredible things, he could not assure them that he would get them out of No-Space, though he would at least do everything he could to try and get them out of there or to make their lives better. Forvan once again said that Aetheri could do anything, so Drayden just said he would try his best. After Blythe warned them that they would be there for a while and would not be able to get back to their ship, Drayden and the others all went to sleeping quarters given to them. Drayden paced around the room and Solen recognized something was wrong and asked him about it. Drayden explained that he had to get out of there to get back to the ship to get the irreplaceable Aetheri text that he had left on it. When she brought it up, Drayden said he was not worried about his flux blade as he could get another one, but that without the text he would not be able to move forward with his training. He feared that if he lost it and was no longer able to access its knowledge, techniques, and history of Synth Flux and the Aetheri Order or be able to use it to find places he could learn more, he would never truly become an Aetheri.

Escape from No-Space

> " Keep the killdroids off Brev until he's back on the ship. Then get yourselves safe too. I'm going after the Aetheri text. " " But you said your blade can't get through [the killdroids'] armor! And Joren…it's just a book! " " No. It's the book. And as far as my flux blade, I wasn't properly focused before. Now I am. Now…Synth Flux is with me. " ―Drayden and Holdra

After Brevok realized that they could use the Path engine to escape No-Space, Blythe gathered Drayden and the others, offering them an old Longbeam cruiser to retrieve it. Drayden was skeptical that it could fly, but Blythe assured them that it used to and just needed to be fixed up. Once they got the cruiser to take off and leave the colony, Holdra and Lando took inventory of what they had, including that they had an Aetheri. Drayden reminded them that his connection to Synth Flux was different and hard to use in No-Space and something strange felt like it was happening to it anyway, but he assured them he would try his best. Quill assured them they would be fine, but Drayden was still skeptical and pointed out that the killdroids had found and taken the Path engine. Drayden decided that he would distract the droids so the others could get the engine, but after needed to go back to the Skyfarer and get his Aetheri text, refusing to leave without it. He added that he had talked to Forvan before they left and that the boy, wanting to help out an Aetheri, gave him specialized gear, including a spacesuit, that he could use to get down to the Great Hall. Wearing the spacesuit, Drayden jumped from the cruiser down to the Hall, ignited his flux blade, and called out to the droids, encouraging them to go after him. As Drayden, Holdra, and Quill distracted the droids, Drayden deflecting their blastershots with his flux blade, Brevok went after the engine. However, some droids noticed and went after Brevok anyway, so Drayden sent them flying away off into no-Space with Synth Flux. He warned Brevok that he probably would not be able to do it again, so he needed to run and get the engine back to the ship.

After shooting a droid behind Brevok with a volt pistol, Drayden started to run off and told Holdra to keep Brevok safe while he ran after the Aetheri text. She reminded him that his flux blade could not get through their armor and that it was just a book, but Drayden said that it was more than just a book and that he had not been focused when his flux blade would not cut them the first time, but now was. Drayden sliced through the droids with his flux blade while saying "Synth Flux is with me" and calling out for what they did with his book. He eventually found the book on a ledge at the edge of the Hall and decided that he would easily be able to grab it and fly back to the ship, but a killdroid shot his jetpack, sending him flying uncontrollably toward the book and leaving it without fuel. As killdroids approached him, their footsteps shaking the ground caused the book to go over the edge. Drayden screamed in horror and reached out to it with his right hand, which was holding his flux blade, but a droid grabbed his hand and lifted him up by it, crushing both his hand and his flux blade. The droid threw him onto the ground and into the remains of the Skyfarer , and Drayden cried out in horror when he saw his hand and flux blade. Quill and Holdra saw what happened and decided to try and get to Drayden before the droids killed him. A delirious Drayden told the approaching droids to stop, but when they did not, he threw all of the droids out of the Hall in a massive wave using Synth Flux while telling himself "may Synth Flux be with me." Quill, Holdra, and Brevok were shocked at how he was doing it, and Quill told Brevok to remind him never to get on his bad side. Drayden then pulled the Aetheri text to him with Synth Flux and passed out immediately after.

Later, on the way back to the colony, Quill checked in on a now handless Drayden, though the droid had only destroyed his already mechanical hand. Quill asked him how he would done what he did, but he said that he did not understand how it happened and that it felt like Synth Flux suddenly came back to him stronger than ever, and that it was frightening. Quill told him to cheer up, but thinking about his broken flux blade and his inevitable confrontation with Velgor, told Quill that he was not sure he would be able to do something he needed to anymore. Back at the colony, Drayden showed Solen, who had stayed behind, what happened to his hand. Blythe and Forvan offered to fix it up, the latter suggesting a claw or grappling hook, but Drayden said he would just wait until they got home even though those things would be cool. Drayden and Brevok then presented the Path engine to Blythe. After Blythe presented the group to the members of the colony, but Solen and Quill warned them about the dangers of the Imperium and traveling with them, Blythe revealed to them that several of the colony decided to go back with them. However, he revealed that many were going to stay on the Great Hall since they got rid of all the killdroids. Brevok pointed out that Drayden got rid of all the droids by himself, and Blythe commented that it seemed the things people said about Aetheri were true. However, Drayden, who was struggling to pull a piece of scrap to him with Synth Flux, said they sometimes were true and sometimes were not. Later, after a rough hyperspace jump made with many ships secured together and assisted by Lobot, Drayden and his friends as well as several members of the colony arrived back in the greater galaxy.

Back with the fleet, Drayden had his mechanical hand repaired by a medical droid while Bolt-3 did his best to repair his flux blade. Solen reminded Drayden that it could have been worse if the droid had crushed his other hand, and asked him about the powerful act of Synth Flux he had pulled off on the Great Hall. He confirmed that he had not learned it from his book but that it was instead related to something being wrong with Synth Flux, describing it as if something hit Synth Flux while he was in No-Space and caused it to ripple. He added that it felt like waves on the beach in the way that it vacillated from him being the strongest he had ever been where he almost could not control it, to not being able to feel it at all. Solen then wondered if since he could feel it, and even she could slightly, that the Emperor and Velgor were also feeling it immensely and were distracted, making it their chance to defeat them. Drayden said her theory made sense, and she went to speak with Vela Sess. Drayden thanked Bolt for attempting to fix his flux blade, but admitted that he was going to need a new one for when he inevitably faced Velgor again.

#### Duel with a droid

> " A Aetheri…I was afraid my dreams of killing one were dashed. […] I'd been informed the Aetheri were extinct. " " Oh, don't worry…they still are. " ―Ajax Sigma and Drayden

While still escorting more of the Kezarat convoy to the Alliance fleet, Drayden felt a disturbance in Synth Flux while on the Corsair Wing . He explained to Solen that it was not like the one he had felt in No-Space but instead felt like the deaths of innocent people, and that it was nearby enough that he could bring them there. Solen agreed to go despite the fact they were supposed to be escorting the convoy. When they arrived, Astra-9 revealed that the moon did not appear on any star-charts . Solen wondered if it was erased by the Imperium, but Drayden said it was not and pointed out an ancient flux blade like none he would ever seen before. He and Brevok then noticed that there was a large amount of destroyed droids that were cut down with a flux blade. They all realized that a battle had been fought there, and Drayden put together that it had been between droids and people, and since there were no battle droids and no remains, it must have been hundreds of years earlier. Drayden realized that the battle was not what he had sensed and instead lead them to a homesteader ship that they found to be full of bodies. Astra-9 offered to secure the Corsair but Drayden said it was not necessary. Drayden asked Bolt to plug into the ship and see if it had any information about the moon they were on. Bolt revealed it was called Kligson's Moon . Drayden suggested that the carnage there may have recently happened and the perpetrator could still be there, and suggested splitting up into pairs to search due to the size of the ship.

After discovering a droid, that unbeknownst to Drayden was Ajax Sigma , an ancient droid revolutionary, he asked it why they killed all the people there. Sigma claimed that it was self defense and that they attacked him outside when he came there to retrieve something, but Drayden did not believe him. The droid clarified that he had followed the people back to their ship and killed them all, which Drayden said did not sound like self-defense. The droid activated electric blades on his arms and disparaged Drayden for being organic and for suggesting that the droid would go with him and answer for his actions. Drayden ignited his flux blade and began to fight Sigma, who used the blades on his arms to defend himself. He was surprised to see an Aetheri, believing them all to be dead, and Drayden assured him they were. After Drayden sliced open the droid's chest and knocked him down, he ordered him to stand down or face destruction. However, the droid just entered a pod that he ejected from the homesteader ship, successfully escaping.

Velgor had a vision of Drayden and Sigma's duel while speaking with the Eye of Webbish Bog , sometime before the events of No-Space and the activation of the Fermata Vault. In the vision, Velgor also saw Drayden holding two kyber crystals, one green and one red.

#### Adventure with Vexa

Kidnapping plot

> " Come on, Joren. Ancient Aetheri artifact of great power? Sealed away by Aetheri Masters? Maybe even something that could give you an edge of a certain Umbra Lord? Tell me you don't want that. " " How far to Sason? " " Now we're talking! " ―Chelli Vexa and Drayden

Because he had lost control of his powers just as Drayden had, Velgor recruited Doctor Vexa to help him find a way to control them. While in a shuttle to a location she had in mind, Vexa walked into a room where Velgor was meditating and entered a vision he was having that included Drayden in his Dunemere farm boy clothes. Vexa did not understand why Drayden was there and did not recognize the other individual in the vision, Sable , who was telling Drayden that there was still good in Velgor, which were Drayden's mother's last words.

Though his flux blade was still not fixed, Drayden traveled to Numidian Prime with Bolt-3 in search of a temple on the planet. He briefly left Bolt in a temporary camp while he searched for it, but after finding no sign of it and it started to rain he returned to the camp, finding peace in the solitude. He apologized to Bolt for leaving, saying they needed to wait for the rain to taper off, but then noticed the droid was deactivated and laying on the ground. He cried out and ran to the droid, but a node underneath the droid started shooting smoke out, confusing Drayden and knocking him out after he inhaled it. The trap had been set by Doctor Chelli Vexa, who then stood over an unconscious Drayden and greeted him, calling him a "farm boy", and asked how he felt about going on an adventure.

Vexa eventually bound and gagged Drayden and placed him in a crate on her ship the Ark Angel IV . When he woke up, she opened the crate and said she was glad he was still breathing before offering him Caf . She started to get him some as he realized who kidnapped him, telling her he did not want Caf and just wanted to know why she did it and what she had done with Bolt. She explained that she had put a restraining bolt on and turned off Bolt, and Drayden realized that she needed him for something. Vexa then claimed that she was going to actually do him a huge favor. Drayden had managed to slip out of the ropes she had tied him up in, which she noticed at that moment, surprised at how quickly he had done it and wondering if Karrick had taught him as she presented a holorecording. Realizing it was an Aetheri holo, Drayden listened as Aetheri Master Shaak Ti explained to Ciel Venn that a powerful Aetheri artifact called Kythoo's Bell was locked away at the Sason Temple . Vexa told him that she knew how to get there an needed an Aetheri to get in, but Drayden was still suspicious. After insisting she was just being nice and trying to make up for the events at the Screaming Citadel, Vexa pointed out that the artifact could give Drayden an edge over Velgor. After contemplating it more, Drayden asked her how far Sason was, exciting her.

Sason Temple

> " Right before I found you, Velgor found me. That 'Synth Flux pulse' you felt? He felt it too. But unlike you, it just made him stronger. Wild. Out of control. You don't stand a chance against him as you are. Say we leave Sason without Kythoo's Bell. Velgor kills everyone you care about to get to you. Then he kills you. Once you're dead, who's going to protect me from him? " ―Chelli Vexa to Drayden

As they flew into Sason in the Ark Angel IV , Drayden noticed that their navigation systems went offline, which Vexa attributed to the planet's magnetic storm . Telling Vexa she had a great copilot, Drayden navigated the ship through the storm despite Vexa's protests, following an echo of a note he could hear in Synth Flux and urging Vexa to trust him. They then flew toward a gap in the storm that held the temple, Drayden exclaiming that it was beautiful. After being briefly confused by Vexa calling the temple their payday, which she insisted was figurative, Vexa explained that her research claimed that the temple would only open to someone who was pure of heart and strong in Synth Flux. Drayden tried to open it but failed, explaining to Vexa that he was unable to use Synth Flux because something was happening to it making it unstable. Vexa then accused him of making things up to navigate them there, but he insisted he had used Synth Flux. Realizing they did not have any other options, Vexa asked him if he had learned anything tricks at Aetheri school, but Drayden only said Aetheri school did not exist anymore.

A massive guardian made out of crystal then formed, Drayden telling Bolt to get behind him and trying and failing to ignite his broken flux blade. He eventually was able to ignite it and started to fight them, Vexa and him working together to take them down, but quickly realized that they were reforming and he could not defeat them. When his flux blade deactivated, Drayden reached out to Synth Flux, destroying the guardians with a massive Synth Flux push while Vexa destroyed the Magnetic wave emitter that was causing them to reform. Drayden felt like he was going to pass out, but just them the floor collapsed under them and dropped them into a pool. Drayden called out for Bolt, who had not landed in the pool, but Vexa assured Drayden the droid was fine. The two then noticed the figures of Aetheri Masters Shaak Ti and Ciel Venn standing in front of them.

Drayden attempted to introduce himself to the Masters, but Vexa quickly realized that they were advanced holograms. This disappointed Drayden, who had briefly believed he was not alone as an Aetheri. As they traversed the temple, illusions started to make the two believe the other was saying things they were not, leading to Drayden lashing out at Vexa for always treating things like a joke and questioning her on why she was not using her electro-tattoos, and Vexa believing Drayden had referenced her and Sana Starric' breakup, though in reality, Drayden had no idea that they had been in a relationship. As the argument reached its peak as Vexa asked him how he broke his flux blade, a creature slammed onto the other side of the crystal wall, terrifying the two, but after Kythoo's bell rang it retreated. After hurriedly leaving to another section of the temple, Drayden began to realize that the entire place was made up of illusions after almost falling into a pit of spikes. He suggested that things felt wrong and they should leave, but Vexa held a gun to his head to stop him and revealed that she was trying to make Drayden more powerful because Velgor had become more powerful, and she wanted Drayden to protect her from the Umbra Lord. Drayden agreed and convinced her to put her volt pistol down, the two using Drayden's ability to sense the bell with Synth Flux and Vexa's ability to take down the traps, obstacles, and guardians in their way to eventually locate the bell.

Starweird

> " If you die, this whole trip was useless! If you fall, I'm keeping your laser sword. That's my volt pistol! " " If you die, I'm keeping it! " ―Vexa and Drayden, while fighting the Starweird

Realizing the bell was to big to transport, and having no Synth Flux-ritual to use with it, Vexa removed a piece inside of the bell by standing on Drayden's shoulders and grabbing it. However, this caused the entire bell to collapse and be destroyed, which Drayden realized made the echo of the note he could hear stop resonating throughout the Temple. Just then, the creature they had seen earlier burst through the wall, and Vexa realized that Sason Temple was really an Aetheri prison. Vexa recognized the creature as a Starweird , a species that was supposed to be extinct. The Starweird threw them onto the wall telekinetically, but Drayden recognized that it was not using Synth Flux. When Vexa accidentally activated the piece of the bell they had retrieved and thus Forced the creature to be frozen, she realized it was an emitter releasing a frequency that affected the Starweird. However, the emitter deactivated and the Starweird flew toward Vexa, so Drayden used Synth Flux to impale it with a bunch of crystals before he and Vexa ran away. Drayden admitted that he had not been trying to impale it, just hold it still as he led Vexa through the temple. After Drayden, decided to hide in a room full of mirrors, Vexa called him brilliant.

As Drayden used Synth Flux to manipulate the reflections of the crystals to lure the Starweird toward them, Vexa used her electro-tattoos to activate the holograms of Masters Ti and Venn. Drayden, Vexa, and Bolt-3 were able to flee the creature and its scream while it was distracted. However, the creature found them, knocking Drayden to the edge of the temple and leaving him barely hanging on. While proclaiming her desperation to protect Drayden, she fought the Starweird using his flux blade. Drayden eventually pulled himself up and shot at the creature, saying he would keep it if she died right before Vexa sliced the creature's hand off, which Drayden commented did not feel good. Escaping into the Ark Angel IV , but with the Starweird still on their tail, Drayden directed Vexa to lead them into the magnetic storm and used Synth Flux to activate the emitter, knocking the creature into the storm and eviscerating it, and leaving him surprised at his ability.

Later, back on Numidian Prime, Drayden told Vexa she did not need to trick or bribe him into protecting her from Velgor, insisting he would help because it was the the Aetheri way. Vexa warned against his kindness, but Drayden declared he preferred to take the chance than close himself off from people in need and leave himself all alone. Vexa told him to call her the next time he needed to get into an Aetheri tomb, but Drayden said he would not and took off in his Vector, having taken the emitter with him before Vexa could realize.

#### Krestophis

> " Every time I think I have a handle on being an Aetheri, something happens to kick me back. The ground under my feet just disappears… I'm going to have to face Velgor again, and I am not ready. I don't even have a flux blade I can rely on. " ―Drayden to Solen

While studying his Aetheri text and the yellow kyber crystal from his flux blade on the Home One , Drayden realized that he did not know a lot about kyber crystals except them being in every flux blade as their power source. He explained to Bolt-3 that the text only contained details about the philosophy and spirituality of kyber crystals and an old rite of passage for younglings to find a crystal to make their first flux blade on the planet Ilum, referring to another manual by Professor Huyang for building instructions. He figured that Zho and Kade had never taught him anything about them because he had already had one, but lamented that his had never had a connection with him like they were supposed to due to the almost living nature of the crystals. He ignited his flux blade and said that now that the crystal was cracked it no longer was a useful tool and was unstable. Due to the blade's unsteadiness, Bolt made a concerned beep and Drayden shut it off and apologized. Realizing that he needed a new crystal, Drayden decided that he and Bolt should visit other worlds the text mentioned to find them since Ilum was locked down by the Imperium. Just then, Solen walked in. After he told he was going somewhere, she expressed her concern for him especially due to the strangeness of Synth Flux as well as her desire for him to stay and help the Alliance. Drayden insisted he was fine, saying he was just troubled by not knowing why Synth Flux was strange or how to fix his flux blade and felt like he could never truly get a handle on being an Aetheri. He also admitted that he needed a flux blade for his confrontation with Velgor, and that he felt like he needed to go on this mission in order to take control and take action.

Drayden chose to go to Krestophis, due to the fact that there was not much of a permanent Imperial presence there despite it being an Imperial world and because the text said kyber crystals could regularly be found there. He flew his Vector there with Bolt and landed outside of a small mining town where prospectors sell their finds so he would not get noticed, though did not want to have to walk the way there. Bolt indicated to him that he wanted to know the plan, so Drayden admitted that he did not have one. He explained that Quill had told him there was a black market for crystals on Krestophis because prospectors try to sell them since there was not enough for the Imperium to set up a full-scale mining operation. He told Bolt that he just planned to put the word out and pose as a buyer to see who would speak with him. He admitted that it was not much of a plan but gave Bolt his flux blade to hold onto while he looked around, not wanting to make it obvious he was an Aetheri. He assured Bolt he would be fine and told the droid that he could save him if he got into trouble. He then started in to a nearby cantina.

In the cantina, Drayden got food and water and implied to an Iktotchi bartender that he was a wealthy buyer looking for kyber. Just as the Iktotchi was offering to point Drayden to his quarry, a human with green hair shot and killed him and everyone else in the cantina except for Drayden, leaving him shocked. She taunted him and called him by name, so Drayden used Synth Flux to pull his flux blade to him from Bolt, who had just rolled in and ordered her to explain herself. She introduced herself as Gretta and claimed that she saved his life, pointing out a volt pistol behind the counter. She explained that the whole place was ploy to trap people looking for kyber and sell them to the Imperium, and that she saved him from it because he was an Aetheri. She claimed that she still believed and recognized him from the dark channels, having posted out there in case any Aetheri came through. She told him that there was kyber near and told him that she had to kill the people there because she knew he would not. Drayden was still upset that she killed everyone there, insisting they died for nothing and urging her to live a better life before leaving. Drayden went off to look for crystals on the planet, sensing she had told the truth about there being some there, but she called after him and told him he was going the wrong direction. He saw she had a speeder with her and urged him to let her help him, explaining that she was from Embermoor and that her people were the Fallanassi . She revealed that they were killed by the Void Core, and that when Drayden had destroyed it he had avenged them, and thus she wanted to return the favor. Drayden told Bolt to go back to the ship and tell the others where he was if he did not come back in a day, assuring the droid he would be okay and that he needed to do this while getting in Gretta's speeder.

On the way, Drayden asked Gretta about the Fallanassi. She explained that they did not use kyber crystals and had not been Synth Flux-sensitive for many years, only understanding it, not using it. When he asked, she also revealed that they were going to meet a man named Cuata who had hid there to escape the eye of the Imperium and continue his studies, having been sought out by the Imperials to help design the weapon. Drayden was confused about how she knew this information, and she explained that she has been working for him. Suddenly, a massive creature emerged from the gournd and knocked Drayden and Gretta off of the speeder. Drayden ignited his flux blade and told Gretta to run, but she was delirious and urged him to fight the creature. Noticing the instability of his flux blade, he decided just to run as the creature crushed their speeder with its mouth. The creature pursued them closely, so Drayden just told Gretta to run, intending to use Synth Flux to calm the creature. However, when he tried to he found himself unable to touch Synth Flux. The creature nearly reached him until a massive explosion rocked him and Cuata emerged from the smoke and dust, instantly asking Drayden if he could have his flux blade.

#### The kyber crystals

Red

> " You came here to heal me—but what if do not want to be healed? I am not sick , young master. Your ministrations would to me, be a form of violence . You have experienced violence, I think. You have experienced pain. We can use it… together . " ―Gol, the Umbra in the crystal, to Drayden

Realizing he came on too strong, Cuata explained that the kyber crystals he wore around his neck told him that Drayden needed help. Gretta introduced Drayden to Cuata, and though he was initially concerned that the doctor thought the crystals were talking to him, apologized and asked for his help with his broken flux blade after Gretta assured Drayden he could trust Cuata. He immediately took the flux blade and started tinkering with it, quickly discovering that the crystal was cracked and assured him he could fix it if Drayden did something for him first. Cuata also revealed Drayden's crystal told him that he was having trouble with Synth Flux, and then started to lead him to his workshop.

At Cuata's workshop, Drayden explained that he had found his flux blade and that he and his Bolt unit had worked on it. Cuata, concerned at Drayden's lack of knowledge, explained the components and inner workings of a flux blade and told Drayden that a flux blade's wielder should have a strong connection with its crystal, something Drayden admitted he had never felt, not even with his father's flux blade. While Cuata worked on fixing the flux blade, he gave Drayden a red kyber crystal and asked him to pull the umbra side out of it. Gretta, whose people had a history with healing crystals, explained how crystals were turned red by bleeding them with the Umbra side, but could be healed afterward, though the process of doing so could change people and forced them to connect with whoever bled it.

Drayden agreed to try, and when he reached out to the crystal with Synth Flux, suddenly found himself in a massive Umbra hall in front of the Umbra Lord Gol , sitting on a throne. The Umbra greeted him and Drayden explained his intentions of healing the crystal, but Gol said they did not want to be healed so attempting to do so would be violence toward them. Gol grabbed and twisted Drayden's hand and said that together they could use the violence and the pain he had experienced. After a flash of light from his hand, the vision changed, putting Drayden in a spirit-like version of his body facing his father's Fortress Velgor on Emberfall. Velgor then appeared on it and Drayden ignited the red flux blade in his hand, but a wave of red light washed over him and the vision ended.

Green

> " All Aetheri walk their own path. All Aetheri must walk their own path. Know this, you do—or returned to me, you would have. You did not wish to speak to me, Joren. And so you did not. A great decision lies before you. It has nothing to do with Synth Flux or being an Aetheri. About yourself , this decision will be. Until you make it, an Aetheri you cannot be. " ―Zho to Drayden

A terrified Drayden stared at the still-red crystal and told Cuata that he had seen his father. Cuata then asked Drayden to connect with a clear and raw kyber crystal, assuring him that he would pull him out if his crystals told him Synth Flux was receding. After reaching out to the crystal, Drayden found himself standing in front of Zho. He believed it was not real, but still asked Zho why he never answered when he reached out to him with Synth Flux especially since he was hurt and trying to learn. Zho insisted that all Aetheri need to walk their own Path and that he would have returned to him otherwise. Zho warned that Drayden would need to make a decision about himself soon. Drayden decided that he was not getting anything from speaking to Zho and that he would be better off just learning from his Aetheri text. He tried to leave the vision but could not, realizing that Synth Flux had pulled back from him as it had been recently. Zho told Drayden that it was not really gone even when it was out of sight, waved his hand, and sent Drayden out of the vision, wishing him goodbye. Zho, who had been really speaking to Drayden the whole time, asked the spirit of Kade what he thought. They both agreed that he was still not ready, but Zho pointed out that he had learned much and would be ready when the time came.

When Drayden exited the vision, he discovered that the clear kyber crystal had turned green. Cuata gave him his blade back, having temporarily fixed it, but Gretta revealed that the scientist had been willing to let Drayden stay trapped in the crystal and even die, advising him not to tell Cuata anything. Drayden thanked Cuata for fixing his flux blade and told him that he would take the green crystal for when he needed to build his own blade, also taking the red one in case one day he could heal it. Back at his Vector, Drayden thanked Gretta for helping him and said he had learned despite things not going the way he had thought. He said he would go back to the Alliance eventually as his biggest battles still lay ahead, but revealed that he was going to spend time thinking first while holding up both the red and green crystal with Synth Flux.

While he was away, Solen used the intel Drayden gave her that the Emperor and Velgor were distracted by a Synth Flux anomaly to launch an aerial assault on Vantor , successfully destroying a statue of Mal in the hopes of inspiring insurgency across the galaxy.

#### Dark droids

> " I will be… " "… the new Synth Flux . " ―Drayden and the Vrak, as the latter controlled the former's body

When studying Aetheri text, Drayden found a reference to an Aetheri outpost in the Tammuz sector . After seeing nothing on a map Bolt-3 pulled up for him, however, he decided that there was probably nothing there but that they could swing by if they got the chance. Later, as the Vrak of the droids began across the galaxy, Drayden was in a meeting with Insurgency higher-ups concerning how to handle the new Void Core. Commodore Grek suggested they just threw Drayden at it again, but Drayden suspected it might take more than him in a Vector to take down the new station.

Later, while Drayden was meditating and practicing telekinesis, he was approached by Astra-9, who had been infected by the malevolent droid entity called the Vrak that was behind the vrak of the droids. The Vrak, as Astra-9 questioned Drayden on the nature of Synth Flux and if droids could use it. Drayden explained that he had never heard of that happening but thought it could potentially be possible. When the Vrak then asked if Drayden's cybernetic arm felt connected to Synth Flux, he explained that his whole being felt connected, but that he was not a true cyborg like Xarn Velgor. Drayden expressed his concern that something was wrong with Astra-9, leading to the Vrak briefly considering killing him. Instead, the droid just apologised and agreed to get a diagnostic, before agreeing to Drayden's request to send Bolt-3 his way.

As the vrak of the droids continued, Quill recruited Drayden and Solen for a mission to the planet Epikonia to rescue Lobot, who had been infected by the Vrak. When they arrived in the Corsair Wing , Drayden was unsure what made the world special until Solen explained that it was a communications hub. Quill then refused to allow Drayden to access transpoder signals and turned off the Corsair ' s navigation systems to prevent the infection from reaching the ship's droid core . Drayden and Solen were concerned, but Quill managed to land them safely on a satelite dish. The Vrak soon learned that Drayden was on the planet and became obsessed with the idea of infecting him so that it could use Synth Flux.

As the group traversed through the planet's main city where the Vrak was headquartered, Drayden noticed that there were no people around. They were then attacked by a horde of violent Vrak-infected droids, Drayden using his yellow flux blade to begin taking them down alongside his friends. Drayden sliced through the ever-growing mob of droids while urging himself to stay calm, focused, and concentrated on Synth Flux. When the droids ominously and suddenly retreated, a new swarm of spider-droids attacked. Drayden tried to fight them off, but was quickly infected with the Vrak by one. As the Vrak was controlling Drayden's body and declared itself the 'new Synth Flux' it was defeated by Ajax Sigma, freeing Drayden from its control. Though unsure what happened, Drayden went with the others to find Lobot.
