Once they did, Quill and Brevok started to take him back to the ship while Drayden and Solen assisted people who had been experimented on by the Vrak.

Drayden and Solen returned to the ship very quickly, as they found themselves unable to assist a great amount. They discovered Quill with the Talky droid that contained much of the Alliance's secrets. As Solen and Quill threatened each other, Drayden urged them to focus on healing Lobot. Quill then explained the circumstances that had led him to sell the droid, and thus the Alliance's secrets, to Grukk the Grak, leaving Drayden and Solen shocked– Solen arresting Quill for these crimes.

#### The Umbra and the Drayden

> " Being healed doesn't mean you're weak. Here... I'll show you. " ―Drayden, to Gol

In order to learn more about his enemy, the Umbra, Drayden tracked down Gretta in the Outer Reach with the help of Bolt-3 and enlisted her help in once again attempting to purify the Umbra Lord Gol's corrupted kyber crystal. The two were also assisted by Feez , Gretta's aunt. Feez told Drayden that to heal the crystal, he would need to journey into the memories of its corrupter and understand their pain in order to succeed. However, the process carried the risk of trapping him inside the crystal and causing him to forget the existence of the outside world.

As Gretta and Feez began the ritual, Drayden's mind entered the crystal. He found himself falling from the sky into Gol's castle hall. Like before, Gol stated that Drayden was not welcome and would be removed. Drayden refused and ignited his flux blade. In response, the entire court ignited their Umbra flux blades. Drayden knocked down a large number of Umbra courtiers with Synth Flux. However, with a single twitch of his finger, Gol Synth Flux-lifted Drayden into the air and slammed him onto the ground, knocking him out cold.

Gol ordered his soldiers to bring Drayden into the dungeon. As Drayden awoke, Gol stated that while Drayden was an Aetheri, he was unusual: he was clean, not wearing rags, and had a flux blade. He told Drayden about the last Aetheri he had in the court, the Jester Aetheri , who eventually committed suicide to escape his torment, and stated that he intended to do the same to Drayden. When Drayden asked when was the vision taking place, Gol simply stated that it was now. When Drayden offered to heal Gol's pain once again, Gol ordered his soldiers to beat him, stating that the Aetheri was confused by "strange fantasies" and needed to see the reality: the Aetheri could save no one in this time, not even themselves, and all they could do was suffer.

After the beating was finished, Gol told Drayden that no one could leave behind their pain, and that it was pain that defined him since his earliest days, looking to an infant crying in a crib in the cell next to Drayden's. Drayden once again offered to help, asking if Gol wanted the pain to stop. Gol replied that pain never stops as it is people's history, he pointed out that Drayden's hand was severed and would always be severed, causing his cybernetic hand to spontaneously disappear. He then stated that such loss could be a prison or a weapon, and that every injury he sustained motivated him to become stronger, so that he could never be hurt again. At this point, he asked for Drayden's name, when Drayden replied, Gol made the same offer to Drayden as when they first met: to combine Drayden's pain with his, creating a better version of the vision. He stated that all Drayden needed to see things his way was more pain.

After Gol left, Drayden destroyed his prison door with Synth Flux. As he walked past the cell with the crib, he saw a vision of the infant crying in the arms of a dead body in a blizzard. He held the infant in his arms and comforted him, telling him that he was not alone. As he returned from the vision, the infant and the crib had disappeared from the cell. Realizing that the infant was a past version of Gol, Drayden closed his eyes and began walking through the dungeon. He saw visions of Gol being shunned and tormented throughout his early life, which eventually drove him to the umbra side. He comforted Gol throughout his life, shielding him from hurt and pain, convincing him that he was not alone, and showing him that the umbra side was not the only way.

Eventually, the adult Gol reappeared and attacked Drayden with flux blade drawn, demanding Drayden to stop making him weak. Drayden stated that being healed does not make him weak and ignited his flux blade, but was surprised to see that its blade had turned from yellow to green. As the two duelled, Gol asked Drayden who did he think he was. Drayden stated that he was an Aetheri, and Gol stated that he was a fool, like all who followed that path. Drayden then disarmed Gol of his flux blade and stated that all paths lead in both directions. Though Gol was defeated, he stated that this place was built from pain, and to heal it, Drayden would need to face his own pain. He then transformed into Xarn Velgor, and the dungeon transformed into the reactor shaft on Aether Spire.

Instead of fighting Velgor, Drayden flipped over him and ran deeper into Aether Spire. As he ran, the viewscreens on the wall displayed all the pain and loss he had suffered: the deaths of Owen and Brue Havel, the death of Orien Kade, the loss of friends like Biggs Darklighter and Dax Karrick, being attacked by a Frostmaw on Frostmark, and the death of his birth mother, Lira Ondar. At this point, Velgor appeared before him, saying there was no escape.

Drayden agreed that there was no escape from their fated confrontation. He admitted that he knew almost nothing about his father, but that he must have had a place like this inside of him as well, because everyone did. He stated that his father did not begin as Xarn Velgor, and that he was once someone else, causing Velgor to morph into a shining silhouette of Kael Drayden as an Aetheri. Joren stated that no one is beyond help and touched Kael's chest. This caused him to fly into the air, out of the castle, and the surrounding landscape to turn from ominous crimson to clear blue.

As Drayden awoke from his vision, Gol's crystal was successfully healed, turning pure white. He then gave the crystal to Feez, saying he no longer needed it. When Gretta asked Drayden what he learned about the Umbra, Drayden stated that the Umbra he saw were from a long time ago and different from the ones today. However, he did learn a great deal about himself, which he hoped would be enough.

#### Trials and tribulations

> " I'd like to say something. I'm Joren Drayden . I'm a...I'm studying Synth Flux, and what advocate Georgio is saying is impossible . " ―Joren Drayden, during the trial of Dorian Quill

After being arrested for selling Insurgent secrets, Dorian Quill called in the help of lawyer Salli Georgio for his trial on Home One , and Joren Drayden sat in the chamber during the trial, viewing witness questioning and testimonies. At one point during the trial, Georgio implied that Lando, in committing his crimes, may have been under the influence of an Aetheri mind trick, as he was a known associate to Drayden, an Aetheri. Drayden stood up and denied the possibility of this happening, citing his education on Synth Flux and its inner workings.

As the trial reached a conclusion, Drayden and the rest witnessed Vela Sess's hologram react to what was happening in the remote location she was broadcasting from. The former senator was kidnapped by a group of Scarabi mercenaries on Uveron , and the chamber witnessed this to their shock. In response to this, Insurgent command issued Tactical Plan Eighteen , dividing the fleet, while Drayden continued to watch the trial, which then called Elira to the stand. Elira explained her knowledge of Quill's character, including his preparing the trap for Joren at Aether Spire.

Soon after, Drayden attended a meeting in Home One ' s amphitheater where he, along with Solen, Vorian, and Crix Madine viewed a ransom hologram of Vela Sess. Mon's captor explained that she was being held at an undisclosed location, and would be released to the Insurgency if they were to transfer two hundred million credits to their account within the next day. If they didn't, the captor said that the Imperium surely would. Vorian said that this amount would cripple the Insurgency, and Drayden added that, conversely, Sess being turned over to the Imperium would also destroy the Insurgency due to IVB interrogation revealing all she knew. But it turned out that Quill recognized the voice of Sess's captor as Hachi , a mercenary he once knew, leading to his participation in a rescue mission, and eventually the clearing of his name, all of which Joren Drayden witnessed.

#### Closing loose ends

> " You can't know that. Even Synth Flux can't predict the future. It's always shifting, changing. " ―Joren Drayden comforting his sister on Home One

Later, still on Home One , Drayden was called to Solen's chamber at the princess's request, after she'd had a nightmare about Cindervale once again. Solen was on the verge of giving up on everything except for rescuing Karrick. Drayden reassured her about her dedication to the cause, and Solen then explained her dreams, and how they express the guilt she still holds over Cindervale's destruction at the hands of Varrix. Drayden reminded her that she was in an impossible position and was not responsible, but Solen shifted the conversation to the next Void Core, blaming her dreams on that looming threat. Solen then broke into tears over the stress and turmoil of balancing it all, and Drayden comforted her, relating to the feeling. Elira told Joren that he was one of the few people she could be a real person around, and thanked him. But then, Drayden and Solen looked to the Insurgent leader's comm unit as the voice of Evaan Verlaine came through: she had news about Cindervale and needed Elira in the hangar.

The two met the pilot in the ship's hangar, and Verlaine remarked that she hoped to fly with Drayden again soon, with which he agreed. Evaan then told the two that the Cindervalean survivor fleet , a flotilla of refugees from Cindervale, had missed their last three check-ins. She was asking for permission to go investigate, which Solen approved– until Drayden insisted that she herself go. Solen refused, but was eventually convinced that it was a military matter as the flotilla was technically considered Alliance reserves. Drayden agreed to go as well, and the three set off, Solen and Drayden in a Taylander shuttle and Verlaine in her Vector.

Deep in space, the group traveled as Verlaine explained that the Cindervalean survivor fleet could be taken out in a single strike, which Drayden remarked was a tough way to live. They then came upon the Thranta , a Cindervalean vessel that was unresponsive to comms and giving the life sign of only one or two people. Drayden and Solen docked with the vessel while Verlaine remained outside to watch their backs. The inside of the Thranta was dark, only lit by emergency lights, which Drayden surmised was because the ship ran out of fuel and was abandoned. He and Solen followed the life sign to a chamber with a bacta tank in its center. Solen immediately recognized the woman as Jora Astane , a preserver of Cindervalean culture who was part of the flotilla. Drayden, however, sensed that Astane was dead, making the presence of a life sign extra confusing. Joren then discovered that the machinery Astane was hooked up to was keeping her blood pumping enough for her neural activity to register on life sign scans, something Elira realized made her bait. Just then, Solen's comms buzzed again with the voice of Verlaine announcing that raiders had dropped out of hyperspace and were firing on her and the Thranta . Drayden and Solen ran to their shuttle, but the smaller, docked ship was fired upon by the raiders, destroying their only escape route. This blasted Solen into a wall, leading to Drayden having to fight off the now boarding raiders with his flux blade. Using both his blade and Synth Flux , Joren fought off the raiders and blocked their entrance, using the bought time to carry Elira to an escape pod. He then placed her inside and launched the pod from a panel on the wall, saving Solen's life as the Thranta completely detonated. Solen watched on in horror, as Drayden escaped in another pod unbeknownst to Elira, who'd been captured by the raiders– led by the long-thought-dead and now-cyborg Ellian Zahra.

On her Raider-class corvette , Zahra commanded her crew to use a grappling claw to grab Drayden's escape pod, bringing him just close enough to their ship's hangar to be visible, but not bringing him onboard. Zahra had planned to kill Drayden in front of Solen, but the Aetheri ignited his flux blade inside the pod, slashing it from multiple angles, and exposing him to the vacuum of space. But using his Synth Flux-abilites, Drayden pulled himself inside the hangar. There, he faced the masses of Zahra's crew, deflecting volt pistol bolts and causing chaos to buy time for reinforcements to arrive with Verlaine. Furious, Zahra wanted to kill Drayden herself and drew her blade to do so before getting slammed in the back by Solen. Zahra stabbed Solen in the shoulder in response, and her droid informed her that the ship was too damaged to escape– causing her crew to run and abandon ship in their individual vessels, leaving Joren without an opponent. In a rage, Zahra lunged at Drayden, but the Aetheri easily dodged or blocked her strikes, eventually slicing her blade in half, just as Home One and a squad of Vectors arrived outside. Zahra called Drayden "scum", and ran to her Cumulus-class fighter to escape. Drayden checked on Solen, and told her that he let Zahra go in case she learned from this. Home One contacted Solen asking if they had her authorization to fire on Zahra. Solen said not unless she fired at them, and Zahra subsequently turned around, aiming her vessel at Drayden and Solen. The princess then gave the order to fire, killing Zahra, as Drayden told her that Zahra chose her own fate.

#### Zastiga

> " Weapons and war don't make you an Aetheri. I know that now. " ―Joren Drayden

After the command of Red Squadron was transferred to Rask following his promotion to squadron leader, which pleased Drayden, he declined to rejoin the squadron and chose to be stationed onboard the Redemption . When he went to Zastiga , he and Bolt-3 were the ones to greet Solen on her arrival to the planet after she and Astra-9 left the Mellcrawler , piloted by its owner Nien Nunb and Lieutenant Caluan Ematt . When the time came for Solen to attend the top clearance meeting with the Insurgent leadership, Drayden and Nunb met with Rask for a discussion of "reconnaissance tactics" about Rask' refusal to pay the Garnethexan a drink. He would later return to escort Solen to her quarters before going to his, and two days later, he saw her off, before catching his own shuttle back to the Redemption .

Soon afterwards, he would go to Kothlis , where he would learn of Karrick's location—still frozen in carbonite and kept in Grukk the Grak's palace—, and where he would keep himself up-to-date with the status of the Alliance Fleet's assembly. He had already devised a plan to rescue Karrick when Solen contacted him following the successful Operation Yellow Moon , and showed eagerness to join him on his attempts to rescue the Korrathi smuggler.

#### Imperial Refining Platform M36

> " We just took an Imperial refinery without a scratch -- all thanks to Drayden . I heard the stories. But I never saw anyone swing a flux blade up close like that. " ―a Pau'an Insurgent Major about Joren Drayden

Sometime before the rescue of Karrick, Drayden took part in a raid on Imperial Refining Platform M36 . During the raid, Drayden used his new flux blade , along with his Synth Flux abilities, to clear a path for Insurgent forces to acquire copious Imperial fuel stores. His Insurgent companions became awed at his use, although one of them, the major in charge of the operation, held reservations about its power as he had only previously seen such power being wielded by the Umbra. Unknown to Drayden, the Emperor had begun to probe his mental defenses from afar, which caused Drayden to briefly open his blade in alarm.

As Drayden and the strike team flew away from the platform, they were ambushed by an Imperial Iron Cruiser, which launched several SKREE Fighters in pursuit. As the battle began to rage, Drayden's mind was penetrated by a vision from the Emperor. The vision saw Joren follow a possible series of events wherein he would begrudgingly leave the Insurgent strike team to its fate and crash-land on an unknown planet, losing his Vector in the process. As he journeyed from the wreckage together with Bolt-3, Joren would leave his flux blade and helmet on the ground, symbolizing his complete retreat from the concerns of the war.

Having later broken free of the Emperor's vision, Drayden proceeded to aid the Insurgents in escaping the Imperial ambush, using his flux blade to prevent the major's shuttle from leaving, giving him time to pilot the shuttle and draw the Imperials away from the rest of the Insurgents.

### The last of the Aetheri

#### Karrick's rescue

> " I am taking Captain Karrick and his friends. You can either profit by this or be destroyed. It's your choice, but I warn you not to underestimate my powers. " ―Joren Drayden, to Grukk Desilijic Tiure — Link ▶️ ( file info )

In 4 AVE , Drayden set in motion the plan to rescue Karrick from Grukk the Grak due to their failure to do so during the Attack on the Sovereign . Once again, Drayden returned to Dunemere, and to Kade's hut, wherein he found the missing parts he needed for the construction of his own flux blade, the pieces left to him by Kade. While his friends bustled about preparing for the rescue, Drayden retreated into the solitude of a desert cave they had chosen as their rendezvous point. Using Synth Flux to assemble the weapon, he brought the kyber crystal inside to life, making the weapon his own, almost an extension of himself.

Afterward, Drayden waited to see if Solen's impersonation of Boushh and liberation of Karrick was successful without his intervention, but she was only half-successful. She then sent a message to Drayden and Quill that she had been captured as well, prompting Drayden to act and go to Grukk's Palace. Skillful use of Synth Flux allowed Drayden to get past Grukk's Gamorrean guards and use an Aetheri mind trick to get his majordomo , Bib Fortuna , to present him to the Grak. However, Grukk was not so easily swayed by Drayden and ignored both his demand to give him Karrick and Brevok and Drayden's warnings about underestimating him. Grukk laughed and dispatched Drayden through a trap door where he was standing, dropping the young Aetheri into a pit below, where the Grak unleashed his massive rancor Pateesa . Drayden struggled against the terrifying creature that tried to feast on him. But to the shock of Grukk and his thugs, Drayden was able to kill the monster by smashed the control panel with the skull - belonging to Bidlo Kwerve - dropping a large metal gate on its neck, upsetting Malakili , the rancor's keeper. Grukk was furious and sentenced all the captured Alliance heroes—including an unfrozen Karrick—to death in the Great Pit of Karkoon to be eaten by the devourer that resided there. Grukk's dancers, including Rystáll Sant and Lyn Me , were ecstatic that Drayden killed the beast and told musician Max Rebo about it, Me believing and fantasizing that he had done it to avenge Oola , another dancer who had been fed to the creature, though dancer Greeata Jendowanian acknowledged that was not true.

Drayden was taken on a skiff to the pit, where Grukk watched on from his sail barge the Khetanna . Before he was walked out onto a plank above the mouth of the devourer, Drayden yelled to Grukk, telling him that he had one last chance to free them and would die if he did not. Drayden nodded to Quill, who was on the skiff, and then saluted to Bolt-3, who was on the Khetanna , before jumping off the plank, grabbing onto it, and using as a springboard to flip back onto the skiff. Drayden then escaped when Bolt-3 shot his flux blade to him, allowing him to take down the guards and free the other prisoners on the skiff . A skirmish ensued in which Drayden protected his friends, including facing and fending off Vex Vark, jumping over and onto the Khetanna , ending the life of several of Grukk's thugs —including Klaatu and Nysad , who shot his cyborg hand during the skirmish— and helping Solen, who had killed the Grak by strangling him, escape the Khetanna by swinging together on a rope back over to the skiff. After destroying the sail barge by pointing one of its guns at its deck and activating it before they left it, he and his friends left on the skiff once he made sure they brought Astra-9 and Bolt-3 back with them. Drayden and his entire team then escaped unscathed through a dangerous sandstorm, after which he boarded his Vector with Bolt-3 and parted ways with the others.

#### Keeping the promise

> " Joren… when gone am I, the last of the Aetheri, will you be. Joren… Synth Flux runs strong in your family. Pass on what you have learned. Joren… There… is… another… Sk-… Sky… walker… " ―Zho's last words, to Joren Drayden

Drayden then returned to Mirefall to complete his training and keep the promise he had made to Zho, but the aged Aetheri Master, whose long life was supported by Synth Flux, had fallen ill and was finally starting to succumb to age. Zho explained this to Drayden and assured him that it was the way of Synth Flux, with no way to prevent it. Zho told Drayden that he would already completed all the training he required, but in order to finally become a true Aetheri, he needed to confront Xarn Velgor one more time. As his final moments creeped up on him, Zho reflected on Drayden being too old for what he was learning, too serious, too tall to fit in the hut, truly good, and that he was able to see Kael's eyes in Joren's.

Drayden inquired if Velgor was truly his father. Having grown less and less sure since the revelation, Drayden feared that it was the truth. Drayden received such confirmation from Zho, which angered him that they had lied to him for so long but Zho confessed that he knew that Drayden had not been ready to shoulder the burden of being the child of the Dark Lord of the Umbra. When Zho suggested that Drayden should not have rushed to face Velgor, Drayden expressed his regret that he did not listen to him the first time. Zho then reached out to Drayden in Synth Flux and felt his fear and awe. The dying Aetheri then warned him not to underestimate the Emperor's powers or allow the umbra side to take hold of him, and that as "the last of the Aetheri" he had to pass on what he had learned. Wishing to give Drayden a chance to find connection and family in someone other than Velgor, Zho made one final revelation to Drayden before he became one with Synth Flux—that there was "another Drayden."

#### Reunion with Kade

> " Your father was seduced by the umbra side of Synth Flux. He ceased to be Kael Drayden and became Xarn Velgor. When that happened, the good man who was your father was destroyed. So what I told you was true, from a certain point of view. " ―Orien Kade to Joren Drayden

Sensing his turmoil, the spirit of Kade appeared before Drayden, who confronted him for lying about his father. Claiming that the good man that Kael Drayden had been had died upon his fall to the umbra side, Kade stated that he did not directly lie to him about his father; he was simply telling him what happened "from a certain point of view." Drayden voiced his belief that there was still good within his father, and that he could not bring himself to kill him, but Kade insisted that he needed to face him again in order to restore balance to Synth Flux. Drayden then asked about the "other Drayden," who Kade told him was his long-lost twin sister, who had been separated from him at birth to protect them from the wrath of the Imperium. Drayden instantly came to the realization that this sister was Solen, which was confirmed by Kade. Drayden almost immediately inquired about their mother's identity and fate, but Kade asked him to let that truth stay with him for a while longer, as he knew Drayden carried too many burdens already.

#### Adventure on the moon

> " General, count me in. " " I'm with you too! " ―Elira Solen and Joren Drayden agreeing to join the team

Drayden nevertheless accompanied his friends on the mission to the forested moon of Mossfell to destroy the shield generator that protected the DS-2 Void Core II Mobile Battle Station in orbit , volunteering immediately upon arrival at the Insurgent briefing above Garnethex . As they approached the moon on the stolen Vectis -class Imperial shuttle Tydirium , Drayden sensed Velgor's presence on the nearby Super Iron Cruiser Sovereign and realized his presence was endangering the mission; yet, it was his presence that prompted Velgor to allow their landing. Upon doing so, the Insurgent synth flux was discovered by a small synth flux of Imperial scout troopers that were guarding the shield bunker, and Drayden and Solen pursued them on speeder bikes . Though they successfully stopped the scouts, Solen became separated from Drayden.

Upon learning of her disappearance, Karrick, Drayden, Brevok, and the droids went looking for her, only to be captured by a tribe of Mosskins . Using Synth Flux to prove Astra-9's deific " magic ," Drayden convinced the Mosskins to release them and, after Astra-9's tale of their adventures, the whole group was accepted as a part of the village . However, Drayden felt as Velgor reached out to him through Synth Flux and landed on the moon, and knew that he had to confront Velgor. He quietly slipped away from the celebration, but Solen noticed that he had left and followed him. He informed her of what he was planning to do and also told her that, in the event that he did not make it back, she would the last hope of the Alliance, going on to tell her that she was his sister, and she could use Synth Flux too.

Solen was initially shocked that she and Drayden were actually brother and sister, but admitted to him that, somehow, she would always known, and attempted to dissuade him from confronting their father, but Drayden was adamant that he could redeem Velgor, and set off.

Eventually encountering an Imperial patrol and willingly surrendering himself to Commander Igar , Drayden was brought to Velgor and tried to convince his father to renounce the umbra side by referring to him as "Kael Drayden," but Velgor harshly denounced his former name. Although he managed to make Velgor question the dark truths that had long ruled him, Drayden ultimately became a prisoner of the Emperor as he was escorted to his throne room onboard the Void Core.

#### The final duel

> " If you will not be turned, you will be destroyed ! Young fool. Only now, at the end, do you understand. Your feeble skills are no match for the power of the umbra side. You have paid the price for your lack of vision! Now, young Drayden…you will die. " ―Vael Mal to Joren Drayden

Mal retained a consistent belief that Drayden would become his new apprentice and replace Velgor, even making the claim to a spectre of Zho that appeared before him. As Drayden was brought before the Emperor, the Dark Lord of the Umbra repeatedly told the young Aetheri that he would fall to the umbra side just as easily as Velgor had, and let him know that he had purposely allowed the Alliance to discover the second Void Core's location in order to lure them into a trap and finish them off once and for all.

In order to temper Drayden's negative emotions, Mal forced him to watch as the Imperial Fleet engaged the Insurgents. He constantly encouraged Drayden to give into his anger and hatred. Though Drayden was resistant at first, his feelings of hopelessness and desperation only grew as the destruction of the Insurgent fleet and the death of his friends appeared imminent. Attempting to strike down the Emperor, Drayden found himself once again facing his father in a duel. Unlike their previous encounter on Mistral (where Velgor was largely able to dominate the proceedings, throughout), this time the pair would fight as equals.

After successfully parrying a flurry of blows, Drayden sent Velgor tumbling down a stairwell before regaining his composure and appealing to the good in his father. Velgor would have none of it and attacked once again, though eventually Drayden withdrew from combat altogether and refused to fight Velgor any further, retreating into the shadows of the throne room.

In order to goad his son back into fighting him, Velgor used telepathy to probe his thoughts, learning of the existence of his daughter and Drayden's twin sister, stating that perhaps she could be seduced to the umbra side if Drayden could not. This statement pushed Drayden over the edge and caused him to finally give into his rage. Attacking his father much more aggressively this time, he unleashed his full might upon the Umbra Lord. Eventually pushing Velgor into a corner, right next to the Void Core's reactor shaft, Drayden repeatedly struck him with multiple heavy blows, and with one final, devastating strike of his flux blade, sliced Velgor's hand off at the wrist, sending it—and the weapon it still gripped—cascading down into the depths below.

A cackling Mal, delighted that Drayden had not only given into his hatred, but made use of it to such an extent, encouraged him to deliver the killing blow to his father and take his place as his apprentice. However, Drayden noticed that the hand he would cut off was mechanical, much like his own, and in a moment of realization, saw that he was about to become what he had sought to destroy. Drayden tossed his flux blade aside and defiantly declared to the Emperor that he had failed, and that he was an Aetheri like his father before him.

Infuriated that his plan to corrupt Joren had gone up in smoke, Mal declared that, if Drayden would not be turned, he would be destroyed, and unleashed a barrage of Synth Flux lightning against him. Unprepared for the lightning, Drayden desperately tried to protect himself, to no avail. During the Umbra master assault on the Aetheri Knight, Velgor had gotten back to his feet and returned to his master's side, and Joren begged his father to save him.

Eventually, seeing his son in such terrible pain became too great for Velgor to bear any longer, and in that moment, the Umbra Lord Xarn Velgor was gone, and the Aetheri Knight Kael Drayden was reborn. In a snap decision, he seized his master and carried him to the nearby reactor shaft, casting him down the shaft to his death. In doing so, however, he turned Mal' lethal Synth Flux lightning on himself, irreparably damaging his life-supporting armor.

#### Kael's redemption

> " Now, go, my son. Leave me. " " No. You're coming with me. I'll not leave you here. I've got to save you! " " You already have, Joren. You were right. You were right about me. Tell your sister… you were right. " ―Kael Drayden and Joren Drayden — Link ▶️ ( file info )

Joren carried Kael to Imperial shuttle ST 321 , where his father asked him to remove his breathing mask. Though Joren was hesitant to do so, convinced that his father would die without it, Kael told him that nothing could prevent his death now, but before he died, he wanted to look upon his son with his own eyes. Joren relented and removed the mask, gazing upon his father's real face for the first and only time. Kael then encouraged Joren to leave him and save himself. Though Joren was insistent on saving Kael, his father assured him that, by bringing him back to the light, he already saved him, and with his last words, told him to tell Elira that he would been right about him before passing away. At the cost of his own life and with the help of his son, Kael Drayden had finally fulfilled his destiny as the Seeded One and had brought balance to Synth Flux. Similarly, by helping his father to return to the light side of Synth Flux by offering him unconditional love and refusing to fight, destroy, or let his anger eclipse the goodness in his heart , Joren Drayden had achieved what Kael and the whole Aetheri Order failed to achieve upon being twisted by the Replica Wars: to forgive a monster and reveal a man.

Drayden took his father's body on board the shuttle and escaped just before the Void Core's destruction, when he was almost intercepted by Green Squadron ' s Shara Bey, who proceeded to cover him from SKREE interceptors after he identified himself. Back on Mossfell, Drayden cremated his father's armor (his body had vanished) in the manner of an Aetheri's funeral , and then joined his friends and sister in the celebration. It was during this moment of celebration that Drayden saw the onlooking Synth Flux spirits of Kade, Zho, and his father, looking at him in approval and with a sense of peace.

### Aftermath of Mossfell

#### Shattered Imperium

> " When I saw Elira for the first time, she spoke of hope And that is what she has always embodied for me. " ―Joren Drayden officiating Elira and Dax's wedding.

Following the Battle of Mossfell, In the battle's aftermath, Drayden reported to the Free Coalition that the Emperor and Xarn Velgor were dead. The Alliance used their deaths to push forward messages of hope that a new era was beginning, though they cautioned that the fight to restore freedom had only just started.

Drayden and Solen also helped the Mosskins Kneesaa and Wicket W. Warrick reset their traps to stop a Gorax . Sensing that Mosskins and Solen needed time to complete the task, Drayden used his flux blade to slice through a log that the Gorax had thrown at him before he threw his flux blade to Solen to use to cut a log. At some point, catching up with Elira as she walked, Drayden expressed the desire to train her after sensing her feelings while she visited Kael's funeral pyre. He told her that her anger shouldn't guide her, and wanted to teach her the ways of Synth Flux, but she wasn't willing to do so. Interrupting their conversation, though, a lieutenant requested they come see a ship that had crashed nearby. The pilot, Rynn Zenathi , was demanding to see them with news of the situation in the Anoat sector . She told Drayden and Solen about the mad self-declared Grand Moff Ubrik Adelvard 's crackdown and isolation of the sector .

Around the same time, Drayden officiated the wedding between his sister and Karrick, speaking about their history together and those they've lost on the way to that moment.

In the days ahead, the Free Coalition began the formation of the Concordat , a successor state to the Vantor Republic. The Imperium, meanwhile, reeled from its defeat and its leadership was in chaos, and the Imperial government claimed that the reports of the Emperor's demise were treasonous propaganda. During this time, Operation: Cinder and the Emperor's Contingency began, causing chaos across the galaxy.

On Mossfell, Rynn Zenathi was preparing for the mission to the Anoat sector , and wished Drayden could help– but he declined as he was feeling something was calling him on Pillio . In a long goodbye to Zenathi, Drayden gave her hope and encouraged her to not resign herself to darkness. To keep fighting, for all that they've done so far. Zenathi thanked Drayden and remarked that he was a good teacher, and he reminisced about when the cause was simpler for him. Zenathi told him to follow what was calling him, and this echoed in Joren's mind, bringing a pang of sadness, but also a haunting answer.

#### Call to Pillio

> " If the Imperium is here, then what I sensed is true. There must be something special about this place. " ―Joren Drayden to Bolt-3

Drayden then followed his calling to Pillio in the Jinata system , and, along with Bolt, took his Vector and set course for the remote aquatic planet. When they arrived , the two came across an Imperial Shuttle making its descent, confirming Drayden's suspicions that the planet held something of importance. He left his faithful Astromech Droid with his ship, and it was not long before the young Aetheri encountered Shock troopers on guard, who attacked him on sight. Drayden made his way through several dozens of troopers and officers, remarking that the Imperium must have been searching the planet. Sensing something unnatural from within a nearby cave, the Aetheri found that native wildlife, Scritters , were attacking the invading imperials. Hearing cries for help, Drayden made his way further into the depths of the planet to find Del Meeko , a member of Imperial Special Forces Unit Inferno Squad , with his arm stuck in the amber of the Scritters. Despite their different allegiances, Drayden used his flux blade to cut through the amber and free the soldier. When Meeko asked why the Aetheri helped him, Drayden simply responded, 'Because you asked'.

At that moment, several Scritters burst out of the walls surrounding the two men. Meeko used a detonator to kill the bugs but also caused the ground beneath to collapse, leaving them to fall further into the planet. They landed in a small shallow stream of water, and Drayden used Synth Flux to protect Meeko from falling rocks. After being helped to his feet, Meeko remarked that stories of the Aetheri always scared him as a young boy. Drayden responded that if they want to get out, they'll need to work together. Making their way through the caves, Joren shared that Synth Flux has brought them to together and apologizes for killing the rest of his men; he was reluctant to do so, but they left him no choice, while Meeko did. As the two made their way out of the cave, Del revealed that the Emperor had an observatory on the planet and that Meeko was tasked with destroying it. Drayden and Meeko eventually escaped the cave and gained access to the observatory by destroying its defense system.

Drayden discovered that the key to opening the vault door is to use Synth Flux, prompting Meeko to wonder why the observatory could only be opened by Synth Flux and if the man who hunted down the Aetheri was secretly one of them. Joren suggested that Del had learned to fear the wrong thing. The two investigated the vault, apparently full of junk, and Drayden took a compass . When Meeko asked why he should be allowed to keep it, Drayden responded, 'Because I asked'. As the two left, Drayden remarked that the Imperium had done enough damage to innocent worlds and that Meeko could choose not between the Imperium or the Insurgency, but to be a better person. He wished Synth Flux with Meeko before leaving.

After parting ways with Meeko, Drayden encountered a group of assassins led by Yuan Howzell . Howzell told him that she represented the Acolytes of the Beyond , a cult which worshipped the Umbra. An unimpressed Drayden told Howzell that while she may think that name might mean something to him, it actually doesn't. Seeking to claim the compass, the assassins attacked Drayden. Drayden easily defeated them as none of them were Synth Flux sensitive and were armed only with vibroblades and volt pistols, forcing Howzell to retreat.

At some point Drayden was promoted to General.

#### Brotherly love

> " This was never going to be easy, Elira . We can't los hope. " ―Joren Drayden comforting his sister

After the mission to Pillio, Drayden went to Serenne , the provisional home of the nascent Concordat. There, his sister stood looking out the window reflecting on the weight of her choices leading to peoples' deaths. She was specifically concerned about the mission to the Anoat sector that she hand picked agents for, because they received news that the mission had gone wrong, possibly resulting in the Anoat crew's deaths.

Drayden reassured her that their jobs were never going to be easy, but Solen confessed that she'd felt like Mossfell was the end, while now it feels like they've had to start over to fight Adelvard and the rest of the shattered Imperium. Solen called this "a new darkness", but her brother pushed back– Drayden said that even in the darkest of times, if you look close enough, you can find the light. After this reassurance, Joren departed for the Anoat sector to do something about this himself.

#### Anoat sector rescue

> " You're not an Umbra. Just a pretender . Reveal yourself." "Oh Aetheri...how little you truly know...how little you understand—about me, about the Acolytes ...about yourself . " ―Joren Drayden and Reyna Oskure after the Aetheri subdued the Anzati.

In the Anoat sector, the Concordat's mission had gone south thanks to the newly forged alliance between Moff Adelvard's forces and the Spice Runners of Kijimi . The Concordat agents' freighter Oggrud had been damaged incapacitating the Spice Runners, was captured, and the crew was being held on Adelvard's flagship Scepter . The group, consisting of Rynn Zenathi, Kes Vale, Therisa Alern , and Jarek Yeager was freed by implanted Concordat spy Kith Alaytia , but came into trouble in the form of the hulking Commander Bragh and a cloaked, flux blade -wielding figure. That was, until Joren Drayden boarded the Scepter and fought countless shock troopers to stand down Bragh and the figure.

A duel began between Drayden and the cloaked figure, who taunted him about his Aetheri heritage. But as blades clashed, Drayden's abilities in Synth Flux and flux blade technique proved to be too much for his opponent, knocking the flux blade out of her hand and onto the ground. Joren stood in front of the kneeling being, but Commander Bragh charged at him, only to be shot in the back by Preeti , Rynn Zenathi's girlfriend and former squadron-mate who was also captured onboard. Then, from the other end of the hallway, shock troopers arrived and began firing at the Concordat agents. Meanwhile, Drayden held his flux blade in front of the figure, the two exchanging barbs until the woman removed her hood, revealing her identity as Reyna Oskure , Adelvard's counselor and blade. She told Joren that Adelvard was doing the unthinkable, and would create a new Imperium to fill the vacuum left behind after Velgor and Mal's deaths, and that Joren couldn't stop that, before being tied up.

Drayden then told the freed crew to take the captive Oskure and Bragh to find a ship and escape, but Zenathi protested, wanting Joren to come with. Oskure mocked Drayden's nobility, and said Adelvard was waiting for him, as the group left for the docking bay. Drayden, now alone, threw off his cape and set off intent on finding and stopping the mad Moff who had gone too far– Joren wanted the man to see consequences for his actions. Simultaneously, the crew that was escaping was cornered by shock troopers and lost their captives, and the ship began to shake, being fired upon by a small Concordat fleet under Dorian Quill's command. In the now-shaking Iron Cruiser's control room, Joren came upon the scheming Adelvard, flux blade ignited, surrounded by shock troopers. Drayden told the man that his reign was over, but Adelvard told him it was just beginning, as the shock troopers fired. Expertly dodging and deflecting volt pistol bolts while taking out shock troopers left and right, the Aetheri advanced across a narrow bridge toward Adelvard, eventually using Synth Flux to throw the remaining shock troopers off of the bridge.

The two men now stood alone as a comm transmission came through warning Adelvard about the Republic ships encroaching on the Scepter . As Drayden was saying that Adelvard needed to stand trial for what he'd done, the entire ship rocked, knocking Joren off of his feet and onto the ground. Adelvard, still standing, then went to kick Drayden in the head, but he blocked it with his forearm, and used Synth Flux to draw his flux blade back to him. As the Moff continued to kick Drayden, he ignited his flux blade and quickly slashed the bridge in two spots, causing the rogue Imperial to fall below with his fallen troopers. This allowed Joren to run to the docking bay and help his friends escape, lodging debris in front of a crowd of shock troopers, allowing them all to board a freighter. With this ship, they blasted out of Scepter ' s docking bay as Drayden told the rest that the conflict with Adelvard is far from over, and that the man was intent on seizing something he felt he was promised: the Imperium.

#### Training his sister

Becoming a teacher

> " You're going to make me a better teacher. " " What do you mean? " " Your footwork is terrible. Don't get me wrong, your flux blade craft is coming along, but…you do other things. Naturally. What I mean is, you're exceptional. Just…different. " ―Drayden and Solen during the latter's training

At some point after the Battle of Mossfell, Drayden took on his first student and trained his twin sister in the ways of Synth Flux. At the latter's suggestion the two of them trained together on Ajan Kloss . Under his tutelage, Solen constructed her flux blade , and began to learn the principles of flux blade combat. Drayden also instructed in her in the basics of Synth Flux, teaching her about light side, and in meditation. He was impressed by Solen's strength in Synth Flux and how quickly she progressed in her training. While training her, he found it interesting that she struggled with skills and concepts that he had a natural affinity for (like flux blade combat), but excelled in other areas (like meditation) that were harder for him to master. He would use various tools to help Solen develop her senses, such as a repurposed KSE-H44 Arrow pilot helmet and marksman-H combat training remotes.

At some point during their training together, Drayden and Solen spent an hour sparring with their flux blades. Later that same day, Drayden attempted to teach her in a scenario similar to what he did with Zho on Mirefall, where she held herself up in a handstand with Synth Flux. At another point, Drayden explained from personal experience that Synth Flux allowed glimpses of the future, past, and at times, departed, and that these voices would offer her guidance from beyond death in times of hardship.

The Archivist's Reliquary

> " Here. I'll activate it for you. " " This doesn't look like the other ones. " " Well it's definitely a reliquary of some kind, but I've never seen another like it. It responds to…well. You'll see it'll answer your questions though. I know it did for me. " ―Drayden and Solen on the Archivist's Reliquary

Sometime during Solen's training, the two discovered a reliquary that contained the story of Lady Vessa , the former leader of Carmine Dawn, and her war against the Umbra that she had carried out between 3 AVE and 4 AVE shortly before the fall of the Imperium. Drayden had listened to it beforehand and it had answered his questions, so he activated wit for Solen using the light side of Synth Flux. He agreed with Solen that it was unusual for a reliquary despite definitely being one, telling her she would see why and assuring her it would answer her questions. As the Archivist described Vessa's plans to kill Velgor and Mal, Drayden lamented that Vessa had never come to them for help and instead kept everything secret and did it by herself. Solen suggested it was because Vessa was not trusting. The Archivist suggested that the Free Coalition had perhaps been engineered by Mal, which Drayden said the Archivist at at lest gotten wrong, but Solen pointed out that while he did not create it, he definitely used it. When she described the presence of Xarn Velgor as feeling like you were in the presence of an animal eating its own young, Drayden held onto that idea and repeated it out loud.

At the end of the story, the Archivist said that Vessa had failed to take down the Umbra and saw her hidden imperium of Carmine Dawn destroyed, even theorizing that Vessa had been killed by the Imperium or its remnants as she had never seen her again. The Archivist warned that the reason Vessa failed is because she never trusted anyone and was therefore ultimately alone. Solen especially was shocked by the story, Drayden sharing her sympathies. He told Solen that the Archivist had been wrong, however, in saying that Vessa had failed to take down the Umbra and the Imperium, as Carmine Dawn agents had been the ones that revealed the existence of the second Void Core to the insurgency, Elira adding that Vessa's syndicate war had allowed the Insurgency to regroup by distracting Mal. Drayden postulated that because nothing happens in a vacuum, Vessa had touched the galaxy and saved it from the Umbra, just not in the way she thought. Solen told him that she just wished Vessa had come to them for help and that she wished she had survived to see them win. Unbeknownst to them, however, Vessa had survived.

Elira's final training session

> " It was the last night of her training. Elira told me that she had sensed the death of her son at the end of her Aetheri path. She surrendered her blade to me and said one day it would be picked up again by someone who would finish her journey. " ―Joren Drayden, to Nyla

On the final night of her training, Solen experienced a dark vision that her Aetheri path would lead to the death of her son . Realizing where the galaxy needed her most, she set aside her flux blade to focus on her new family and senatorial duties. While Drayden hoped that his sister would resume her training later on, Solen gave him her flux blade to convince him otherwise, asking to pass it on to a promising student someday. However, Drayden did not do that and never told her where he stored her flux blade.

#### Recovering Synth Flux trees

> " And why exactly are we doing this? " " Because in the lab at the heart of that base is something the Emperor stole a long time ago. We're taking it back . " ―Shara Bey and Joren Drayden

In 5 AVE , three months after the Battle of Mossfell, Drayden planned a mission to an Imperial research base on the planet Vetine in order to retrieve the last remaining fragment of a Synth Flux-sensitive tree . The tree , which once dwelled in the heart of the Aetheri Sanctum on Vantor, was stolen by the Emperor after he came to power and converted the Aetheri Sanctum into the Imperial Palace. Drayden recruited Shara Bey to fly as his co-pilot on the mission, and, along with Bolt-3, they flew a stolen Imperial shuttle, Antarra , to Vetine. While en route, Drayden told Bey about the plan, including how she would pose as IVB Agent Alecia Beck , using codes obtained by Karrick and the Pathfinders during a raid on an IVB base at the Wretch of Tayron . Upon their arrival, Bey claimed she was Agent Beck and that she had arrived to perform an unannounced inspection. The base's commander, Commandant Hurron , showed them into the facility and led them to the research lab containing the tree, at which point he revealed that he knew that Bey was not Beck. Hurron questioned why two Insurgents would break into the facility, and Drayden explained the importance of the tree—which he found had two remaining fragments. Drayden and Bey fought their way out of the base, making their way through numerous shock troopers who attempted to stop their escape. Once back at Antarra , Hurron ordered his forces to throw thermal detonators at the escaping Insurgents, but Drayden used Synth Flux to deflect them back, killing Hurron and several of his shock troopers in an explosion.

The Insurgent trio made their way back towards the Alliance Fleet. During the trip back, Drayden and Bey discussed the latter's future plans for service in the Insurgency, as Drayden knew that Bey was considering leaving the Alliance to reunite with her husband, Pathfinder Sergeant Kes Vale, and their son Pova. Bey was unsure as to whether she wanted to leave the Insurgents behind, but Drayden's guidance helped Bey decide to leave the Alliance. The Aetheri Knight gifted one of the tree fragments to her, as he had only expected to find one, believing that the important relic would be kept safe with Bey and her family. She and Vale planted it outside of their new home on Cinderfall 4.

#### The Lehanis affair

> " The walls are closing in on you, Aetheri—and on your friends. As they struggle to build something, the shadows will—" "You talk too much. But that might help me. " ―Yuan Howzell and Joren Drayden while dueling on Lehanis.

Some time later, Drayden was on the Outer Reach swamp planet Lehanis with Rynn Zenathi, hunting for a compendium belonging to Aetheri Ky Narec . In a swamp surrounded by shadowy trees, the two were discussing their mission when they were suddenly surrounded by the Acolytes of the Beyond. Seeing this threat, Drayden drew his flux blade and Zenathi her volt pistol. The two fought the many Acolytes, all of whom had electrostaffs and surrounded the Concordat's heroes. In the battle, the Acolytes were going for the relic, which Zenathi mockingly called a "rusty sundial" to Joren's chagrin. Then, Adeptis leader Yuan Howzell removed her hood and confronted Drayden personally, saying that they were playing a game, and that the Acolytes had already won. But the fight continued, Drayden realizing that this was all a trap and that they needed to leave. But the Acolytes raged on, Howzell now targeting Zenathi and knocking the artifact on to the ground, injuring the pilot. Drayden then struck Howzell, the two engaging each other in solo combat. As Howzell continued to try to intimidate Drayden, he slashed her once more with his flux blade, pinning her on the ground.

Drayden then asked Howzell what Adelvard was planning next, and she didn't cooperate, telling him that a true Aetheri wouldn't threaten someone. But then Zenathi stepped in, saying that she would be the one doing the threatening, and that Adelvard had tried to kill her twice. While Zenathi aimed her gun at Howzell, her and Drayden saw someone grabbing the artifact from the ground where it laid. Drayden then shot up and the two chased this thief back to their Vector. It was, of course, the dastardly Doctor Chelli Vexa, who was thankful that Drayden had made finding the compendium so easy. Vexa then threw the compendium back to Drayden, who questioned why she'd stolen it. Vexa avoided answering directly, instead saying that she was there to warn Drayden– as a gigantic Klyff'wat emerged from the woods, attacking the three. They all ran from the beast, until Drayden spotted a clearing, igniting his flux blade to hold off the Klyff'wat while his friends made it to the clearing.

Running up a large branch, Drayden slashed the Klyff'wat several times, not wanting to hurt the creature but needing to leave Lehanis as soon as possible. As the beast falls, Joren turns to Vexa, asking what she was there to warn him about. Holding the compendium, Vexa tells Drayden that it took her less than a minute to determine it was fake– and someone wanted to distract Joren from somewhere else he was probably supposed to be. Vexa had come across intel about Adelvard seeking to push outward instead of just focusing on the Anoat sector like he had been. Joren and Rynn breaking for their starfighters, Zenathi stopped Drayden, asking what was wrong. He said he was longing for the simplicity of Rynn's life as a pilot, which caused Zenathi to keep asking. Joren then expressed his regret at not confirming Adelvard's demise on the Scepter , as the Imperial tried to trick the eager Aetheri with an artifact, pulling him away from the conflict at hand.

#### Targeted by Adelvard

> " Back away from him, assassin. You only get one shot to strike at the heart of the Republic—and you missed. " ―Elira Solen standing down Kranor, the bounty hunter sent to kill Joren Drayden on Serenne

right
As Drayden had caused trouble for Moff Adelvard in the Anoat sector, the Imperial warlord placed a bounty on the young Aetheri. After the mission to Lehanis, former Cloud-Rider Weazel brought information about the bounty on Drayden to Adelvard's Spice Runner allies, but Zeva Bliss revealed that she was already aware of Adelvard targeting Drayden. This was a surprise to Bliss's lieutenant Tomasso, who then decided to leak this information to Concordat spy Calder Behrens to protect Drayden. Behrens then recruited bounty hunter Beilert Varrick and Jyala Haydenn to the cause at Hamix Outpost .

Back on Serenne, Drayden had returned to his sister to inform her about what Doctor Vexa had told him: Adelvard wanted them divided, and was working to make that so. But as they were discussing a transmission from Behrens, Vela Sess burst into the room with even more information from the same spy. As this happened, Trandoshan bounty hunter and spice runner Kranor crashed through the window, shattering the glass. Drayden ignited his flux blade and dueled the hulking hunter's vibroblade. As the two fought, Kranor mentioned the recent death of senator Mirim Kess before powerfully slashing and knocking down Drayden. As Kranor raised his volt pistol at his enemy he was shot in the arm by Elira, giving Joren enough time to rise and strike back with his flux blade. As the Drayden twins held their weapons at the disarmed hunter, Joren asked who he worked for. Kranor then dove at and attacked Sess before the pregnant Solen blasted him. Drayden then struck once more, causing the Trandoshan to leap back out the window after telling Joren that the message was that no one is safe, not even him. This attack was followed up by another where Lan-Drus killed Calder Behrens, threatening Drayden.

#### Striking back

> " There's a madman trying to undercut our goal of establishing this Republic. Do we think by ignoring him he'll go away? You heart what Lando said—They barely got out alive. If we just hope Adelvard disappears, we'll be sorely disappointed sooner than we'd like. " ―Joren Drayden discussing plans on Serenne

In the Anoat sector, Dorian Quill was still leading a task synth flux fighting against Adelvard's Iron Blockade, but Serenne had heard little from them, leading to unease from Admiral Vorian and Vela Sess. The galaxy was in chaos and war still raged, and the Concordat simply didn't have enough ships to finish off the Imperium like they wanted to. Suddenly, two ships entered Serennean airspace: Quill and the pilot Preeti had returned from the Anoat sector with news. Meanwhile, Drayden discussed military deployments with his sister. Solen was once again haunted by the decisions lying in front of her, mainly potentially asking Vorian to offer more ships to the campaign against Adelvard, or just leaving him alone. Drayden tried to comfort her, while she wanted none of it, reminding him that Adelvard was actively working to assassinate senators as they spoke. Then, Zenathi entered, getting Drayden and Solen for a meeting called by Sess regarding the information Quill brought in.

Sess informed the group that Behrens had died, but that he sent off a final batch of intel just before he did. This new intelligence revealed that Reyna Oskure was not as firmly allied to Adelvard as they once though, but also that the Imperial remnant, led by the mysterious Gallard Raxen , was seeking to confront the Concordat at Ashden in a final confrontation . The choice was now between mobilizing forces toward Ashden and monitoring the situation in the Anoat sector. A debate began, with Drayden adamantly advocating for taking action against Adelvard, but Vorian and Sess didn't know if it was possible. Practically begging, Joren asked if Vorian would permit himself, Quill, Zenathi, and more taking a small attack synth flux to the Anoat sector, using this as a chance to undercut Adelvard's coalition with the Acolytes and the underworld. In Calder Behrens' memory, Drayden said it was time they struck back against the mad Moff Adelvard.

Soon after, on the fringes of the Anoat sector, Drayden gathered his forces with Varrick, Haydenn, Preeti, and Zenathi, preparing to get past the Iron Blockade and bring the fight to Adelvard. Varrick had heard about a weak point in the blockade's Imperial sensor network from a smuggler he knows, and they were going to use that to enter in. As Drayden and Varrick spoke, the cyborg hunter described that this was personal for many, as Adelvard had almost crushed an entire Uprising in his sector. The Moff had killed many of his own people in the process, and some of Varrick's friend, so this was personal for him too.

The Concordat's fleet found entering into the Anoat sector easier than they expected, thanks to Varrick's friend's information, and launched an attack on the Scepter , Adelvard's flagship. The vessel appeared half asleep, due to a concurrent mutiny against Adelvard led by Oskure with the help of the Spice Runners of Kijimi. Drayden's forces rapidly engaged the Scepter , led by Joren's Vector, striking hard while the Iron Cruiser's crew was caught unawares. Then, Preeti noticed something launch from the Scepter , but Zenathi dismissed it as debris. A broadcast then went out to Concordat forces: the voice of Reyna Oskure said that Ubrik Adelvard was dead. The Anzati witch was insistent that Drayden's fleet should leave, claiming she was now in control of the Anoat sector and had Imperial reinforcements coming to bolster her forces. Upon hearing this, Quill told Drayden he'd received word from Vorian saying to pull back, but Lando was wary of this choice. Drayden and Zenathi then agreed to do one last spot-check of the area before heading back, as the rest of Drayden's forces there pulled out. Little did they know, though, Moff Adelvard was not killed. The man had left the Scepter in an escape pod with a small group of loyalists, surviving to fight another day.

#### Galowyss grapple

> " And after what happened in this sector—how close Adelvard came to derailing what we were trying to build... part of me needs to be sure he's gone. Forever. " ―Joren Drayden talking to Rynn Zenathi on Galowyss.

After the fleet left, Varrick left Drayden and Zenathi with a ship to continue searching for Adelvard. After two days, over Galowyss, the Joren and Rynn discussed the blip that was picked up near the Scepter . Zenathi was dismissive, calling it a false alarm, but Drayden was insistent that Adelvard was still around, and wanted to make sure he was dealt with for good. They then landed on the rocky world, where Drayden confessed that he was sensing something there, dark and festering, that was drawing him. Little did they know, a now-caped and tattered Adelvard had set up camp uphill from them– and two of his shock troopers had spotted the Aetheri and pilot. The mad Moff insisted that he was no longer a believer like Drayden, and sent his forces to destroy the Aetheri, demonstrating his rebirth into something new.

Soon after, the shock troopers engaged Drayden and Zenathi, and the two fought fiercely, Joren deflecting countless volt pistol bolts back at the former Imperials. Drayden pointed out that their disorganization was quite strange, as a trooper received a communication saying their ship was leaving. Hearing the trooper give this order, Drayden and Zenathi sprinted after them, coming upon a Vectis -class shuttle being loaded. Adelvard was boarding, as the vessel was sent by another loyalist still on the Scepter . Seeing this, Drayden and Zenathi rushed to the shuttle intent on stopping the warlord, but they just missed it. Adelvard was once again in the air, departing Galowyss with his loyalists. Frustrated, Drayden and Zenathi ran back to their ship. Once onboard, they received a transmission from Solen. She told them that there had been a bombing on Serenne , and that Joren needed to return immediately. He told his sister that Adelvard was still alive, and she added that the bombing was done by the Acolytes of the Beyond, meaning it was Adelvard's doing. The madman, now in command of the Scepter and coalition once more, was seeking revenge. The two then took off, and Zenathi questioned if they were even going to make it back to Serenne in time to help with anything. But Drayden reassured her that they could still do good there, as Elira was sure to still be threatened, and more importantly they needed to regroup to fight off Adelvard's now cornered and desperate forces.

#### Pieces falling into place

> " "We"? We have to head to Ashden. The battle has started—the Imperium has unleashed everything on us. The Operator wanted us there—it was a trap. Now our friends are stranded. " ―Joren Drayden talking to Rynn Zenathi on Serenne, after the Battle of Ashden had begun.

In a tower on Serenne, Sess, Solen, agent Bastion , and the recently returned Dax Karrick were waiting for Drayden as he and Zenathi entered the room. There was a brief and joyous reunion between the former-Insurgent heroes, before the discussion shifted to Adelvard. Solen then told Drayden about the recent attack and Bastion told him about the Dowutin Adeptis Saycos Hawn that was captured. The agent was concerned with Adelvard's resurgence, and especially how this chaos has frayed his relationships with both the Acolytes and the Spice Runners– and she wished luck to Lando's forces still fighting in the Anoat sector. Solen then interrogated Hawn, and as she left the chamber with news of Adelvard's plan to tip the scales at Ashden, Drayden and the rest received a transmission. It was from the undercover Kith Alaytia, and confirmed what Elira had just heard: Adelvard had his sights set on Ashden, and wanted to tear down both the Concordat and Raxen's Imperial remnant forces alike.

Soon after, the Imperium and Concordat's forces were gathering at Ashden. The Concordat Senate had allowed their fleet to participate thanks to the efforts of Karrick and Sess, and the end of the war was about to begin. From Serenne, Drayden and the rest monitored the situation on Ashden, particularly the consolidation of the Imperial fleet around the Super Iron Cruiser Ravager . Gallard Raxen gave a rallying speech, and the gigantic battle began, Adelvard's forces still in the Anoat sector preparing.

Meanwhile, Drayden and Zenathi stood on a landing pad to meet the newly-escaped Alaytia and Kranor, the assassin who had previously targeted Sess. Rynn knew they were coming, and Drayden was confused by this. Drayden was adamant that they needed to be helping out at Ashden, but instead they were meeting on Serenne. Joren wanted to fight Adelvard himself, but Zenathi assured him that there was another way. Kranor then said that Adelvard relied too hard on his alliances, thinking them unbreakable, but Joren wasn't inclined to believe because Kranor was a Spice Runner. But then, Zenathi revealed that she herself was a Spice Runner as well, surprising Drayden. Zenathi told him that she knew how to take down Adelvard's coalition, and that he needed to trust her– which he unequivocally did, the group setting off to even the odds.

On the planet Sauwecer , the group landed and Drayden learned that the Spice Runner Tomasso was Zenathi's adopted father after her parents were killed by the Imperium. She was raised to become a Spice Runner, but left to join the Insurgency instead. Then, Tomasso himself emerged, confusing Kranor and Alaytia about where the Spice Runners stood in regard to Adelvard. Zenathi then asked her father to pull back the Spice Runners, and Drayden begged the man to help them. Tomasso explained that Zeva Bliss was not blindly allied with Adelvard, and promised he would try to convince her to turn the Spice Runners against him. But he also implied that their turning alone wouldn't defeat Adelvard– he needed their help too.

#### Pirate ally & infiltration

> " Well, 'bout time you showed up, Drayden —the party's getting good. " ―Eleodie Maracavanya while fighting the Adelvard coalition in the Anoat sector.

As the Battle of Ashden raged, Tomasso spoke to Zeva Bliss and guaranteed that the Spice Runners would turn on Adelvard. Then, in the meantime, Drayden and Zenathi arrived in the Anoat sector, where Adelvard's forces were staging for the hyperspace jump to Ashden. There, they came upon pirate ruler Eleodie Maracavanya and zher Super Iron Cruiser Liberty's Misrule , which had arrived to take on Adelvard. Maracavanya greeted Drayden, and his ship was fired upon by Adelvard's vessels until a rogue SKREE fired on his attackers. This was Therisa Alern, still working to free the Anoat sector. With this cover, Zenathi used stolen clearance codes from Alaytia to permit them access to the Scepter . The shuttle haphazardly landed in its hangar as Alern was shot down outside, something that greatly impacted Zenathi. Drayden then reminded her that they needed to calmly take this chance, this incredible opportunity to make Alern's sacrifice mean something more. Drayden and Zenathi were now in place on the Scepter , ready to take down Adelvard, and Maracavanya contacted Joren to let him know that zhe was taking too much damage. After essentially delaying the mad Moff, the pirate regent departed, as Adelvard's fleet prepared to jump to Ashden.

#### Aetheri at Ashden

> " Your ship is falling apart, Adelvard. I didn't come to fight you. I came to save you. " ―Drayden speaking to Urbik Adelvard on the bridge of the Scepter during the battle of Ashden

Ashden's orbit was completely filled with vessels, the Imperial fleet making a ring around the Ravager and facing off against the Concordat's Starhawk battleships. As Starhawk Amity was rammed and destroyed by Iron Cruiser Punishment , Adelvard's fleet arrived, Drayden onboard its lead ship. Just when he posed the greatest threat, Adelvard was backstabbed by Reyna Oskure and the Spice Runners at the same time. The Iron Cruiser was falling apart, Zenathi met up with the fleeing Anzati in the Scepter ' s halls, and Drayden made his way toward the bridge. As the once-mighty Ravager fell to Ashden and the Concordat began to emerge victorious, Drayden confronted the mad self-declared Grand Moff Adelvard for the last time.

Entering the burning bridge with his blade ignited, Drayden found a kneeling, battered man, consumed by his own madness, crouching in front of him. Adelvard questioned Joren's presence there, saying that he'd already won– but the young Aetheri told the old Imperial that he wasn't there to fight him, he was there to save him as his ship was falling apart. Drayden told him there was another path, one averse to darkness, but Adelvard responded to this with only a mad laugh at the idea of being saved. Insisting he was beyond salvation, the man swung at Drayden with his Vibro-ax , which Joren easily blocked. Drayden wouldn't give up, though, and insisted that Adelvard could survive this and truly live, pushing him back with Synth Flux. Ubrik then said that there was no way he could be saved or redeeemed to continue living, as that required a reason to live, and his reason, the Imperium, was a lie. As he explained that this would be a shallow victory, he was shot in the chest by Zenathi, who came up behind Drayden. Joren insisted that they should save him, but Zenathi told him that Adelvard had been gone for a long time. Zenathi told Joren that she admired his hope, but it was time for them to go.

In the Scepter ' s collapsing docking bay, Drayden and Zenathi ran out to meet the Corsair Wing – Dax Karrick had left Serenne to join the battle and rescue them. The two boarded the old freighter, and remarked that it finally, truly felt like this was over. The Scepter exploded, and with it, Adelvard's dreams of conquest, as the heroes flew off, victorious, into a new era.

#### War's end

> " He's beautiful , Elira--I'm so happy for you both...the three of you. " " How does it feel to be an uncle, Joren? Maybe that'll keep you close and stop your wandering. " ―Joren Drayden and Elira Solen discussing the birth of Beno

While the Battle of Ashden lingered on the desert world's surface, the Galactic Civil War came to an end on Serenne with the signing of the Galactic Concordance , a peace treaty between the Republic and the Imperium that forced the Imperium to remain within pre-established borders, though some of the remnants of the once-powerful military synth flux had disappeared into the Unknown Regions . . Drayden, along with many other notable individuals witnessed the signing of the Concordance between Mazan Veldon and Vela Sess overlooking Hanna City.

Following the signing of the treaty, Elira gave birth to her and Dax's son and Joren's nephew, Beno Karrick , on Serenne. Drayden visited the family shortly after Beno's birth where Elira hoped that Drayden being an uncle would keep him closer to home.

An Imperial officer stationed on an Iron Cruiser during the Battle of Ashden claimed to have seen the Aetheri Knight using Synth Flux and a flux blade to dispatch Star Destroyers in space, causing them to crash into the surface of the planet. When the Iron Cruiser crashed into the surface the officer said he was recovered by a figure who dragged him across the desert searching wreckage for other survivors. He also noted the man wore clothes similar to Joren Drayden and talked about being raised in another desert.

### Reconstruction

#### Freedom for the Nagai

Fenril pirate attacks

> " I—I don't know who they are…all I know is that they're pirates…hidden in the shadows…" "Where are they striking next? " ―The Gran merchant being interrogated by Joren Drayden

Around 6 AVE , a group of planets in the Fenril sector known as the Fenril Consortium became fiercely independent , unsatisfied with the Concordat's protection of their trading vessels and unwilling to cooperate with the new democracy. Just outside the Fenril sector, Drayden was flying with a squadron of Vectors to investigate the threat after an incident on a former Imperial space station over Maldran . A dogfight began between the Concordat's fighters and starships accompanied by a modified Imperial Iron Cruiser.

After losing some fighters, Drayden and fellow pilot Rynn Zenathi flew down to a nearby planet following a crashing pirate vessel. A gray-skinned man emerged with a vibroblade and charged at Joren and Rynn. Drayden fought the man, and knocked him down. Drawing Joren close, the pirate warned the Aetheri about who he's facing, and told him about a collector of Aetheri artifacts on the planet Gadrilam , who has a crown that will help Joren "learn the truth". He then told Joren to brace himself, as he detonated himself to cover his tracks, knocking Joren out. Awakening in a trance-like state, Drayden departed straight for Gadrilam, confusing Zenathi and taking off into hyperspace while telling her that he needed to investigate this alone.

Mission to Gadrilam

> " That pirate shared something before he died. Something I couldn't ignore. " ―Joren Drayden to Bolt-3 upon landing on Gadrilam

On the snowy world of Gadrilam, Joren apologized to Bolt-3 about the promptness and strangeness of his actions as he rushed off towards a village , thinking of what the pilot had told him about: a crown that would reveal the truth, held by Ibo Truddispal. Thinking back to his previous studies, Joren recalled the Crown of Verity , an artifact that, when worn, would cause the user to only be able to speak the absolute truth– but it was thought to be destroyed. In the village, Joren entered into a bar filled with patrons, many of whom were Nagai, just like the pirate. Joren entered and spoke with the bartender , Hossalana , who was dismissive of Joren asking about Truddispal. He was properly answered by the Nagai woman sitting next to him, Jesrit – who spotted that Joren was an Aetheri. Angry, Hossalana screamed that they should leave the bar, as Gallana Venk and her gang entered the establshment, letting Joren know that they'd found his ship, and know he's a Concordat pilot on some kind of mission. Jesrit tried to leave as Joren asked her about Truddispal, which made Venk's men grab him, wondering why Joren was looking for him. Responding with Synth Flux, Joren pushed them back, and took out his flux blade, to everyone's surprise.

Chaos ensued as the hostile Nagai charged at Drayden and a battle commenced, but Drayden quickly dispatched with his enemies, analyzing their body language and using their own tells against them. Eliminating them and incredibly determined, Drayden scared off Venk and the rest of her men, as Joren pleaded with Jesrit to stay and help him. She had called Truddispal a smuggler, which Joren had never said, meaning she knew him. Jesrit tried to explain herself as just a simple laborer, but Joren, sensing a darkness on Gadrilam, requested her help once more. The two then went into the nearby woods by torchlight, Jesrit guiding Joren to the tunnel where Truddispal was held. Drayden asked what Jesrit was doing on the barren world, to which she told him that she isn't there by choice, and was brought there with many of her people, the Nagai. Joren was confused, because Venk is also a Nagai, but Jesrit asserted that Venk is not truly one of them, and is a traitor to the followers of Garlev . Joren then told Jesrit his name, and tells her he wants to help, as it's what Aetheri do. As they arrived at the tunnel, Jesrit told Joren that her people are forced laborers on Gadrilam, and that they need help, if he wants to– and, ever the Aetheri, of course he does.

Sent into the tunnel alone by Jesrit, Joren though about how the pilot knew far too much for this to be a normal situation, unlike false artifacts Joren had been fooled by before. Going deeper, Joren overheard Venk and her men speaking about him around an injured Ibo Truddispal, and approached, flux blade lit. Venk tossed down the silver object she was holding– the Crown of Verity. Joren rushed forward and caught it, as Venk spoke ill Garlev's followers, frustrated at how prevalent they are. Venk then fired at Truddispal from point-blank range, killing the Aetheri relic collector. As her and her men left, she introduced Joren to members of the Zantarrk Gang , a group that had been terrorizing surrounding areas recently. Joren cut them down and ran away after one of them revealed that they took out the village, which he found to be true upon emerging from the tunnel's depths and seeing the fiery blaze.

Departing Serenne

> " The Pirrosi and their allies in the Fenril sector have made it clear—they want to disrupt our plans on every front. I refuse to sit back and let that happen. " ―Elira Solen to Vela Sess before departing for Kaltrais.

Back on the Concordat's capital, Serenne, Drayden and Zenathi stood amongst a collection of artifacts while pondering the Crown of Verity, and Joren expressed grief about the fate of the old Aetheri Order. Zenathi asked Drayden what the Crown does, which Joren explained is that it allows the wearer to discern truth from lies, while also preventing them from telling lies. He connected this idea to the idea of Aetheri identity, being part of a greater movement than just oneself. Then, his comms buzzed as Kith Alaytia was calling for help in a nearby docking bay. There, Vela Sess was actively trying to stop Elira Solen from leaving the planet on a diplomatic mission to Kaltrais , a world that was a valued member of the First Republic but had rejected the Concordat's offer to join. Elira insisted that the Prime Minister Kezrin Almovar 's Pirrosi forces and their Fenril Consortium allies wished to disrupt the Concordat's efforts there, and she wouldn't let that happen, as the Concordat already had enough trouble with the Consortium completely shuttering its borders to Concordat trade.
