Elira had a bad feeling about Kaltrais, as she'd spoken to President Tusvein Shoirin to arrange the meeting in-person, and he sounded "off". Drayden and Zenathi arrived to speak to Elira. Joren attempted to sway Elira into staying, but she was adamant about going, seeing Kaltrais as a great way to demonstrate that the Concordat cares about the planets that join it. Joren begged to accompany Elira, but his stalwart sister declined and boarded the transport with Alaytia, bound for Kaltrais.

Meanwhile, the Corsair Wing was back on Serenne as well, and Drayden and Zenathi met in its cockpit with Dax Karrick and Beilert Varrick, who had just returned from investigating the Zantarrk Gang, and found the Nagai Garlev dead on Aether Spire. Karrick was shocked at Elira departing for Kaltrais, and Varrick told Drayden that Garlev's killer was moving towards Kaltrais as well. Joren then began connecting the dots between the oppressed Nagai, the Zantarrk Gang, and the rising Fenril Consortium. He'd heard Garlev's name on his mission to Gadrilam, and knew that he was a freedom fighter. Joren told his friends about his investigations so far, saying that the Nagai have been part of the groups hitting their supply lines, which led to him discovering the Zantarrk Gang holding Nagai slaves. Karrick said that that sounded correct, and Drayden told him about Jesrit, the Nagai who mentioned Garlev. This confirmed that the Zantarrk were connected to both the supply line hits and Garlev's death, leading Drayden and the rest to depart for the Fenril sector immediately.

Fenril frenzy

> " Now what's the fun in that?" "Joren... the way this is all shaping up—it's gonna be anything but fun. " ―Joren Drayden and Dax Karrick over Kaltrais

On Kaltrais, Elira's meeting with Shoirin had quickly gone awry because of the Zantarrk Gang's presence on the planet, breaking out into a firefight on the rocky world. As Drayden and his allies arrived in the Corsair , they were surrounded by Fenril Consortium vessels. The modified ships fired on the Korrathi freighter, and it took lots of hits– until the antagonistic starships suddenly pulled back because of Shoirin's final act on Kaltrais. The world's defenses fired on grounded ships, and the attack was completely called off. This gave Solen and Alaytia an opening to escape in their shuttle into orbit, where they were contacted by the Consortium's true leader, the devious Reyna Oskure. The Anzati witch had been manipulating the situation from the beginning under the guise of being an "advisor". Drayden and the rest of the Corsair ' s occupants then received a transmission from Chancellor Sess, requesting that they go to the desert world Loradil to investigate a Concordat trading vessel that had been shot down by Fenril affiliated Zantarrk Gang vessels. Joren and the rest heard audio from the crash site– the Concordat traders were in distress and apparently surrounded.

Arriving on the sandy world, Drayden and his allies emerged from the Corsair as he pointed out a crashed Dissident vessel, a Providence -class carrier . There was blood on the ground near an opening into the carrier's insides, and the heroes entered, searching for the merchant crew. Its systems were not fully down, and Zenathi was able to reengage power, bringing lights on and allowing the crew to explore more effectively. But as they continued, a group of B1 battle droids led by a tactical droid stopped them in their path, as even more appeared behind them. Joren ignited his flux blade, and a battle started against the former Dissidents that continued a long-ended war. During the chaos, Zenathi was grabbed by droids and dragged away, and Drayden followed her, as Karrick and Varrick destroyed the tactical droid. While Joren followed Zenathi, the pilot took down her battle droid captors and escaped out into the sand, running towards the wreck of the Nechrim's Wake . Back inside, Drayden reunited with Karrick and Varrick, now fighting a droideka , super battle droid , and several commando droids . The Aetheri made quick work of them all, as the other two descended further into the carrier to look for anything that could help the fight. As Drayden joined them, Karrick found a pile of Phase II replica trooper armor, which they equipped, fighting their way outside into a huge field of not only battle droids, but Armored Assault Tanks , concerned about saving Rynn Zenathi and reflecting on the armor's storied history.

Drayden, Varrick, and Karrick fought countless security droids in their armor, but after Karrick destroyed a group of droids surrounding him, he and Varrick lost track of Joren. The Aetheri was out of sight, hiding under an AAT, calling upon Orien Kade and Zho for help in the battle. The droids were catching on, though, and began to search under the tank– but Drayden wasn't there! Suddenly, the inspecting droid was thrown upward into the tank's front, flying up to the tank's pilot, destroying them both. Joren used Synth Flux to destroy the rest of them, and after eliminating the final droid with his flux blade, headed forwards toward the Nechrim's Wake . Inside the Wake , Zenathi had been saved by its captain, Gauvis , and the Kessith Finch . Then, a third figure emerged from the shadows– it was another Nagai named Marntiv , a member of the Nagai insurgency , who was being transported on the Wake . The man revealed that he was the reason the Zantarrk Gang attacked the convoy, the supplies gained just being a bonus. They aligned themselves with Reyna Oskure and the Fenril Consortium for wealth and power, and are trying to squash the Nagai insurgency while using Nagai slaves as troops. Trying to get to Rynn and the Wake , Joren lept into battle as his father did before him, destroying countless droids, while reminding himself that he must avoid the traps and temptations that got his father. As Karrick ran towards the Corsair , he was knocked down by an onslaught of droids, and as they closed in on him, the entire crew, Drayden, Zenathi, Varrick, Gauvis, Finch, and Marntiv, destroyed the droids and rescued him. Dax immediately suggested they head back to Serenne, but Joren let him know that the Nagai were rising up on Kaltrais , a Concordat agent was present, and it was the Nagai's only chance at freedom. The group, having survived the Dissident onslaught, then left for Kaltrais.

Uprising at Kaltrais

> " ...You're jere...you can help..." "I'm an Aetheri, remember? It's what we do, Jesrit. " ―Jesrit and Joren Drayden, after meeting again during the Nagai insurrection on Kaltrais

Soon after, the Corsair Wing arrived over Kaltrais, but didn't receive any communications from the surface– something was wrong. And as the freighter descended further, towards a central city, volt pistol fire could be seen from the sky. As the entire Concordat team exited the Corsair , Joren Drayden ran forward,  shocked to see the Nagai he met on Gadrilam, Jesrit, alive and leading the insurrection in the plaza! Amidst the chaos, and a reunion between Jesrit and Marntiv, Joren called out to Jesrit, and the two fought the pirate troopers together. They took out several as Joren explained that he's an Aetheri, and that means coming back to help. He sadly informed her of Garlev's death, and the two kept fighting the oppressors, many of which were Nagai themselves, enslaved by the Zantarrk or promised reward. They were then interrupted by Karrick and the rest, Dax informing Joren that Rynn Zenathi was not doing okay, and was feeling weak. Meanwhile, Concordat agent Bastion was in place infiltrating the Zantarrk's base of operations, and entered Prime Minister Almovar's chamber, where the regal woman stood surrounded by holograms. Alarmed, Almovar immediately questioned who Bastion was, as Drayden, Zenathi, and Jesrit caught up and entered the room as well.

Finally speaking to someone responsible for the continued oppression of the Nagai, Drayden tried to rationalize with the fierce leader, as he and Jesrit told her that her actions were putting countless people–including her own–in danger. Almovar said she was doing everything for her home, but Joren and Rynn reminded her that her will doesn't exactly line up with what the people of Pirros really want. Kaltrais is in shambles, the people of Pirros don't want war, and she's been manipulated by Reyna Oskure. Jesrit was furious at the Prime Minister, seeing Nagai blood on her hands as she'd worked with Gallana Venk and used Nagai slaves. A bit resigned, Almovar then admitted that war was already here, that the Fenril Consortium had permanently allied itself with Oskure and the Zantarrk, and that it was too late to stop it all. Bastion refuted this, and Joren extended a hand of grace to the misguided woman, but Almovar was still concerned about Nagai retribution. At this, Jesrit told her that the Nagai insurgency's two goals were the freedom of the homeworld Nagi , and the deaths of Venk and Oskure, as Joren rebuked her for this retributive anger and hate.

Joren then testified of his experience with the Zantarrk, as they'd built a slave army and killed many, and then spoke of Oskure's Imperial past. Accepting that she had indeed failed her people, Prime Minister Almovar then, finally, declared that she would be withdrawing her fleet, as the Nagai insurrection's explosions outside rocked the building. Almovar dismissed the group and said that she had much to repair and ponder, as Rynn promised that the Concordat would be there to help deal with it. As the group left, quietly, Almovar said her fate was sealed, as a pair of white eyes opened in the shadows behind her, Reyna Oskure killing the Pirrosi leader. Outsiude, while Zenathi grew weaker, Joren Drayden helped her as the group fought their way towards a major hangar. Joren tried asking what was wrong, but Rynn refused to answer. The Nagai were winning, fighting their way onto and stealing ships, as the Zantarrk fell one trooper after the last. Joren and Rynn held the line while more Nagai escaped, until the two were picked up by the Corsair , which departed into space towards Nagi to win this revolution once and for all.

Liberation of Nagi

> " We've got to locate Venk and Oskure . If we take them down, the Fenril Consortium will be finished. " " Easier said than done, kid. " ―Joren Drayden and Dax Karrick on the surface of Nagi.

As the Corsair and its Nagai allies arrived over Nagi, they were confronted with a Fenril Iron Cruiser and lots of Zantarrk Gang vessels. The Fenril fleet fired on the freighter and other insurgent ships as Dax Karrick performed a spin technique to batter away some fighters, while sending the Corsair down to the surface. The freighter crashed into a crowd of Zantarrk pirates, taking them out as the smoke rose from the vessel. The heroes emerged, and Rynn's condition was worsening while Drayden helped her stand. But then a convoy of Nagai insurgents on landspeeders and speeder bikes appeared, one member saying that it was prophesized that Jesrit would arrive. Jesrit set their goals straight and introduced her new allies to the recently arrived Nagai. The group then decided to split up, Joren, Rynn, Jesrit, and Marntiv going with the Nagai to find medical aid for Zenathi, while Karrick, Varrick, Gauvis, Finch, and Bastion set out to find and eliminate Venk and Oskure as quickly as they could. Drayden and his allies were on a Nagai convoy as Zenathi's illness quickly became all-consuming, as if her life is being drained. Jesrit promised that they wouldn't let Rynn die, as a group of Tof emerged in front of them and blew up their transports. As Drayden woke up, he saw that Marntiv was killed in the blast, and that two of the large men were taking Jesrit and Rynn with them. The Tof departed on speeder bikes while claiming the Nagai insurgency was destined for death– with Drayden left for dead in the barren wastes of Nagi.

Wandering the wastes injured and reflecting on what and who he'd lost, Joren Drayden fainted from exhaustion, but awoke to the Nagai Tuula , a friend of Jesrit's and the daughter of Garlev. Tuula says that if Oskure kills Jesrit, the hope of the Nagai insurgency dies with her. She helps Joren up, and the two set off to find their friends, as Tuula used her tracking skills to guide the two towards Oskure. In the capital city of Siva , Oskure was escorting the captured Zenathi and Jesrit to a shuttle, guarded by Zantarrk shock troopers, while her fleet gathered in orbit. Suddenly, a Concordat fleet, led by Alaytia, consisting of a Defender -class cruiser , at least two escort frigates , several corvettes, and many starfighters arrived to confront the Consortium. But as the Anzati witch boasted, Drayden, Karrick, Varrick, and Tuula, reunited, fired on Oskure. A new battle had begun as the shock troopers unleashed a volley of fire at the Concordat heroes, who had the high ground. Joren noticed Oskure draining more of Rynn's life synth flux from above, and slashed shock troopers with his flux blade as he dove down to face the Anzati. She'd been waiting for this, and accepted the young Aetheri's challenge. The witch dove in with her claws out, slicing Joren and seeking to feast on his life synth flux. She knocked his flux blade out of his hand, and asked him if he'd made peace with his past and future. He then said that he'd made peace with who he was, and that his past is the Aetheri's past, and that was also his future. Using Synth Flux, Joren held Oskure back, pushing her to the wall as he held her still and approached her.

Joren then put the Crown of Verity on Reyna's head, causing her to scream as she was now only able to speak the absolute truth. Just before she began to speak, Oskure secretly pressed a button on a remote behind her back, and told Joren that if he wanted the "truth", that she, a creature of lies and shadows, would reveal it. She confessed to forcing the Nagai peasants to haphazardly assemble a fleet made of Imperial scraps, but in a way that would specifically ensure victory for herself. To Joren's horror, Oskure then said that the truth would set her free, and prove to be his final resting place. Then, to the shock of Stannix in orbit, the fleet above Nagi suddenly detonated, causing massive explosions and scaring Concordat pilots. Nagi had now become a death trap. Concordat pilots in orbit dodged and fought to eliminate debris from the detonated fleet, some of it managing to fall to the surface, threatening the Nagai. There, the standoff between Drayden and Oskure ended with the Anzati throwing off the Crown of Verity as wreckage from the fleet rained and Drayden used his flux blade and Synth Flux to redirect falling debris.

On the wastes of Nagi, the final battle for Nagi had begun as Oskure's forces, consisting of Tof soldiers, pirates, and Zantarrk shock troopers faced off with Drayden and his allies. The heroes fought fiercely, as Joren helped Rynn Zenathi to limp away, her condition worsening even more. Drayden wanted to help her, but she begged him to stop Oskure and end the fighting first. While Drayden deflected a sudden barrage of volt pistol fire, Zenathi crawled on the ground to grab the Crown of Verity as Oskure approached her ship to escape. Then, while the battle raged, a Concordat transport accompanied by two Vectors landed– and Vela Sess and Elira Solen emerged to announce that the Concordat stood with the Nagai– the Pirrosi delegation had surrendered. The fighting ceased upon this declaration, the combatants looking at the arriving dignitaries. Jesrit took this opportunity to address the crowd– saying that they now stood on the precipice of true peace, a free Nagi, and freedom from mainpulators, monsters, and their lackeys. Elira denounced the Fenril Consortium as a shadow government run by Oskure, stated that the Concordat stands with Nagi as a free, independent world they will help rebuild, and the enemy Nagai removed their salvaged Imperial trooper helmets, cheering the phrase "in Garlev's name", freed. Close by, the heroes cornered the escaping Oskure, Drayden telling her that she had much to answer for, attacking the vampiric witch. Oskure was able to knock Drayden down while her guards were killed, but Zenathi emerged from behind, placing the Crown of Verity this time on her own head.

Rynn's eyes glowed, and she explained that the Crown allows the wearer to not only see the truth, but all that they've left behind. Grabbing Oskure, Zenathi told the ancient Anzati that she'd taken something from her, a piece of her that's helped her destroy so many lives in the Fenril sector. Oskure mocked Zenathi's spice runner background, but the pilot continued, telling Oskure that she'd left something behind inside of her, the sorceress's skin growing more and more decrepit as the rapidly aging woman begged to be left alone. This began to restore Zenathi, who became somewhat mad with power, taunting the dying witch– to Drayden's dismay. He begged her to stop, to not lash out for revenge, and it was Karrick's hand on Zenathi's shoulder that finally stopped her, as he reminded her that she had won, and shouldn't drag herself down with Oskure. The crumpled, old body of Reyna Oskure then fell to the ground, barely clinging to life, as Rynn removed the Crown.

Some time later, Rynn asked Joren if it was really over, to which the Aetheri said that it was, for now– but there was always a potential challenge waiting around the corner. In the city of Siva, the Concordat heroes celebrated and gathered with the Nagai, as they began clearing debris and rebuilding. Jesrit thanked Joren and his friends, and Joren clarified that he was just keeping his promise from Gadrilam. Zenathi saw this moment of reconstruction, celebration, and all the rest of it, and reflected on the moment of happiness with Joren, who told her that these moments were incredibly important. The Aetheri said these moments were what keep one moving through the dark times, even when the fight feels endless: moments of peace and happiness like this give one life.

#### Foundations of an order

> " I had great hope for the future of the Aetheri. I came to believe that if a single Aetheri could help bring balance back to Synth Flux, then perhaps a restored Aetheri Order could maintain peace and prevent the umbra side from ever regaining its hold on the galaxy. So I scoured worlds for the remaining Aetheri texts and gathered a new class of apprentices under my tutelage. " ―Joren Drayden

Just as he promised Farnay near the start of his own Aetheri studies, in 5 AVE, Drayden set about rebuilding the Aetheri Order. Despite his optimism, he had many unanswered questions and was hesitant to take on another apprentice after his sister Elira, unsure of his grounding in Aetheri lore. Joren became a caretaker of the Aetheri Order's legacy, and largely disappeared from galactic view and dedicated himself to traveling the galaxy in hopes of finding what remained of the Aetheri after the Imperium's long efforts to eradicate their history. Through his quest, he came to understand much about himself and his destiny and gained the confidence he needed to rebuild the Aetheri Order. As he traveled the galaxy, he also searched for synth flux-sensitives who could possibly become his students. During this long and difficult task, Drayden was aided by Lor Saan Tarka , an explorer and member of the Church of Synth Flux who, though not Synth Flux-sensitive himself, believed in the Aetheri ideals. Saan Tarka was old enough to remember the Aetheri Knights before the Replica Wars, and he did not believe the Emperor's lies about how the Aetheri betrayed the First Republic. Saan Tarka, who believed that the Return of the Aetheri Order was essential to bring balance to Synth Flux, became a valuable ally for both Drayden and Solen.

Gatalenta was one of the first destinations in Drayden's search, and he also traveled to Moraband, the ancient home of the Umbra, to investigate anything he could about Synth Flux. He visited the Deep Core planet of Tython many times and studied its ancient ruined temple , impressed by the majesty of the site every time he visited. He scoured many worlds and assembled a collection of eight ancient bound books and scrolls that described Aetheri tenets, history and guidance for those following the path of the Aetheri— sacred Aetheri texts . One of the books was the Rammahgon , an influential work that was thought destroyed over 5,000 years ago but retrieved by Drayden while he explored the subterranean ruins of Ossus . The Aionomica was another, a two-volume collection of Aetheri explorations and codifications of Synth Flux, part codex, correspondence and scrapbook. He also found a book that was a catalog of the Eeshaypher Collection , a forbidden gallery of artifacts related to the umbra side of Synth Flux. At one point, Drayden journeyed to Black Spire Outpost on Batuu , searching for ancient Aetheri texts and artifacts.

Drayden dedicated much of these years to finding the site of the first Aetheri Sanctum . He uncovered many tantalizing clues that hinted at the origins of the Aetheri, but the precise location was a mystery. One key to unravelling the mystery was his study of the spread of the Synth Flux-sensitive uneti saplings. He also made use of the Aetheri star compass he had discovered in the Emperor's storehouse on Pillio. With a plasma -encased supraluminite lodestone at its center, the compass functioned much like the ancient wayfinders and was attuned to specific points in hyperspace. Through studying the compass, Drayden uncovered a path to Tidehollow the site of Temple Island and the first Aetheri Sanctum. With the help of Lor Saan Tarka, he finally put together the many clues and learned the location of the temple, a discovery that he and Saan Tarka kept a secret.

With Saan Tarka, Drayden hunted down relics synth flux across the stars. Following the Aetheri compass he recovered on Pillio, the pair mapped out the network of Aetheri temples scattered across the galaxy, many of which were forgotten after the end of the Aetheri Purge. Their voyages proved fruitful most of the time, Drayden amassing a massive collection of tomes, texts, scrolls, data cards, ritual items, sigils, and other antique symbols. Besides the Aetheri star compass, Drayden also acquired flux blades. All of this, the pair of men researched, gaining even deeper understanding of the old Aetheri Order and its teachings.

Around 6 AVE , in his explorations of Aetheri lore, Drayden discovered the cache of Aetheri Chief Librarian Jocasta Nura and recovered her reliquaries, which contained a wealth of Aetheri knowledge and history. He uncovered many lost Aetheri and Synth Flux relics throughout the galaxy, including an Aetheri Crusader pendant and a reliquary belong to Aetheri Master Avar Kriss of the High Republic era.

Drayden came to learn about some of the Aetheri Masters that existed before the fall of the Order, such as Nura, Drevan Koss , and Sorin Vael . He became versed in the Synthcells , the Aetheri Code , and the various Aetheri temples that existed, like the one on Lorath . As well as the light, Drayden also learned about the Umbra side. He studied the history of the Umbra, including the creation of Vael Vorek's Rule of Two and events like the Vrak of Searmark. He learned of several Umbra Lords and umbra side warriors: Vael Vorek, Vael Vreth, Vael Varek , and the Aetheri hunters of the Imperium's Warden Order among them. Along with Fortress Velgor , his father's castle on Emberfall, he became familiar with several locations and temples strong with the Umbra side.

Another relic Drayden recovered was a page of the Journal of Caleb Dume , a survivor of the Aetheri Purge and early insurgent who fought the Imperium under a new identity, Brenn Aldric, and eventuallly trained his own apprentice, Cass Virel . He brought this and the other Aetheri artifacts he had uncovered to Tidehollow and compiled his knowledge into a book he called The Secrets of the Aetheri . Tidehollow was also where Drayden collected the Rammahgon , the Aionomica , the Chronicles of Brus-bu , Poetics of an Aetheri , and the other sacred Aetheri texts into a small library housed in an uneti tree .

#### Rescue of Pip

> " He is strong with Synth Flux, but talent without training is nothing. I will give my life to protect the Child… but he will not be safe until he masters his abilities. " ―Joren Drayden, to Dinna Verrick

In approximately 9 AVE , a Synth Flux-sensitive member of Zho's species named Pip reached out in Synth Flux from the seeing stone on Tython. Pip had been raised at the Aetheri Sanctum on Vantor before the Great Aetheri Purge but had not yet been fully trained as an Aetheri, and had since become a foundling cared for by the Ferrik bounty hunter Dinna Verrick , whom had been tasked with taking Pip to someone who could train him in the use of Synth Flux.

Shortly afterwards, Pip was taken by an Imperial remnant . Feeling a presence in Synth Flux, Drayden tracked Pip to a light cruiser that Verrick and his allies had boarded in their own efforts to rescue him. Drayden boarded the vessel with Bolt-3 and defeated a platoon of third-generation dark troopers that had trapped Pip and Verrick's team on the ship's bridge. Afterwards, Drayden offered his hand to Pip, and, noting the child's strong attachment to Verrick, told Verrick that he would take care of him if Verrick allowed it. Verrick relinquished Pip to Drayden to be trained in the ways of Synth Flux and Drayden left the ship with the child.

#### Building an academy

> " You've taught him well. " " It's more like he's remembering than I'm actually teaching him anything. " " Sometimes the student guides the master. " [...]" Sometimes I wonder if his heart is in it [...] What should I do about him? " " Trust your instincts. " ―Joren Drayden and Sela Marr on Pip's training

Fulfilling the dying wish of Zho, Drayden set out to begin training a new generation of Aetheri. Having rescued and brought in an original Aetheri Adept from the twilight years of the Vantor Republic, Drayden ventured to Ossus with the young Pip to build the first Aetheri Academy after the Aetheri Order's near extinction following Directive 66. Possessing the wisdom and teachings of his own masters, Drayden tried to educate his young student through the ways of Synth Flux. Telling tales of Master Zho onto Pip, the young Aetheri Master passed on what he had learned, echoing Zho's words "size matters not" and to do, not try. However, Drayden strived to make impression on his small student, having to stop him from using Synth Flux to eat creatures and let Pip use Synth Flux as he did. Knowing that knowledge lied in Pip's memory, Joren offered to help him remember and placed his hands gently on the alien's head. Seeing the traumatic images of the 501st Legion gunning down three Aetheri Knights protecting the Adept, everything faded to black when the replicas killed the Aetheri and moved towards Pip. When his student awoke, Joren greeted his return and began to better understand the turmoil and subsequent reluctance of Pip towards his lessons.

While carrying Pip on his back as he had done with Zho on Mirefall, Drayden felt the presence of the Ferrik, Dinna Verrick. After a time, Pip seemed to master balance and studied in awe as Joren practiced his skills with the flux blade. Eventually, he put the young Pip's reflexes to test on a training remote, which the young Adept managed to dodge and destroy with ease. Sela Marr approached Drayden and complimented him on his progress with Pip. The doubtful Aetheri expressed his concerns that Pip's progress was due to him gradually remembering his training on Vantor, not necessarily his training so far with him. Sela stated that sometimes the student teaches the master. Drayden noted Verrick's presence and Marr gave him a present meant for Pip. Not sure whether Pip had heart for the way of the Aetheri, Drayden shared his thoughts. Fortunately, Marr knew the same mind of Drayden's father, seeing that same compassionate heart which she felt Kael had for her and mentioned Joren's alikeness. Drayden asked what should be done and Marr told him to trust his instincts, wisdom she learned from her master. She then departed the planet and said the two would perhaps meet again and told Synth Flux be with him.

Heeding the advice of Marr, Drayden decided to offer Pip a final choice. In the stone hut, Drayden presented Pip with Dinna Verrick's gift as well as the flux blade of Master Zho, which Drayden had kept. The first path was to accept the present from Verrick and be returned to him, however, by choosing attachment he would forsake the Aetheri way. The second was to pick up Zho's flux blade and pledge himself to Joren's teachings to become his first Aetheri Adept. He told Pip that to become an Aetheri would take years and hard work, though at the end he could be a great Aetheri. He then let Pip choose.

Pip would choose the armor, thus leading Drayden to put him in his Vector starfighter and instruct Bolt-3 to pilot him to Dunemere to be reunited with Verrick. Despite their time together being brief, Drayden's guidance had a significant impact on Pip. After being asked about Pip's impressive use of Synth Flux by fellow Ferrik Bo-Katin Velara , Verrick noted that his abilities were a result of Drayden's training.

Some time after his meeting with Sela Marr, the Nightsister Morgan Elveth recruited former Aetheri Knight of the old Aetheri Order Baylan Skoll , who was aware of the truth behind Drayden's father, to help locate Imperial Grand Admiral Vorren . As Drayden had become prominent during the Galactic Civil War, Skoll became aware of his existence, and kept his name, alongside the names of other Insurgent heroes, listed in a device on his gauntlet during the search for the planet Peridea in a distant galaxy .

#### Recruiting his nephew

> " He's an amazing teacher. Very strong. I've learned so much from him… " ―Beno Karrick, on his uncle

Though reluctant to take on another apprentice at first, when Synth Flux awakened in Drayden's nephew Beno Karrick and his mother Elira could see the umbra side taking hold of her son, she entrusted the boy to her brother to help him find balance. He agreed to train the 10-year-old Karrick, in 15 AVE . Drayden took on the responsibilities of an Aetheri Master by teaching his nephew. After Beno's fall to the umbra side however, Joren would say that he was merely playing the role of Aetheri Master as it was a title he had not formally earned.

Drayden taught Karrick the ways of the Aetheri, in its many forms, under the tutelage and impressed on his pupil the importance of practicing discipline and control. Karrick gave his best efforts to gain his Master's approval, although at times their familial bond caused him to address Drayden as uncle instead of Master. Though he loved his nephew, Drayden had committed his life to reestablishing the Aetheri Order's teachings, which had virtually nothing to do with family ties; therefore, he taught Karrick that detachment and distance were necessary to gaining the pure focus that every Aetheri aspired to achieve.

His nephew soon joined in exploring the galaxy for ancient Aetheri lore, and together they found reliquaries from forgotten eras and ancient Aetheri texts with arcane writings. The two shared more than the same Drayden blood, for like him, Beno gained a strong devotion to the ways of the Aetheri Order. Drayden saw his nephew's raw power and believed he could pass on his strengths to him, and he attempted to manage his progress through Synth Flux by having him exercise a level of restraint. Karrick was his strongest and most prized student, and Drayden went on to place his hope for the Aetheri Order's future within his nephew, and despite knowing of the darkness within his pupil, the Aetheri Master believed he was strong enough to keep it in check.

Drayden was a kind master, and Karrick considered his uncle to be a strong and amazing teacher, but Drayden made mistakes that culminated over time, protecting Beno from the dark truths of their family, much like his sister did before him, and inadvertently isolated Beno over the years of his training. At the request of his sister, Joren never provided his nephew with the details of Kael Drayden's fall to the umbra side or Xarn Velgor's redemption and return to the light.

During his training of his nephew, a powerful umbra side-practitioner known as Vorr , who was the Supreme Leader of a splinter group of the Imperium known as the New Imperium , and also secretly a puppet of the resurrected Theron Mal, began influencing Beno from afar, clouding him with the umbra side. At some point Drayden had encountered Vorr and supposedly damaged him, and Vorr was responsible for indirectly inspiring Joren to rebuild the Aetheri Order, having tried to shatter his resolve.

#### More apprentices

> " We all studied together. I was first, but they came not long after. " ―Beno Karrick, on Joren's training other apprentices

Around 16 AVE , Drayden gathered disciples, young synth flux-sensitive beings adrift with no way to harness their abilities as a result the Aetheri Purge, who went on to be his first students. Drayden's new students numbered about a dozen besides his nephew, including students Voe , Tai , and Hennix , and he worked to teach them everything he had learned in his own training and about the light side of Synth Flux per Zho's command.

Drayden hoped that teaching the students in a real Aetheri Sanctum would offer them a more traditional Aetheri path than his own training under Orien Kade and Zho. He kept temple's location on Ossus a secret known only to the members of the Order and his loyal droid Bolt-3. Drayden stored the recovered Aetheri knowledge and relics that he and Lor Saan Tarka had recovered within the Aetheri Sanctum, a decision which Karrick disagreed, which he believed was just locked up and never used.

With the small gathering of his new students looking to him as a spiritual guide and mentor for the Aetheri arts, Drayden believed he could usher in a new era of enlightenment. With his attention now split between several other apprentices, Drayden's nephew Beno Karrick's focus was drawn away to dark Synth Flux knowledge. Teaching several Aetheri techniques and traditions as telekinesis, meditation, and the use and construction of flux blades , Drayden's approach for guiding the young minds under his care was steeped in his own master Zho's teachings passed on while he was on Mirefall, so would often be preaching about patience and balance.

While also instilling the belief that all beings were equal within Synth Flux, he gave lectures outside his temple, passing on ever more experience from training with Zho on Mirefall as he taught the levitation of rocks through Synth Flux. During one such event, student Voe asked why Beno Karrick was stronger, where Drayden compared Synth Flux to a door, that it would become easier to use the more she opened herself up to it. The Aetheri Master also tutored Hennix in opening reliquaries through Synth Flux, reminding the young Quarren to listen to the unique voices of each reliquary, for each one was different based on their teachings.

Drayden desired heavily to preserve the teachings of the Aetheri Order, laboring to pass on the best of what Zho and Orien taught him as Aetheri mentors at his Aetheri Sanctum and to instill the core beliefs they embodied within the next generation. Additionally, Joren understood what Zho experienced with himself on Mirefall as teacher, having the same unique frustrations with his own pupils; a master guiding a young unseasoned student that was convinced they were wasting time on pointless exercises, such as stacking stones. At some point during his time as an Aetheri Master, Drayden would face a group of IG-RM assassin droids alongside his nephew.

#### Encountering the Knights of Karrick

> " Well, hello there. Everything here is ours. We're going to take it…but how we take it is up to you. Your call, friends. " " You're wrong. So, so wrong. This is an Aetheri outpost . Everything in it belongs to the Aetheri Order, which, at the moment, means me. I am the last Aetheri. " ―Karrick and Drayden

In 19 AVE , the year his nephew turned fourteen, Drayden, Beno, and Lor Saan Tarka traveled to an Aetheri outpost on Elphrona , a location from the days of the High Republic Era, hoping to find Aetheri artifacts. Joren flew the ship and conversed with Saan Tarka, while Karrick, without his master's knowledge, was communing with Vorr. Drayden was initially skeptical of an Aetheri presence so far from the core, but Saan Tarka assured him that with his expertise he had recognized the description from his contact as an Aetheri Outpost, and explained that the Aetheri's activity was greatly expanded throughout the galaxy during the High Republic Era. Meanwhile, Karrick telepathically communicated with Vorr, expressing that he was tired of missions to find artifacts with Drayden that he would just lock away in the temple and never use. Karrick admitted that Drayden was an amazing teacher, but was frustrated that he never wanted Karrick to use any of the things he had taught him, which Vorr claimed was because Drayden viewed Karrick as a child and had not realized his true capabilities. Drayden then told Karrick that they would possibly find insights into the old Aetheri, and added that they could find interesting weapons, as he knew that Karrick was interested in such subjects, which Saan Tarka affirmed. Drayden told Karrick he was glad he came along, and Karrick said he shared the sentiment.

When they landed, Drayden was impressed by the sight of the outpost. He cautioned his apprentice to careful and warned that such a place was likely to be rigged with traps, recalling a time he found a flux blade in a similar place that turned out to be a trap and nearly killed him. Inside, they found a treasure trove that Drayden judged so impressive he thought it may be better than Jocasta Nura's cache, including many Aetheri reliquaries and a flux blade rifle . Drayden was excited by the possibility of learning many new things. As the three perused the outpost, instantly, both Drayden and Karrick felt a cold presence within the structure. They were then confronted by a group of umbra side warriors known as the Knights of Karrick revealed themselves.

Their master, " Karrick ," declared themselves, and claimed ownership of all within the outpost and that they way they took it was up to Drayden. Drayden countered, shooting back that all within the outpost, due it being an Aetheri location, belonged to the Order; all the reliquaries and relics belonged to Joren, for he was the last Aetheri Knight even though he would not be for long. Drayden told the Knights he could sense the umbra side in them and would never let them have the artifacts. When Karrick insulted him, Karrick stepped forward and declared that his master was a legend. Drayden ignited his blade and instructed Saan Tarka to hang back and Karrick to protect Saan Tarka, while handled the threat, assuring Karrick he could do it even thought there were seven of them. Karrick tried to taunt him, but Drayden just taunted him back, angering him and moving him to order the Knights to attack. As Karrick watched on and called out to him, Drayden engaged the knights, not intending to kill any of them, and managed to overpower them quite easily despite their numbers, which he explained as him not being alone because he had the powerful ally that was Synth Flux by his side. He criticized them for their crude use of Synth Flux, saying they wielded the umbra side like a hammer, contrasted by his blade-like precision with the light side. He eventually used Synth Flux to destroy the Knights' weapons and proceeded to prepare himself for a duel with Karrick , who wielded a red flux blade . Karrick informed Drayden that he activated a kill switch on his flux blade that would detonate the room if it left his hand. Drayden warned that move might not go the way Karrick expected, which Karrick agreed with. Realizing that Drayden's power was far superior to their own, Karrick withdrew his knights and left the planet. However, before he left, Karrick removed his helmet and warned Drayden about the strong presence of the umbra side within Karrick, before offering the boy chance, if he chose, to seek them out if decided he wanted another path.

Ultimately, the structure proved to be a source of more knowledge for Drayden, for he recovered the reliquaries and other relics inside, leaving the outpost empty, but his nephew was prompted with the umbra side of Synth Flux.

### Quest for Umbrale

#### Visions and voices

> " But this…place, whatever it is. I'm seeing it for a reason, Lor. I can feel it. " ―Joren Drayden to Lor Saan Tarka on his visions

As his first disciples grew older and new younglings came into the New Aetheri Order's care, Drayden had a Velari named Enyo help him as one of the younglings' instructors.

In 21 AVE , Drayden started feeling an increasing darkness in Synth Flux and having dark visions that frequently came to him in meditation. One day, Lor Saan Tarka, who was the only person Drayden told about the visions, visited Drayden in his temple on Ossus, his old friend's arrival heralded to Drayden by Beno Karrick. Saan Tarka greeted Drayden and Karrick, but once the latter was gone, asked Drayden about the visions and whether or not they had become stronger or changed. Drayden asked Saan Tarka to help him understand the visions and learn what the place in them was by standing vigil over him while he accessed the vision and described it. Saan Tarka was hesitant, but agreed, and Drayden thus began the meditation.

Much quicker than his other meditations, Drayden found himself in an empty dark void but felt an all-consuming presence within it which he had only thought he had sensed before. Then the vision shifted and he found himself standing on the ancient hidden Umbra world of Umbrale , though he did not know that was what he was seeing. Eventually the vision broke, and Drayden explained to Saan Tarka what he had seen. Saan Tarka wondered if the vision was just a general representation of lingering darkness or an influence from an artifact, but Drayden still believed he was having the vision for a reason. Drayden asked Saan Tarka the reason he had come to Ossus in the first place, and he explained that there was an archaeological dig on the planet Yoturba that he wanted to check out with Drayden, convincing him to leave the temple by saying that it would be good for Karrick to learn responsibilities while he was gone.

#### Yoturba

> " I saw… a place. Darkness, and lightning. It was so dry , so old. And…There was something there. A…person. No. A shape. Like a…a shadow. It was…on me, I could feel it, I could hear it speaking to me. " ―Mico Haswell to Joren Drayden, after being influenced by a kyber crystal bled with the umbra side of Synth Flux

Drayden and Saan Tarka arrived at the archeological expedition on Yorturba that was being conducted by the Lerct Historical Institute and were greeted by archaeologist Dr. Beaumont Kin , who informed them that the dig site was disappointing and they had not found an Aetheri Sanctum. Drayden still carried a feeling that he was there for a reason despite this, and he would be proven correct when an archaeologist named Tee Mairi discovered an Umbra reliquary at the site. Drayden recognized it immediately, and Saan Tarka recognized the writing on it as the Umbra's mirrored Aurebesh . Drayden still was not convinced that it was what he had sensed there, and would be again proved correct when he heard screaming echoing throughout the site. Archeologist Mico Haswell had discovered an Umbra kyber crystal and had his mind influenced by it, causing him to uncontrollably lash out and cause destruction with Synth Flux. Joren restrained him with Synth Flux, but in reaching out to him once again had a vision of Umbrale that Haswell was now present in. Haswell leapt toward Drayden in the vison and moved in a way that Drayden recognized to be inhuman and even reptilian. Then Drayden was on Yoturba again and managed to restrain the man with Synth Flux again, calling a team of medics to sedate him via an injection.

Haswell was once again in control of himself, and Drayden questioned him about what happened, though the man was not able to remember much. He did tell Drayden that he saw a dry, dark place filled with lightning and felt a shadow-like sentient presence there. Once Haswell passed out, Drayden discovered the bled kyber crystal in his hand, and confiscated it. He told Saan Tarka to tell the others to shut down the dig at least temporarily, and then headed to the section of the site where Haswell had been excavating. He discovered that the chamber was actually a crashed ship, and discovered more bled kyber crystals within it, that like the one Haswell had been holding, were the wrong size and shape for a flux blade. This and his experience on the planet confirmed for Drayden that something dark and evil was coming, deciding to leave Saan Tarka there to look over the dig site while he went to Tython in search of answers.

#### Ghosts of the past

> " Remember, my son, no matter how dark the night may grow, you are never alone. You will know what to do, my son. Let Synth Flux guide you. Let it flow through you as it flows through me. Use that power, and your instincts, for they are one and the same. " ―Kael Drayden, to Joren Drayden

Drayden traveled to Tython and visited the ruined Aetheri Sanctum known as Synth Flux-henge, where he sat on the seeing stone and began to study the Umbra Reliquary and the kyber crystals and perform research through his many ancient tomes and personal notes. In a book that was a catalog of the Eeshaypher Collection, Drayden discovered an entry about an Umbra wayfinder , which he found remarkably similar in appearance and utility to the Umbra reliquary. The book stated that the wayfinders contained navigational data to an ancient Umbra world called Ixigul—or in Drayden's translation: Umbrale. Drayden gleaned from both the catalog and his other research that Umbrale was meant to be a place of power and eternal life, and that its secret was so important that only two Umbra wayfinders were ever made. He theorized that the crashed ship from Yoturba had been from someone attempting to use the reliquary and the kyber crystals to navigate to Umbrale, and thus decided to use the power of the seeing stone to meditate while reaching out to the crystals and reliquary to see what he could discover.

During his meditation, Drayden once again had a vision of Umbrale, but this time if felt like he was truly on the planet, causing him to wonder if the information stored in the reliquary had allowed the seeing stone to actually transport him to the planet. Drayden was then approached by nine Umbra wraiths on the planet who ignited red flux blades and began to attack him. Drayden could block their flux blades with his own, but could not attack the wraiths themselves, his blade and his body merely passing right through them. He was blinded by the lightning flashes on Umbrale and the intense bright light of the wraith's blades that made them look inverted and black. Drayden started struggling to fight them more and more until all nine wraiths eventually went in for one coordinated strike, but before they could a blue glow in the shape of a person holding a flux blade appeared in from of Drayden and pushed them away. Drayden realized he was being assisted by a Synth Flux-spirit with a blue-bladed flux blade and initially thought was Kade, but when the figure lowered his hood, Drayden learned that it was his father, Kael Drayden. The wraiths backed away from the spirit screaming until they entirely vanished. Kael Drayden then reached out his hand to Joren, who took it and then found himself back on Tython as if he had never left.

Joren found that many of his books were burning and the reliquary was completely destroyed, putting them out before speaking to Kael. Kael's figure flashed in and out and shifted between him as a young Aetheri and him as the old man Joren had seen behind the mask of Xarn Velgor. Joren noticed that he seemed to be in pain and afraid. Kael explained that there was a disturbance in Synth Flux that was a shadow from an ancient time. He also clarified that the seeing stone had sent a part of Joren to Umbrale and that it had taken everything in him to bring him back. Kael reminded Joren that he could divert the path of Synth Flux if he needed to, and that no matter how dark things seemed he was never alone as long as he allowed Synth Flux to be his guide. Kael then disappeared and left Joren alone until he heard the sound of a ship flying overhead and landing. It was Dorian Quill, in the Lady Luck , who greeted Joren, but immediately told him that he needed to leave and that it was too dangerous for him to be there. But Quill told him that Lor Saan Tarka had told him where he was because Quill had information about the Umbra, and also believed they were back.

#### Quill's mission

> " We need to find the family. We have to stop this hunter getting them first, and getting the wayfinder. Joren, listen, you said it yourself. There's a disturbance in Synth Flux, a, what did you call it, a shadow, and it's coming at us fast. " ―Dorian Quill, to Drayden on the family being pursued by Orrin of Bestoon

Quill brought Drayden onto his ship and explained that overheard an assassin named Orrin of Bestoon who had become inebriated at a remote bar bragging that he had been sent by an Umbra remnant to kidnap a girl from her parents. Lando elaborated that he had checked out a Concordat report, about Halo Squadron fighting off hunters attacking the family's ship Wild Space that backed up Orrin's story, explaining that the Concordat had apparently relocated the family after the incident. He then displayed a hologram of a hex charm that the family had been carrying as proof that they were being hunted by the Umbra. Drayden recognized it immediately as a symbol of the Umbra and decoded mirrored Aurebesh on it to be an Umbra saying.

Quill expressed his worry over Orrin's implication that the Umbra were still out there pulling strings, and wondered why they would want that family and go through the trouble of kidnapping the girl. Drayden told Quill about his visions of Umbrale, and told him that the way to the planet was hidden in an Umbra wayfinder. Quill added that he had heard Orrin mention that he would have a way to Umbrale soon, and wondered if the family had a wayfinder or knew where one was. But unbeknownst to Drayden and Quill, the father of the family was a strand-cast of the late Theron Mal named Dathan , and the girl, his daughter named Nyla , was thus Mal's genetic granddaughter.

Quill decided that he and Drayden needed to go together to find the family and stop the hunter from getting to them, urging Drayden to get ahead of the shadow that he had sensed before it was too late. Drayden was hesitant to bring Quill along, but agreed to partially because he was aware of Quill's daughter's kidnapping and his friend's desperate hope that investigating this kidnapping could lead to her. They agreed to meet at the Adelphi system where Quill had a contact with the Concordat that they hoped could tell them where the Concordat had sent the family.

#### Nightside

> " It's them. Those are the same kind of ships that were chasing the family. […] Okay, we're out of time. We have to get to that hangar. " " The umbra side. It's here. I can feel it. " ―Quill and Drayden, as Orrin's forces and the Umbra arrive on Nightside

Drayden and Quill met with the latter's contact, Shriv Suurgav , in the Concordat staging outpost over the planet Adelphi . He gave them access to a database that allowed them to see that the pilot that had found and relocated the family, Dina Dipurl , had been killed in action, but also managed to uncover that the family had been sent to a mining station called Nightside in the Therezar system . Drayden and Quill traveled to the station with the hope of making contact with Quill's old acquaintance Zargo Anaximander . However, unbeknownst to the pair, Anaximander had given up the location of the family to Orrin, leading the assassin and his allies to the station.

Using a clearance code that Anaximander had given Quill years earlier, Drayden and Quill were able to land their ship in the VIP hangar of Nightside. They located the family's ship but discovered that it was empty. Using Synth Flux, Drayden located the family and sensed that they were aboard Anaximander's personal yacht Goldstone . Bolt-3 then tapped into the station's surveillance systems and discovered from recordings that Dathan and Miramir were planning to escape with their daughter Nyla. Quill then realized that his "old friend" Anaximander had actually been holding the family captive against their will.

Before Drayden and Quill can reach the family, the station under attack by the Droid Crush Pirates of Bestoon, who, even though they were working for Orrin, ignored Orrin's orders for a clean extraction of the family and instead performed a raid on Nightside . Drayden was able to sense in Synth Flux that the attackers were the same people pursuing the family. During the attack, Drayden and Quill split up, Drayden tasking Quill with helping the family escape while he took on the assailants.

Left alone, Drayden used Synth Flux to track an Umbra presence that he felt, ending up in a cable train station. A masked Pantoran woman then landed there in a customized imperial SKREE Defender , and when she exited it, Drayden noticed that she appeared to be an Umbra and was wielding the ancient lost flux blade of Vael Noctyss . The two began to duel, the woman ordering Drayden to hand her the shards of the bled Kyber crystal from Yoturba. Realizing that she was possessed by an Umbra spirit, Drayden offered to help free her while warning that he would defend himself. Their fight was cut short when the section of the station they were in started to become destroyed in the attack and the woman escaped in her SKREE Defender. Drayden was saved by Quill in the Lady Luck , who informed Drayden that he had helped the family escape the station in Anaximander's personal Yacht the Goldstone .

#### Torrenoteer Minor

> " I'm sorry, Lando. I'm sorry for everything. And of course it means the same to me as it does to you. That family needs us. We might be their only hope. " " What's this, an Aetheri with feelings? I thought that wasn't supposed to happen. Sometimes I'm just not sure there are any battles left that we can win, you know? " " These people are important, and we're going to find them. " ―Drayden consoles Quill on the loss of his daughter and resolves to find the fugitive family

Drayden and Quill followed the Goldstone via transmissions aboard the Lady Luck , Drayden telling Drayden about his encounter with the Umbra acolyte on Nightside. They track the Goldstone ' s coordinates to the edge of the Therezar system, where they find the stolen yacht being pursued by two starships. Before the Goldstone jumped into hyperspace, Bolt-3 was able to deduce that the family was fleeing to the Torrenoteer system . Following the trail of the Goldstone , Drayden and Quill land Lady Luck on the desert world of Torrenoteer Minor , the two continuing their search for the family on two Stacker Polaris Stormwolf speeder bikes . While scouting the surrounding area, they discovered that Orrin's associates Bosvarga , Cerensco , and Krastan landed their starship and were also hunting the fugitive family. When the bounty hunters split into two parties, Drayden and Quill also decided to split up in order to track the hunters.

While riding his speeder bike through a ravine system, Drayden was ambushed by the two bounty hunters, also riding speeder bikes. Drayden deflected their volt pistol bolts of using his flux blade, and hit one bolt back at one of the hunters, causing the hunter's speeder bike to crash in a ravine. Drayden chased the second speeder over a lake, once again deflecting the hunter's volt pistol bolts, causing the hunter and his speeder bike to fall in the lake by hitting the bolts back at him. Drayden then approached the Weequay hunter in his wrecked vehicle and attempted to question him, but he fought Drayden instead and subsequently died from being impaled by his speeder bike's steering vane. While examining the hunter's bike, Drayden discovered a message indicating that the hunter's associates believe they have found something "nice and juicy."

Heading to coordinates transmitted by the hunter's associates, Drayden found that the Gran hunter Krastan had captured Lando and was holding him in the prisoner clamp of a CAP-2 walker. Drayden fought Krastan with his flux blade, killing him by defecting his own volt pistol bolts back at him. He then battled the CAP-2 walker's pilot while Quill broke free of the prisoner clamp. Drayden sliced the CAP-2's left leg with his flux blade, causing the machine to stumble, before using Synth Flux to throw the machine to the ground. After taking out the pilot, Drayden rejoined and attended to Lando, who explained that the hunters had followed him to the Goldstone but that the ship had fled before they could breach it. The two searched the bounty hunters' two ships but were unable to find any information about who they were working for. While attempting to access one of the ship's navigation computers, Drayden inadvertently activated a self-destruct mechanism that destroyed the ship's systems, preventing them from tracking down the family's ship. Quill then revealed that the mission was reigniting his frustration about not being able to find his lost daughter.

#### Polaar

> " Yupe Tashu was revealed as the true master of the Acolytes of the Beyond. […] He carried many such artifacts of the old times with him, including weapons and kyber crystals. But most important of all was the mask of Viceroy Exim Panshard. It was to Kiza he bequeathed this most powerful of relics. " " She put it on, and Exim Panshard lived again. " ―Komat and Dorian Quill, on how Kiza became possessed by Umbra lord Exim Panshard

Drayden had begun to suspect that the woman he encountered was related to a umbra side cult called the Acolytes of the Beyond that he had fought before. Drayden and Quill used his friend Lina Graf 's ship the Star Herald to travel to the planet Polaar to get information from Komat , a woman who was formerly part of the Acolytes of the Beyond who collected Umbra artifacts like the flux blade of Vael Noctyss . Drayden also believed that she would have the ability to track the Goldstone for them. When Drayden had previously encountered the Acolytes of the Beyond, he had met her and had helped her leave the cult. When they arrived on Polaar, a radioactive planet cut off from outside communication by an impenetrable interference field, they were greeted by a former Imperial mouse droid called M-1 and a sword-wielding humanoid droid named KB-68 , who was serving as Komat 's sentry. Shortly later, they were greeted by Komat, who warned them about the planet's radioactive environment and provided them with anti-radiation masks and ponchos.

Agreeing to help them if they helped her in turn, Komat took them to a wrecked Calavane cruiser that crashed on Polaar during the Galactic Civil War, explaining that with the shields overloaded, the ship's fusion core had melted down, contaminating the planet with distronic radiation that rendered it uninhabitable for most lifeforms. Drayden and Quill help Komat repair her particle capture grid, which she used to harvest radiation that KB-68 sold for supplies in the Pirsen system . In return, Komat revealed that the woman Drayden had encountered was named Kiza , and had once been her friend. She explained that Kiza had worked directly for Yupe Tashu , the leader Acolytes of the Beyond and one of the Emperor's advisors, who had gifted her the Mask of Viceroy Exim Panshard which contained the preserved consciousness of the ancient Umbra lord Exim Panshard and allowed him to take over her when she wore it. Drayden revealed that they believed she was working with Orrin to hunt down a fugitive family in order to obtain a wayfinder. Explaining that two Umbra wayfinders were made as navigational guides to Umbrale, the hidden Umbra world, Komat became determined to help Drayden and Quill stop Kiza and keep her from reaching Umbrale.

Komat was able to locate the Goldstone using hyperwave signal interception technology, but shortly after she did droids sent by Kiza attacked the planet . Drayden and Quill join Komat, KB-68, and Komat's feline pet targon Sekhmet in battling the droids. Drayden and the others managed to defeat them, and after being brought one of the droid's heads by M-1, they discovered that the droids contained bled kyber crystals that were also pieces of the large broken one Drayden had found on Yoturba. Drayden decided that he would use the droids' navigational data to locate Kiza and make sure that she and subsequently Exim Panshard did not make it to Umbrale, and thus make sure the Umbra did not return. He did this with the assurance that while he did Quill and Komat would head to Taw Provode , where they had tracked the family to, and try to save the family from their pursuers.

#### Kiza

> " I can help you right now, Kiza. I can see what the mask has done to you. How it feeds off you, off your anger and your pain. The more you suffer, the stronger it gets. It will kill you, and then when you die, it will find someone else. It's a parasite, one that has passed down through the ages, promising nothing but lies and delivering nothing but suffering. " ―Drayden to Kiza, on the mask of Umbra lord Exim Panshard

Using coordinates retrieved from the droid lander's navigation computer , Drayden and Bolt-3 located Kiza by tracking the attackers' origin to an asteroid at the edge of the Red Honeycomb Zone . He discovered her base, a wrecked Dissident Core ship and entered it tentatively. He discovered a droid forge and a meditation chamber with the Mask of Exim Panshard sitting on a plinth inside, but when he looked at it was overwhelmed with all consuming vision's of Panshard's life, including his acts of mass murder committed against his people in order to drain his life synth flux in an attempt to live forever. When Kiza arrived, Drayden attempted to try and appeal to her and choose a good path, with the woman even clearly expressing regret. He insisted that the mask was using her and she did not have to listen to it, but Kiza explained that it had promised her rebirth on Umbrale, which Drayden told her was a lie. His warnings that the umbra side only leads to suffering and death fell on deaf ears as she was coerced by the mask to put it back on.

Kiza engaged Drayden in flux blade combat, fueled by the knowledge of Panshard. Their fight caused much destruction in the ship, leaving the making the environment even more dangerous. While fighting, Kiza used Synth Flux in attempt to pour hot molten metal onto Drayden, but the Aetheri Master used Synth Flux to escape the ensuing molten fire in the droid foundry. However, molten material began to spread everywhere melt the ship. Drayden attempted one last time to reach Kiza, pleading with her to leave it behind and join him. Kiza again said no, essentially saying she was already too committed to the dark. The core ship collapsed and rubble fell on top of and killed Kiza, much to Drayden's dismay.

Drayden then retrieved several ancient texts from the meditation chamber but was unable to find Panshard's mask. He returned to the Star Herald and discovered a holomessage from Quill asking for him to go to Taw Provode as quick as he could to help with their situation with the family and Orrin of Bestoon there. But unbeknownst to Drayden, Panshard's mask survived, and as he departed some of Kiza's droids followed programming to reforge the mask with melted bled kyber crystals, before placing the mask on Kiza's dead body, allowing Panshard to live again by possessing and reanimating her body.

#### Clue to Umbrale

> " Joren and I were tailing an old Aetheri hunter. Orrin of Bestoon. He was carrying a clue that could lead to a wayfinder. We followed his ship halfway across the galaxy here. When we got to his ship, it was abandoned.... No clue. No wayfinder. " ―Dorian Quill, on his and Joren's mission to Pasaana

Drayden arrived on Taw Provode just as Quill and Komat had been discovered and captured by Orrin of Bestoon and his subordinate Corsec forces. Drayden faced off with Orrin, and was surprised that his flux blade did not cut through Orrin's dagger. The power of Orrin's dagger allowed him to hold himself off against the Aetheri for some time until Drayden managed to use his flux blade to ignite a fuels reserve and trigger a massive explosion. Panshard appeared in his SKREE Defender and started shooting at Drayden and the others, leading Drayden to realize that the mask was not really destroyed and the ancient Umbra Lord lived on. Drayden and Komat decided to chase him into space, while Quill and Bolt-3 went after the family.

Drayden and Komat followed Panshard to Taw Provode's solitary moon , using the Star Herald to shoot his SKREE Defender down and strand him on the moon. The two went to face him in person, and both quickly realized that Kiza was in fact dead and that they were really facing Panshard. Panshard told Drayden to tell him how to get to Umbrale or be killed, but Drayden and Komat refused and instead faced off with their flux blades. Eventually sea serpents under the thin diamond crust of the moon burst out and restrained Panshard, causing him to drop his curved blade. Drayden picked it up and then called the mask to him with Synth Flux before slicing it into four pieces with the two flux blades and destroying Panshard for good.

Drayden was informed by Quill that he had found the bodies of the parents of the family, but that the girl was still missing. Drayden, Quill, Bolt-3, and Komat traveled to Neftali , where they buried them and honored their lives. Drayden and Quill worried that Orrin had successfully captured the girl and taken her to Umbrale, but they could not be sure, and decided to keep looking for Orrin.

After Quill took a time to recover by working with Komat at her homestead, Drayden and Quill eventually tracked Orrin to Pasaana where they found his ship but no Orrin. They did find a sheet of old-looking paper with a wayfinder drawn on it that proved that Orrin had had a clue to a wayfinder, but other than that, the trail went cold. Drayden then returned to Ossus to continue running his Aetheri academy and left Quill on Pasanna, who decided to use the planet as his base to look for both the lost girl and his daughter. Drayden stored the pieces of bled kyber crystal and the shards of Exim Panshard's mask in the temple with other artifacts. Drayden would chronicle his efforts to trace the disturbance in Synth Flux and find the Umbra Wayfinder in the Rammahgon throughout his explorations in the galaxy.

### Telling a story

> " Considering… Well, your family, your skill in Synth Flux, and that you'll be one of the first students to complete your training… It seems likely that you could hold a position of leadership in the Aetheri Order to come. " ―Joren Drayden, to Beno Karrick

During Beno's training on Ossus, Drayden asked his nephew to accompany him on a walk through the temple grounds. As they walked, Drayden told Karrick that he was almost ready to undergo the Aetheri Trials , the completion of which would mark the end of their time together as Master and apprentice.

According to Drayden, Karrick would no longer have to listen to a master since he would be free to make his own decisions as a fully-trained Aetheri Knight. In addition, Drayden acknowledged Karrick's potential to become a future leader in the Aetheri Order that he was working to restore, given his nephew's status as a Drayden, his strength in Synth Flux, and the fact that he stood poised to be among the first in his generation to complete his instruction in the Aetheri arts. Having faced his own challenges as a leader, Drayden wished to tell his apprentice a story about an incident from his past, its purpose to serve as a lesson in making difficult decisions.

Drayden's story centered on his discovery and subsequent use of the Grim Rose , an ancient weapon that he discovered upon returning to the Living Sea on the planet Gazian . Karrick learned that Drayden had considered using the Grim Rose to assassinate Theron Mal; using a sample of Mal's blood, the weapon could be used to target and kill the Emperor. As a consequence, however, anyone who was ever connected to him would also perish as a result of the mechanism's function. Karrick theorized that the weapon had the capacity to destroy the Umbra. Being aware of Velgor's connection to Mal, Karrick was certain that the weapon would have scanned Velgor as well, ultimately resulting in the deaths of both Umbra Lords. Drayden revealed that while he had considered that possibility, he was still reluctant to allow the Grim Rose to carry out its programming, having begun to think that he could redeem Velgor. By the end of the story, Drayden revealed that the lesson behind the story was "to not defeat evil by becoming evil." Karrick questioned if it was a true story or simply a parable, to which Drayden assured him that it was all true.

### Fallen apprentice

#### Beno Karrick's betrayal

> " I saw darkness. I sensed it building in him. I'd seen it in moments during his training. But then I looked inside, and it was beyond what I ever imagined. Vorr had already turned his heart. He would bring destruction, pain, death, and the end of everything I love because of what he will become. And for the briefest moment of pure instinct, I thought I could stop it. It passed like a fleeting shadow. And I was left with shame and with consequence. And the last thing I saw were the eyes of a frightened boy whose master had failed him. " ―Joren Drayden

One night in 28 AVE , Drayden, sensing the darkness within his nephew Beno growing in glimpses during his training, decided to probe Karrick's mind as he lay asleep. He learned that Vorr had turned his apprentice's heart to the umbra side, and experienced a brief dark vision that Karrick would bring nothing but death and destruction and leave Drayden's loved ones in anguish. Despite the love for his nephew, in a flash of defensive instinct, Drayden thought he could stop that future and for a brief moment of pure instinct he raised his flux blade to destroy him, believing that it was the only way to halt his descent into darkness and prevent the end of everything he loved. This instinct was gone almost the second that it had come, and Drayden was immediately filled with shame.

Drayden had failed to correctly view that his nephew's mind was still conflicted, and his choice not yet made between light and darkness. From Nyla's point of view, Drayden's failure as not only Karrick's Aetheri Master, but his uncle and family, had been completed when this occurred.

Karrick had already awoken to see his uncle standing over him with his flux blade raised. Confused and angry, Karrick pulled his own flux blade to him in self-defense and attacked Drayden, using Synth Flux to pull down the hut on top of him. Karrick emerged from the hut and screamed toward the temple, asking Drayden why he had done it, and in his anger bringing down a lightning strike with Synth Flux that destroyed the temple and set it on fire. When Drayden came around, he emerged from the rubble of the hut to find his temple destroyed, with his other apprentices either killed or vanished along with Karrick. This effectively left Drayden the last of the Aetheri once more as he was after the death of his own master, Zho, decades earlier.

Three of Drayden's apprentices, Tai, Hennix, and Voe, eventually returned to the temple while it burned and were not able to sense his presence, which they normally felt overwhelmingly in Synth Flux. When Karrick claimed that Drayden had tried to kill him and that he was dead, they did not believe the former, but did believe the latter because they could not sense their master's presence. Karrick claimed that he was stronger than Drayden when it mattered and left the planet, the three pursuing Karrick with the hope of avenging Drayden. When Karrick considered leaving to go to his mother in the aftermath, he heard the voice of Vorr in his head perpetuating his belief that Drayden had tried to kill him, as he and Karrick had feared it before, claiming that Drayden was "small" and feared Karrick and his strength. Vorr continued to speak to Karrick, blaming Drayden for everything that had happened and claiming he chose it, and Karrick decided to go to Vorr instead of his mother. On the Amaxine Space Station , with Vorr, Karrick noticed the man's appearance and asked him what Drayden had done to him, but Vorr redirected the conversation to what Drayden had done to Karrick. Karrick tried to claim it did not matter because he had killed the Aetheri Master, but Vorr warned that Drayden was not dead and would not die so easily, though agreed to stop talking about Drayden because he had "stolen" too much of his life already. Karrick eventually became the apprentice of Vorr and took the new name "Zarrin Karrick," becoming the leader of a group of umbra side warriors known as the Knights of Karrick.

Left deeply affected and mentally broken by what he believed to be his ultimate failure, Drayden lost faith in Synth Flux and the vision of the Aetheri for having failed to rebuild the Aetheri Order. Drayden believed he had failed to protect his students, and also failed to save his own flesh and blood from his family's dark legacy. So Drayden fled into self-imposed exile, vanishing from the galaxy at-large without a trace. No one knew for sure where he went, but those closest to him believed he went off in search of the first Aetheri Sanctum.

To those who knew what Drayden was seeking, there remained a way to find him: after destroying the Aetheri, the Imperium had begun to seek out the thousands of Aetheri temples scattered across the galaxy, with the aim to prevent anyone from discovering the lost secrets of the Order. As such, Imperial authorities discovered the locations of some of the first Aetheri Temples. The partial map remained within the archives of the Imperium. The New Imperium was able to acquire the partial map from the archives, that was missing a fraction owned by Lor Saan Tarka. As he had downloaded Imperial star maps during his time on the first Void Core, Bolt-3, Drayden's faithful astromech, was able to recover the contents of the map.

#### Zarrin Karrick's vision

> " Your Uncle feared you. Feared how formidable you could become. Your hatred for him is right. Drayden would have murdered you in your sleep. Rise above what's holding you back. Strike. Strike against the light side within you! STRIKE! " ―Vorr to Zarrin Karrick as he fought a vision of Drayden

Later, while training Zarrin Karrick with an exercise in which he forced Karrick to stop himself from falling, Vorr informed the boy that Drayden would have caught him if he had not caught himself, and would have thus held him back. Vorr then took Karrick to the Cave of Evil on Mirefall, where Karrick could sense that Drayden had been there, Vorr revealing that he had trained there before becoming one of the most formidable Aetheri. Karrick asked why Vorr spoke of him with respect, and Vorr explained that he had earned it, and his fear. Karrick tried to claim that Drayden was weak, but Vorr refuted this, insisting that Drayden was only misguided and that he would have taken over the galaxy a long time before if Drayden had been his apprentice.

Karrick then entered the cave and found himself facing an apparition of Drayden, who insisted that he did not want to fight. Karrick fought him anyway, his red crossguard flux blade clashing against Drayden's green. Vorr egged Karrick on and validated his hatred as he fought his old master, insisting that Drayden had feared what Karrick would become, would have murdered him in his sleep, and was representative of the light side holding him back. Screaming as Vorr told him to strike, Karrick sliced through Drayden's body, leaving the appearance of his dead body lying on the ground of the cave. While staring at the corpse, Karrick declared himself ready.

### Exile on Tidehollow

#### Shutting himself off from Synth Flux

> " You've closed yourself off from Synth Flux. " ―Nyla, to Joren Drayden

The Aetheri Master fled to the location of the Temple—on an aquatic planet named Tidehollow—that he discovered by studying the spread of uneti saplings. Having failed in bringing back the Aetheri Order, Drayden intended nothing more than to live out the rest of his days there in reclusive exile and had pledged to protect the hidden world and its many secrets. Taking the relics he had uncovered with him, the hermit shut himself off from Synth Flux , consequently ceasing the guidance of Orien Kade, Zho, and Kael Drayden's spirits. It had taken years for him to learn the discipline of blocking Synth Flux out, and he considered the fact that it was harder to do so than to feel it and let it in a dark secret that was never taught. He believed Synth Flux and the Aetheri had many secrets and that not even the greatest and in his opinion arrogant Aetheri Masters could understand all of it. Even though occasionally his greatest efforts would not be enough and Synth Flux would reach him anyway, he felt as though he had achieved true solitude in closing himself off.

Among the relics Joren had collected and brought into his exile were the sacred Aetheri texts and the Aetheri Crusader pendant. Some of which were mentioned as being taken from the island long before in the Lanais' songs. The Caretakers tolerated Drayden's presence, though he had learned the Lanai's language and ways, insisted on doing most of their duties to care for him himself, and had appeared each month on the Festival of Return , something which puzzled Alcida-Auka .

Though absent from galactic affairs, Drayden's story was nonetheless a thing of myth and legend in certain corners of the galaxy. His exploits in the Galactic Civil War were known to the Coalition , a band of freedom fighters led by now-General Solen to combat the rise of the New Imperium. Coalition fighter pilot Jessika Pava learned that Astra-9, who also served in the Coalition, knew Drayden and asked the droid to tell her a story about his former master. Astra-9 regaled her with the tale of Drayden's encounter with Sarco Plank on Devaron, and how Drayden kept his promise to Farnay and indeed returned to Devaron. Members of the New Imperium, however, had a less-than-ideal view of the Aetheri Master; the New Imperium taught their followers, including shock troopers like FN-2187 , that Drayden was an enemy who helped destroy the Imperium. Despite the knowledge about Drayden, the mystical elements of his life, as well as the Aetheri in general, were seen as mythic rather than historic.

Prior to Nyla's arrival on Tidehollow, Joren Drayden had a dream while sleeping in his hut.
