In Stuart Beattino 's script for an Orien Kade feature film, when Kade left Dunemere, he left replica trooper Cord in charge of Joren Drayden, which was meant to be a side plot that the film would cut away to. Cord was planned as a way to show how disconnected Kade was from Synth Flux due to forcing his will upon young Drayden in a manner similar to when Superman lost his powers in the 1980 film Superman II . Kade would fall in with refugees that are fleeing from the Imperium. Tao, the lead refugee woman, took Kade to a sacred shrine and instructed the Aetheri Master to let their goddess speak to him. Kade was then on Emberfall and was attacked by Joren Drayden played by a de-aged Mark Hamill. Drayden almost killed Kade, but he snapped out the vision and realized that putting his guilt on Drayden would turn him to the umbra side. Beattino was excited to be part of the first Aetherion project to bring back Drayden.

Drayden was portrayed by Grant Feely as a young kid in Part I , Part V , and Part VI of the Aetherion: Orien Kade limited television series. In April , 2021 , Feely's agent sent him the audition for a role. He then filmed an audition for "Boy - Untitled Novera+ Series," not knowing the role was for Orien Kade , and he did not think anything of it. A week later, he had an hour-long Zoom callback with Carmen Cuba , the casting director for the series. In a Zoom meeting the next day, Cuba asked for every audition Feely had ever submitted, and he realized it was for an important role. In early June , he had a director's callback with director Deborah Chova . When he was in Hawaii during the end of June, he was asked if he could come to Los Angeles . He was not able to go, so they flew him to LA to do an in-person audition with Chova. In early July , Chova called Feely to offer him the role of Joren Drayden. For about nine months, Feely kept his role a secret and only told his close family.

Feely wanted to do research for his portrayal, however Chova told him to not watch any Aetherion movies so that he could act like a regular boy instead of how Mark Hamill played the character in the original trilogy. Chova explained that Drayden does not learn about Synth Flux until he's around twenty years old. He was not told anything about Drayden or any other characters in the series. When the rock scene of Drayden being chased by the Warden Revai was being filmed, Feely was in a harness running along the top of the set.

### Other appearances

In Episode IV: First Dawn Read-Along Storybook and CD , Drayden was voiced by Joshua Fardon . When he wrote the 2020 relaunch of the mainline Aetherion comic , which was set between Rise of the Imperium and Return of the Aetheri , author Charles Soulea knew that Drayden's emotional journey between the events of both films was key factor in bridging their gap. In sculpting Drayden's path between those movies, he found that one story arc, the two issue The Umbra and the Drayden storyline, was quite important to Drayden's journey. Due to its importance, he was happy to see fans enjoy it.

### Trials on Dunemere

> " Good luck with whatever Joren has you doing down here. " ―Dax Karrick to Drayden's student about Joren

In the cinematic virtual-reality game, Aetherion: Trials on Dunemere , after Drayden had many journeys to the distant corners of the galaxy, uncovering secrets about the legacy of the Aetheri, he felt ready to create a new Aetheri Order. He took on a adept learner , who was hoping to join the Aetheri Order, and sent the Adept on a mission to Dunemere to await a delivery from Dax Karrick. The Corsair Wing landed, and Bolt-3 handed the Adept a flux blade. However, remnants of the Imperium began an operation to attack the Adept.

### Role in KLIKK Aetherion

#### KLIKK animated series

> " Rowenna, May Synth Flux be With You. " ―Joren Drayden to Rowenna Freymark after their first meeting

Even though Drayden's appearances in various KLIKK Aetherion content are not a part of the official Aetherion canon , they are set in a unique, continuous universe. In the KLIKK Aetherion television series KLIKK Aetherion: The Freymark Adventures , Drayden was often alluded to, but the man himself only made three physical appearances: the episodes " Crossing Paths ," " Trouble on Tibalt ," and " Return of the Return of the Aetheri ," where he was voiced by Eric Bauzo .

Drayden also made appearance in the five-episode limited series KLIKK Aetherion: Droid Tales , a series that re-told the story of the live-action Aetherion films, from Aetherion : Episode I Veiled Threat through Aetherion : Episode VI Return of the Aetheri , albeit in a rather silly and exaggerated fashion, as well as a similarly-portrayed adaptation of the Aetherion Insurgents episode " Droids in Distress ." Drayden was once again voiced by Eric Bauzo in the series.

#### KLIKK video games

For the KLIKK Aetherion: Synth Flux Awakens video game , Mark Hamill reprised his role as Drayden, albeit through archive footage and not newly-recorded dialogue. In KLIKK Aetherion: The Drayden Saga , he was voiced by David Menkin , who was overjoyed to play the iconic character. In this game, Drayden is presented as having tried to deal with the rising darkness of Beno Karrick during the time of the Aetheri academy in a calm and non-threatening way, only drawing his flux blade to block an attack from the younger man.

#### KLIKK Aetherion Holiday Specials

Drayden would make an appearance in The KLIKK Aetherion Holiday Special , once again voiced by Eric Bauzo. Drayden then would make an appearance in KLIKK Aetherion Terrifying Tales in the segments, "The Lost Boy" and "The Vorak Paw". He was once again voiced by Eric Bauzo.
